### [1] [Analyzing Interaction Between CCAs and Traffic Policers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09984)
*Ammar Tahir*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化框架，用于研究流量监管器（如虚队列或令牌桶）与任意拥塞控制算法（CCA）之间的交互机制，帮助网络运营商确定监管器配置参数，并解释了CCA与监管器和流量整形器的不同交互方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商需要配置流量监管器参数（如虚队列大小、安全速率阈值等），但缺乏系统性的方法来理解流量监管器与拥塞控制算法之间的交互行为，这导致配置困难且容易出错。

Method: 提出了一个形式化框架，该框架能够建模和分析流量监管器（包括基于虚队列和令牌桶的实现）与任意拥塞控制算法之间的交互。框架提供了配置参数的确定方法，并对比分析了流量监管器与流量整形器的差异。

Result: 框架成功揭示了流量监管器与拥塞控制算法的交互机理，为网络运营商提供了确定监管器配置参数的理论依据；同时阐明了拥塞控制算法与流量监管器的交互方式显著不同于与流量整形器的交互。

Conclusion: 该形式化框架为理解和配置流量监管器提供了理论基础，有助于网络运营商更科学地设置监管参数，同时加深了对不同网络流量管理机制与拥塞控制算法交互差异的认识。

Abstract: We describe details of a formal framework to study the interaction between traffic policers, implemented using phantom queues or token buckets, and any arbitrary congestion control algorithm (CCA). This framework allows network providers to figure out configurations for their traffic policers (phantom queue size, safe rate thresholds, etc.). We also use this framework to describe why CCAs interact differently with a traffic policer compared to traffic shapers.

</details>


### [2] [CSI-Assisted Edge SLAM Testbed Platform for 5G Connected Unmanned Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10394)
*Boris Radovanovic,Sasa Talosi,Srdjan Sobot,Dejan Vukobratovic*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一个CSI辅助的边缘SLAM测试平台，集成了UGV、ROS2 SLAM框架和5G O-RAN系统，实现了端到端跨层的CSI暴露与集成，揭示了通信感知SLAM在延迟、数据流、同步和跨系统集成方面的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着5G向6G演进，连接机器人通过URLLC链路将计算密集型任务卸载到边缘服务器，SLAM作为机器人基本功能需要边缘部署。同时ISAC技术使得CSI可作为无线电SLAM的额外感知模态，为边缘SLAM提供了新的技术路径。

Method: 设计并实现CSI辅助边缘SLAM测试平台，集成定制UGV、ROS2 SLAM框架和5G O-RAN系统。架构提供ROS2传感器数据流经5G的端到端跨层视图，分析ROS2 DDS通信、RTPS打包和5G用户面传输，讨论通过O-RAN组件进行CSI提取和交付的机制。

Result: 平台成功实现了CSI暴露与集成到SLAM流程中，支持通信感知SLAM的真实实验。分析揭示了与延迟、数据流、同步和跨系统集成相关的关键挑战，为未来6G机器人平台提供了见解。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构为通信感知SLAM研究提供了实验平台，揭示了实现6G使能机器人平台需要解决的关键技术挑战，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The evolution from 5G towards 6G reinforces interest in connected robotics, where mobile robots offload compute-intensive tasks to edge servers over ultra-reliable low-latency communication (URLLC) links. Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), a fundamental yet demanding robotics function, is increasingly considered for edge deployment within mobile edge computing (MEC) frameworks. In parallel, integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) enables the use of radio channel information, such as channel state information (CSI), as an additional sensing modality in radio-based SLAM. In this paper, we design and implement a CSI-assisted Edge SLAM testbed integrating a custom unmanned ground vehicle (UGV), a ROS2-based SLAM framework, and a 5G Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) system. The proposed architecture provides an end-to-end, cross-layer view of ROS2 sensor data streaming over 5G, explicitly enabling CSI exposure and integration into the SLAM pipeline. We analyze ROS2 DDS communication, RTPS packetization, and 5G user-plane transport, and discuss mechanisms for CSI extraction and delivery via O-RAN components. The platform enables realistic experimentation with communication-aware SLAM and reveals key challenges related to latency, data streaming, synchronization, and cross-system integration, providing insights for future 6G-enabled robotic platforms.

</details>


### [3] [The Emerging 3GPP 6G RAN Architecture: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10543)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了3GPP 6G RAN架构的演进，重点关注边界选择问题，包括CU/DU分割增强、RU作为独立逻辑单元的识别以及RAN-核心网接口研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G RAN引入了模块化基站架构，为多样化部署场景和多厂商互操作提供了灵活框架。6G RAN面临的核心架构挑战是在适应新部署和服务需求的同时，保留5G架构的这些优势。

Method: 回顾和分析3GPP正在制定的6G RAN架构标准，重点研究架构边界选择问题，包括CU/DU分割增强、RU逻辑单元识别以及RAN-核心网接口设计。

Result: 识别出关键架构决策：CU/DU分割的增强方案；RU作为独立逻辑单元的正式识别；RAN-核心网接口中，连接服务倾向于采用点对点信令而非服务化接口。同时识别出开放研究问题：非连接服务的RAN-核心网接口、数据采集框架、6G RAN中的人工智能应用。

Conclusion: 6G RAN架构是5G RAN的有序演进，需要在继承5G模块化优势的同时适应新的部署和服务需求。边界选择是关键架构决策点，仍有多项技术领域需要进一步研究和标准化。

Abstract: The 6G radio access network (RAN) architecture is emerging as a disciplined evolution of 5G RAN. The 5G baseline introduced modular base station, providing a flexible framework for diverse deployment scenarios and multi-vendor interoperability. The key architectural challenge for 6G RAN is to preserve these benefits while adapting the RAN to new deployment and service requirements. This article reviews the emerging 3GPP 6G RAN architecture with emphasis on boundary selection. It discusses central unit and distributed unit split enhancements, recognition of radio unit as a distinct logical unit, and the RAN-core network interface study, where point-to-point signaling is favored over service-based interface for connectivity services. It also highlights open areas including RAN-core network interface for non-connectivity services, data collection framework, and artificial intelligence for 6G RAN.

</details>


### [4] [SAIL: Perceptual Quality-Aware Rate Control for Cloud Gaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11231)
*Houde Qian,Chenglei Wu,Jiaxing Zhang,Rui-Xiao Zhang,Jing Wang,Meijia Song,Sijia Chen,Xiaozhong Xu,Zhi Wang,Lifeng Sun,Honghao Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出SAIL系统，通过质量感知的码率控制减少云游戏44.27%带宽消耗，同时保持感知无损质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云游戏平台面临严重的带宽成本问题（占总运营成本30-60%），现有生产系统采用保真度优先策略，在达到感知无损阈值后仍持续推送冗余码率，造成带宽浪费。

Method: 提出SAIL系统，采用后编码架构实现毫秒级反馈：1）利用编码器零成本输出的质量评估模型；2）平衡稳态适应与动态峰值吸收的混合码率控制机制；3）与拥塞控制协同的网络感知策略。

Result: 在T云游戏平台部署，带宽消耗降低44.27%，端到端延迟降低8.37%，服务数千万用户，累计数十亿小时游戏时长，未降低感知质量。

Conclusion: 通过质量感知的码率控制，可以在不降低感知质量的前提下显著降低云游戏带宽成本和延迟，证明了感知无损阈值在码率控制中的重要性。

Abstract: Cloud gaming streams cloud-rendered frames under strict motion-to-photon latency, yet its at-scale viability is increasingly constrained by bandwidth cost: in our study of the T cloud gaming platform, bandwidth accounts for 30-60% of total operating expense. This high bandwidth consumption stems from a fidelity-first objective of making the stream perceptually indistinguishable from local gameplay. It drives production systems toward best-effort bitrate allocation that pushes the encoder to the highest rate allowed by congestion control. However, the bitrate-perception relationship saturates: beyond a frame-dependent perceptually lossless threshold, additional bits yield negligible perceptual improvement, creating systematic redundant quality that wastes bandwidth.
  We present SAIL, a production quality-aware rate control system with the goal of achieving perceptually lossless quality while avoiding unnecessary bandwidth waste. SAIL adopts a post-encoding architecture to enable millisecond-scale feedback at near-zero overhead. It comprises three key designs: (i) an encoder-driven quality assessment model that leverages zero-cost encoder outputs for real-time quality estimation; (ii) a hybrid rate control mechanism that balances steady-state adaptation with dynamic spike absorption; and (iii) a network-aware strategy that coordinates with congestion control to prevent capacity underestimation. SAIL has been fully deployed on the T cloud gaming platform and reduces bandwidth consumption by 44.27% and end-to-end latency by 8.37% without degrading perceived quality, serving tens of millions of users and accumulating billions of hours of total gameplay.

</details>


### [5] [An Experimental Evaluation of Accurate Scheduling and Hardware Timestamping on NVIDIA ConnectX NICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11305)
*Takahiro Hirofuchi,Takaaki Fukai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对NVIDIA ConnectX-7网卡的精确调度和硬件时间戳功能进行了实验表征。结果显示硬件时间戳精度约为±7-8 ns，精确调度的传输 timing 准确度在±900 ns内达到99%，适合5G前传等微秒级延迟要求的应用，但不足以满足需要纳秒级精度的高确定性TSN应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度数据包传输在确定性网络应用（如5G前传和TSN）中日益重要。NVIDIA ConnectX网卡提供了精确调度和硬件时间戳功能，预期满足5G前传的严格时间要求，但缺乏关于其时间精度和性能特性的公开信息。因此需要对这些功能进行实验评估和表征。

Method: 使用基于FPGA的测量平台，该平台具有确定性帧生成和纳秒分辨率时间戳功能。首先评估接收和发送硬件时间戳的精度，然后评估精确调度的传输时间准确度。测量平台包含独立时钟的以太网实体用于对比测试。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 接收和发送硬件时间戳在独立时钟以太网实体间的测量变化约为±7-8 ns；(2) 精确调度功能约99%的帧在指定传输时间的±900 ns范围内传输，但观察到偶发的高达约5 μs的异常值。

Conclusion: 精确调度功能适用于具有数十微秒级延迟要求的应用（如5G前传），但其时间准确度不足以满足需要纳秒级传输时间精度的高确定性TSN应用。这为确定性网络应用的硬件选型提供了重要参考。

Abstract: High-precision packet transmission is becoming increasingly important in deterministic networking applications, including 5G fronthaul and Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN). Recent NVIDIA ConnectX network interface cards (NICs) provide Accurate Scheduling, a hardware-assisted mechanism for transmitting Ethernet frames at designated times, as part of their 5T for 5G feature set. They also provide hardware timestamping for received and transmitted frames. Although these functions are expected to satisfy the stringent timing requirements of 5G fronthaul, little public information is available regarding their timing accuracy and performance characteristics. This paper presents an experimental characterization of the Accurate Scheduling and hardware timestamping capabilities of the NVIDIA ConnectX-7 NIC. Using an FPGA-based measurement platform with deterministic frame generation and nanosecond-resolution timestamping, we first evaluate the precision of the receive and transmit hardware timestamps and then evaluate the transmission timing accuracy of Accurate Scheduling. The experimental results show that the receive and transmit hardware timestamps exhibit a measured variation of approximately $\pm$7-8 ns when compared across independently clocked Ethernet entities. Furthermore, Accurate Scheduling transmits approximately 99% of frames within $\pm$900 ns of the specified transmission time, while occasional outliers of up to approximately 5 us are observed. These results indicate that Accurate Scheduling is well suited for applications with latency requirements on the order of several tens of microseconds, such as 5G fronthaul, whereas its timing accuracy is insufficient for highly deterministic TSN applications, which typically require transmission timing accuracy on the order of several to several tens of nanoseconds.

</details>


### [6] [GNSS Spoofing Detection in TDD Networks: A 3GPP Standards-Based Security Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11398)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,John Owens,Kevin Kiernan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对TDD移动网络中GNSS欺骗攻击威胁，提出了一种基于现有3GPP管理架构的检测与监控框架，在漂移率超过0.5 ns/s时检测概率超过95%，误报率低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDD移动网络依赖GNSS授时的大主时钟提供同步，精度要求为±1.5 μs。GNSS欺骗已成为实际运营威胁，可破坏下游所有基站的授时，但现有3GPP管理框架（SA5）和安全框架（SA3）均缺乏标准化机制来检测或报告此类攻击，存在安全缺口。

Method: 提出运行于现有3GPP管理结构内的检测监控框架，包括：（1）与TS 28.111和TS 28.552对齐的GNSS定时告警和性能计数器；（2）拓扑感知的关联机制，通过按服务大主时钟分组gNB-DU来分类异常；（3）连接故障管理与SEHAND事件处理（TR 33.894）的安全事件桥接。框架无需新接口，具有代际无关性。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明，在良好配置的PTP网络条件下，漂移率超过0.5 ns/s时检测概率超过95%，误报率低于1%。场景分析验证了框架能区分欺骗攻击与信号丢失、设备故障和维护瞬态。

Conclusion: 该框架在无需新增接口的前提下，有效填补了3GPP标准中GNSS欺骗检测的空白，为TDD网络提供了实用的安全增强方案，且具备良好的部署可行性和代际兼容性。

Abstract: Time Division Duplex (TDD) mobile networks require synchronization accuracy of $\pm$1.5 $μ$s (3GPP TS 38.104), with GNSS-disciplined grandmaster clocks as the predominant timing source. GNSS spoofing -- now a documented operational threat -- can corrupt timing across all downstream base stations, yet neither the 3GPP management framework (SA5) nor the security framework (SA3) provides standardized mechanisms to detect or report such attacks. This paper proposes a detection and monitoring framework operating within existing 3GPP management structures. The framework introduces GNSS timing alarms and performance counters aligned with TS 28.111 and TS 28.552, a topology-aware correlation mechanism that classifies anomalies by grouping gNB-DUs by serving grandmaster, and a security event bridging fault management with SECHAND incident handling (TR 33.894). Monte Carlo simulation demonstrates detection probability exceeding 95% for drift rates above 0.5 ns/s with false positive rates below 1% under well-provisioned PTP network conditions. The framework requires no new interfaces, is generation-agnostic, and is validated through scenario analysis distinguishing spoofing from signal loss, equipment faults, and maintenance transients.

</details>

### [7] [Chiplet3D: Pin- and Thermal-Aware 3D Chiplet Floorplanning via Convolution-Embedded MILP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09742)
*Shuo Ren,Libo Shen,Yaohui Han,Rongliang Fu,Junying Huang,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了Chiplet3D，一种面向双层3D-IC的引脚与热感知布图规划工具，通过精确引脚定位与热场建模协同优化，显著降低线长与峰值温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着摩尔定律放缓，3D-IC垂直堆叠带来严重的热聚集问题。现有布图规划方法在引脚连接与温度估算上的简化假设导致线长优化失准且热点未解，亟需更精确的早期热解决方案。

Method: 提出Chiplet3D布图规划器，采用精确引脚位置计算线长并支持模块旋转翻转；将快速粗粒度卷积热场嵌入混合整数线性规划（MILP）中以准确追踪三维热分布。

Result: 在ICCAD'24 ATPlace基准测试上，Chiplet3D平均降低线长39%–43%（最佳62%），峰值温度降低高达45.9°C，热不均匀性减少高达56%，均优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过引脚对齐与热场的协同优化，Chiplet3D建立了热感知布局与互连效率之间更强的帕累托前沿，验证了早期布图规划阶段协同优化的有效性。

Abstract: As traditional Moore's Law scaling slows down, 3D-ICs stack multiple active dies vertically to sustain performance scaling. However, this vertical stacking traps heat inside, making temperature a design concern. Although we can fix thermal issues at different design steps, floorplanning is the earliest and most cost-effective stage to solve it. Previous methods handle this by assuming wires connect to block centers and estimating temperature through simplistic power-based calculations, but these assumptions mislead their wirelength optimization and leave hotspots unresolved. To address these limitations, we present Chiplet3D, a pin- and thermal-aware floorplanner for two-die 3D-ICs. To achieve pin-awareness, it supports all four rotations and two flips, measuring wirelength from exact pin locations so the solver can flip or rotate blocks to pull connected pins closer. On the thermal side, Chiplet3D replaces the inaccurate power-based metrics of prior work with a fast, coarse convolution field embedded directly in a mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to accurately track the true 3D heat spread. We evaluate Chiplet3D on the ICCAD'24 ATPlace benchmarks, validating every temperature with a golden 3D-ICE simulation. Chiplet3D reduces wirelength by 39\%--43\% on average (and up to 62\% in the best case), while lowering peak temperatures by up to 45.9$^\circ$C and reducing thermal non-uniformity by up to 56\% compared to the SOTA baselines. Overall, these results demonstrate that by co-optimizing pin alignment and thermal fields, Chiplet3D establishes a stronger Pareto frontier between thermal-aware layout and interconnect efficiency.

</details>


### [8] [WINT: A Novel Weighted Integer Representation with Improved Error Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09775)
*Cheng-Yen Lee,Zach Assad,Gautham Nemani,Sunil P. Khatri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了WINT（加权整数）格式，这是一种可配置的尾数指数数字格式，允许用户在2-3个指数位配置下，相比整数基线实现12-50%的平均相对误差降低和2-16倍的动态范围扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嵌入式系统和边缘机器学习等应用中，存在严格的内存约束，但同时需要大动态范围的数据表示。现有的数字表示方案难以在低错误率和大动态范围之间取得良好平衡，因此需要一种简单、可配置且能在设计时进行精度与范围权衡的数字格式。

Method: 提出WINT格式，采用用户可选的尾数和指数位分配机制。建立完整的平均相对误差(MRE)分析框架作为主要误差度量指标。针对精确MRE计算随尾数大小呈指数增长的问题，开发谐波和泰勒级数近似方法，将时间复杂度降至O(1)。

Result: 泰勒级数和谐波近似方法相比精确方法实现显著加速，准确度保持在0.2%以内。在8-32位配置的实验中：(1)分配2个指数位在12位及以上位宽下，MRE降低12-33%，动态范围扩大2倍；(2)分配3个指数位在16位及以上位宽下，动态范围扩大16倍，MRE降低15-50%。

Conclusion: WINT格式成功实现了应用特定的精度与范围权衡，通过简单的可配置设计，在保持低错误率的同时显著扩展了动态范围，为内存受限应用提供了有效的数字表示解决方案。

Abstract: In computing, there is a need for number representation schemes that provide large dynamic range with low error. Many applications, including embedded systems and edge machine learning, have stringent memory constraints yet require large dynamic range for data representation. We present Weighted Integer (WINT), a simple and configurable mantissa exponent number format with user-selectable mantissa (m) and exponent (e) bit allocations (also referred to as configurations) that enables application-specific precision versus range tradeoffs at design time. We develop a complete analytical framework for computing Mean Relative Error (MRE), the primary metric for characterizing WINT's error. Since exact MRE calculations grow exponentially with mantissa size, we introduce harmonic and Taylor series approximation methods that achieve O(1) time complexity regardless of configuration. The Taylor series and harmonic approximations demonstrate significant speedups over the exact method while maintaining accuracy within 0.2% for the configurations presented. Our experiments across 8 to 32-bit configurations show that allocating 2 exponent bits consistently yields both lower MRE by 12-33% and 2X greater range than the integer baseline for bit widths of 12 and above. Allocating 3 exponent bits extends range by 16X while reducing MRE by 15-50% for bit widths of 16 and above

</details>


### [9] [FlashAccel: Leveraging High-Bandwidth Flash for High-Throughput LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10186)
*Xinyu Wang,Yalong Xue,Xiaotian Sun,Xiaoyu Zhang,Chunmeng Dou,Xueqi Li,Xiaoming Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了FlashAccel系统，通过将高带宽闪存（HBF）集成到GPU中，解决了大语言模型推理中的内存容量瓶颈问题，实现了2.54倍的吞吐量和1.93倍的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理面临HBM容量限制瓶颈，模型权重和KV缓存快速增长导致内存需求超出HBM承载能力。HBF虽然提供更高容量且保持相当带宽，但其高访问延迟、低带宽利用率以及缺乏异构资源管理支持，使其难以直接应用于GPU LLM推理。

Method: 提出FlashAccel协同设计系统，包含三个关键技术：1）架构层面的HBF集成与访问延迟缓解机制；2）针对模型权重和KV缓存的专用数据布局优化带宽利用率；3）HBF感知的存储管理层与编程模型，实现持久化数据组织和异构内存资源系统级协调。

Result: 实验表明，在GPU中集成六个HBF堆栈后，在100ms延迟约束下，FlashAccel相比纯HBM GPU平均实现吞吐量提升2.54倍，能效提升1.93倍。

Conclusion: FlashAccel成功证明了HBF作为LLM推理内存扩展方案的可行性，通过软硬件协同设计有效克服了HBF的技术挑战，为容量受限的大语言模型推理提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is increasingly limited by the capacity of High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) in GPUs, as model weights and KV cache grow rapidly. High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) provides higher capacity than HBM while retaining comparable bandwidth, making it a promising substrate for capacity-constrained LLM inference. However, its inherently high access latency, low bandwidth utilization, and lack of support for heterogeneous resource management make it difficult to integrate HBF into GPUs for LLM inference. We present FlashAccel, a co-designed system that enables efficient LLM inference using HBF. FlashAccel integrates HBF into HBM-based GPUs, providing architectural support to mitigate access latency. It improves bandwidth utilization through specialized data layouts for both model weights and KV cache, and introduces an HBF-aware storage management layer together with a programming model to organize persistent data in HBF and coordinate heterogeneous memory resources at the system level. Experimental results demonstrate that integrating six HBF stacks into the GPU enables FlashAccel to deliver an average improvement of 2.54$\times$ and 1.93$\times$ in throughput per GPU and energy efficiency over the HBM-only GPU under 100ms latency constraint, respectively.

</details>


### [10] [When Fuzzing Meets Understanding: LLM-Driven Semantic Test Generation for RTL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10340)
*Kun Wang,Cangyuan Li,Kaiyan Chang,Siyang Cai,Yinhe Han,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChipFuzzer，一个利用大语言模型(LLM)语义推理能力来增强硬件模糊测试效果的框架，通过覆盖率引导和缺陷引导的双阶段工作流，显著提升了条件覆盖率和缺陷检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代芯片复杂性不断提高给硬件验证带来了巨大挑战。现有的覆盖率引导硬件模糊测试工具主要依赖启发式策略，缺乏对被测设计(DUT)内部逻辑和语义行为的推理能力，导致难以实现高覆盖率并发现边界情况缺陷。

Method: 提出ChipFuzzer框架，采用双阶段工作流：(1)覆盖率引导阶段：利用控制流相似性和差异性分析来指导LLM驱动的测试用例生成，从而提高覆盖率；(2)缺陷引导阶段：利用历史缺陷数据识别易出错代码区域，并优先为这些区域生成测试用例，从而增强缺陷发现效率。

Result: 在三个开源CPU设计上的实验结果表明，与最强基线相比，ChipFuzzer将平均条件覆盖率提高了5.8个百分点，缺陷检测率提高了21.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过结合大语言模型的语义推理能力与覆盖率引导和缺陷引导的双阶段策略，ChipFuzzer有效解决了现有硬件模糊测试工具在覆盖率和缺陷检测方面的局限性，为硬件验证提供了一种更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing complexity of modern chips poses significant challenges to hardware verification. In recent years, coverage-guided fuzzing has emerged as a promising approach for improving verification efficiency. However, existing hardware fuzzers still struggle to achieve high coverage and expose corner-case bugs, as they predominantly rely on heuristic strategies with limited ability to reason about the internal logic and semantic behavior of the design under test (DUT). In this work, we propose ChipFuzzer, a hardware fuzzing framework that leverages the semantic reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) to improve fuzzing effectiveness. ChipFuzzer adopts a dual-stage workflow comprising a Coverage-Guided stage and a Bug-Guided stage. In the Coverage-Guided stage, ChipFuzzer employs control-flow similarity and discrepancy analysis to guide LLM-driven testcase generation, thereby improving coverage. In the Bug-Guided stage, ChipFuzzer leverages historical bug data to identify bug-prone code regions and prioritize testcase generation for those regions, thus enhancing bug discovery efficiency. Experimental results on three open-source CPU designs show that ChipFuzzer improves average condition coverage by 5.8 percentage points and bug detection rate by 21.1 percentage points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [11] [Dynamic Rowhammer Threshold Management:Temperature-Aware Threshold Degradation for In-DRAM Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10392)
*Aziz Alajmi,Hoeseok Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态Rowhammer阈值管理方法，可根据运行时温度调整防御阈值，有效降低Rowhammer攻击风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的In-DRAM Rowhammer防御机制在制造时固定了缓解阈值，但真实的Rowhammer阈值（TRHD）实际上会随运行时温度变化而变化，导致防御效果不佳。

Method: 提出动态Rowhammer阈值管理（Dynamic Rowhammer Threshold Management），这是一种防御无关的运行时层。该方法通过线性-T模型结合VRD启发的保护带宽g，根据观测到的温度每个周期重新校准各防御的阈值，并将结果映射到SALT-C、PRAC和TRR等防御机制上。

Result: 该方法将PRAC在85°C下的72次陈旧性违规降为零；在σ=0.10时，调整保护带宽g可将PRAC违规从38.4（g=1.0）降至9.6（g=0.9）。SALT-C从10次名义静态违规降至2次（动态）再到0次（bootstrap），延迟增加≤5.1%。对于TRR，该方法作为诊断工具使用。

Conclusion: 动态Rowhammer阈值管理能够有效解决传统静态阈值防御机制的局限性，显著提高防御效果，为In-DRAM Rowhammer防御提供了一种实用的运行时解决方案。

Abstract: In-DRAM Rowhammer defenses pin the mitigation threshold at manufacture time, yet the true Rowhammer Threshold (TRHD) varies with runtime temperature. We propose \emph{Dynamic Rowhammer Threshold Management}, a defense-agnostic runtime layer that re-sources each defense's threshold from the observed temperature once per epoch via a linear-$T$ model with a VRD-motivated guardband $g$, projecting the result onto SALT-C, PRAC, and TRR through each defense's threshold parameter. A decoupled oracle that scales physical TRHD per-DIMM by $δ\sim \mathrm{N}(1, σ)$ breaks model self-consistency. The layer drives PRAC's 72 staleness breaches at 85$^\circ$C to zero; at $σ{=}0.10$, sweeping $g$ collapses PRAC breaches from 38.4 ($g{=}1.0$) to 9.6 ($g{=}0.9$). SALT-C drops from 10 nominal-static breaches to 2 (Dynamic) to 0 (bootstrap), at $\leq$5.1\% latency. TRR is capacity-limited; the layer acts as a diagnostic.

</details>


### [12] [Soft-Error Characterization and Hardening Trade-offs in Static PCHB Asynchronous Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10788)
*Ramya Karri,Srija Rasoori,Ashiq A. Sakib*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了静态预充电半缓冲器(PCHB)电路的软错误特性，通过故障注入框架识别脆弱节点，并评估了四种加固策略的效果与开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预充电半缓冲器(PCHB)是一种适用于恶劣环境的异步数字设计范式，但其软错误特性尚未得到充分探索和研究，缺乏系统的脆弱性分析和加固指导。

Method: 开发了受控的晶体管级故障注入框架以提取内部节点的极性相关临界电荷；基于大量仿真识别脆弱节点；实现了四种加固策略（双端施密特触发器、单端施密特触发器、传输门增强、基于复制的冗余）并在五个代表性PCHB单元上进行评估。

Result: 获得了内部节点极性相关的临界电荷特性；识别出脆弱性节点；完成了四种加固策略在延迟、能耗和面积方面的全面韧性-开销对比分析。

Conclusion: 提出了针对鲁棒静态PCHB设计的架构特定加固指导原则，为异步电路在恶劣环境下的可靠设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Pre-Charge Half Buffer (PCHB) is a promising asynchronous digital design paradigm for harsh-environment operation; however, its soft-error characteristics remain largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic soft-error characterization and hardening trade-off analysis for static PCHB circuits. A controlled transistor-level fault-injection framework is developed to extract polarity-dependent critical charge at internal nodes. Vulnerability nodes are identified based on extensive simulation. Four mitigation strategies, double-sided Schmitt trigger, single-sided Schmitt trigger, transmission-gate reinforcement, and duplication-based redundancy, are implemented and evaluated across five representative PCHB cells. Comprehensive resilience-overhead comparisons in delay, energy, and area are reported, leading to architecture-specific hardening guidelines for robust static PCHB design.

</details>


### [13] [Edge Physical AI Deployment of Vision Transformers on Heterogeneous Edge GPU Targeting Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10942)
*Ashiyana Abdul Majeed,Mahmoud Meribout,Neethu Joseph,Abel Kidane Haile,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了H-FraDS硬件感知调度方法，通过将帧在GPU和双DLA核心之间分发，并适配不兼容的transformer组件，在边缘GPU上实现了2.36倍加速和实时推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI系统（如自动驾驶车辆）需要满足严格的边缘延迟和能耗约束的基于transformer的感知模型。然而，异构边缘GPU部署受到硬件引擎利用不足和加速器不兼容算子的限制，导致执行碎片化和每瓦吞吐量降低。

Method: 提出异构帧分发调度（H-FraDS）方法，使用固定分发比例将帧路由到GPU和双深度学习加速器（DLA）核心。通过重塑张量、用tanh近似误差函数（ERF）、用有界tanh替换层归一化来适配不兼容的transformer组件以支持DLA执行，并利用光流加速器（OFA）进行推理侧光流估计。

Result: 在使用Swin Transformer进行自动驾驶感知任务中，H-FraDS平衡调度（1:2）实现125.93 FPS（相比独立适配DLA执行2.36倍加速）、4.0 FPS/W、约24ms DLA延迟，满足30 FPS实时操作要求；GPU-DLA-OFA场景实现2.02倍DLA吞吐量加速。适配模型保持92% F1分数，仅比原始模型降低2%。

Conclusion: H-FraDS成功解决了异构边缘GPU部署中的硬件利用和不兼容算子问题，在不显著牺牲精度的情况下显著提升了transformer推理性能，满足实时自动驾驶感知的严格要求。

Abstract: Physical AI systems, such as autonomous vehicles and intelligent machines, require transformer-based perception models that satisfy stringent edge latency and energy constraints. However, heterogeneous edge-GPU deployment remains limited by underutilized hardware engines and accelerator-incompatible operators, causing fragmented execution and lower throughput per watt. This paper presents Heterogeneous Frame Dispatch Scheduling (H-FraDS), a hardware-aware frame scheduling methodology for transformer inference on a recent NVIDIA edge GPU. H-FraDS routes frames across the GPU and dual deep learning accelerator (DLA) cores using fixed dispatch ratios to improve utilization under latency and power constraints. To enable scheduling, incompatible transformer components are adapted for DLA execution by reshaping tensors, approximating error function (ERF) with tanh, and replacing layer normalization with bounded tanh. The adapted model maintains a 92% F1 score, with only a 2% reduction from the original. Optical flow accelerator (OFA) is further used for inference-side optical-flow estimation. To the best of the authors' knowledge, prior work has not addressed these combined issues. Using Swin Transformer for autonomous-driving perception, H-FraDS Balanced Dispatch (1:2) achieves 125.93 FPS, a 2.36x speedup over standalone adapted-DLA execution, 4.0 FPS/W, and approximately 24 ms DLA latency, satisfying 30 FPS real-time operation; the GPU-DLA-OFA case achieves a 2.02x DLA throughput speedup.

</details>


### [14] [IRONSmith: A Visual Dataflow Design Environment for AMD Ryzen AI NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10944)
*Brock Sorenson,Samer Ali,Curt John Bansil,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了IRONSmith，这是首个针对AMD Ryzen AI NPU的可视化数据流设计环境，通过拖拽式界面实现了NPU的无代码编程，降低了专业硬件编程的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习推理越来越多地依赖专用硬件加速器（如NPU）来获得吞吐量和能效优势，如何降低NPU编程的专业门槛成为关键问题。现有的NPU编程需要掌握专门的框架和专业知识，这限制了教育工作者、学生、研究人员和工程师对这些日益普及的硬件资源的使用。

Method: 开发了一个可视化数据流设计环境IRONSmith，提供交互式画布展示AI Engine tile网格作为可视连接块。用户通过连接表示FIFO、split/join模式、广播连接和DDR传输的线来设计ML数据流应用，无需编写代码。计算内核从预构建库中分配，工作函数通过属性面板配置。后端管道自动将可视化设计转换为可执行的IRON Python代码，自动处理结构补全、导入解析和依赖管理。

Result: 通过从单tile向量穿透到多tile矩阵操作再到完整的多层感知机（MLP）等逐渐复杂的ML设计演示了IRONSmith的能力，所有设计均通过可视化方式完成并成功在AMD Ryzen AI NPU上执行。生成的代码可直接在NPU硬件上运行。

Conclusion: IRONSmith通过搭建ML知识与NPU编程专业知识的桥梁，服务于教育工作者、学生、ML研究人员和工程师，扩大了对正快速成为消费级和企业级设备标准的硬件的访问范围，有效降低了专用硬件编程的技术门槛。

Abstract: Machine learning inference increasingly relies on specialized hardware accelerators for throughput and power efficiency. Neural Processing Units (NPUs), such as the AMD Ryzen AI NPU, offer significant ML advantages over CPUs and GPUs, but programming them requires expertise in specialized frameworks. We present IRONSmith, the first visual dataflow design environment for programming AMD Ryzen AI NPUs. IRONSmith provides an interactive canvas displaying the AI Engine tile grid as visually connected blocks, allowing users to design ML dataflow applications by connecting tiles with wires representing FIFOs, split/join patterns, broadcast connections, and DDR transfers without writing any code. Compute kernels are assigned from a pre-built library, and worker functions are configured through property panels. IRONSmith's backend pipeline automatically translates the visual design into executable IRON Python, handling structural completion, import resolution, and dependency management automatically. Generated code executes directly on the AMD Ryzen AI NPU. We demonstrate IRONSmith across ML designs of increasing complexity, from a single-tile vector passthrough to multi-tile matrix operations to a complete Multi-Layer Perceptron, all designed visually and successfully executed on the AMD Ryzen AI NPU. IRONSmith serves educators, students, ML researchers, and engineers by bridging the gap between ML knowledge and NPU programming expertise, widening access to hardware that is rapidly becoming standard across consumer and enterprise devices.

</details>


### [15] [Reliable Associative Lookup in Content-Addressable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11153)
*Fan Li,Yanan Guo,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次针对内容可寻址存储器(CAM)的可靠性问题提出了保护码设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAM作为一种重要的存储范式，通过并行比较实现O(1)查找复杂度，但其可靠性面临底层技术(SRAM和NVM)的挑战。传统内存系统中保护码已广泛研究，但CAM的保护码设计长期未被探索。

Method: 提出了一种非传统的编码设计方案来解决CAM的可靠性问题。

Result: 首次将保护码引入CAM系统，填补了该领域长期存在的研究空白。

Conclusion: 这项工作为CAM可靠性保护奠定了基础，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Content Addressable Memory (CAM) is an important memory paradigm, which performs fast search by comparing an input query against all stored entries in parallel, achieving $O(1)$ lookup complexity. CAM is typically built upon conventional memory technologies, such as SRAM and Non-Volatile Memory (NVM). Accordingly, CAM can also be subject to the reliability challenges of these underlying technologies. In traditional memory systems, protection codes play a critical role in ensuring reliability and have been extensively studied. However, protection codes for CAM have remained largely unexplored. This paper takes an initial step toward addressing this longstanding gap by introducing a non-traditional code design.

</details>


### [16] [From Tool Invocation to Source-Mechanism Exploration: Protected White-Box DSE for Open-Source EDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11294)
*Zhiyu Zheng,Yiming Du,Ziyi Wang,Zhiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ReviewDSE，一个受保护的白盒设计空间探索框架，通过探索源代码级别的机制优化EDA工具，相比传统公开参数的黑盒方法，HPWL平均降低1.78%（对比0.38%），在运行时间约束下仍保持1.68%的优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源EDA工具使设计空间探索能够超越公开参数，深入到分段优化器内部的有界源代码级机制。现有黑盒DSE方法仅能探索公开参数，无法利用源代码级别的优化潜力。

Method: 提出ReviewDSE框架：在受保护评估器下评估完整源代码候选方案，将可复用搜索知识记录为审查机制级证据；通过校准设计构建方法证据和源启动分支；使用固定预热启动产品初始化目标案例探索，在教师审查和全流程验证下进行探索；在OpenROAD详细布局上实例化实现。

Result: 在九个目标任务上，相比公开参数黑盒DSE的0.38%优化，ReviewDSE在2×运行时间约束下将最终后详细布局半周长线长（HPWL）平均降低1.78%；运行时间感知的ReviewDSE选择在1.11×运行时间下仍保持1.68%降低；全流程审查暴露了分段组合失效，源机制探索修复了硬切割行合法性失效。

Conclusion: ReviewDSE成功实现了受保护的白盒DSE，通过源代码级机制探索显著优于传统黑盒方法，在运行时间约束下仍保持良好性能，并能发现和修复分段优化器中的组合性失效和合法性问题。

Abstract: Open-source EDA tools allow design-space exploration (DSE) to move beyond public knobs and into bounded source-level mechanisms inside staged optimizers. We present ReviewDSE, a protected white-box DSE framework that explores such mechanisms for a target design. ReviewDSE evaluates complete source candidates under a protected evaluator and records reusable search knowledge as reviewed mechanism-level evidence. It first constructs method evidence and source-start branches from calibration designs, then uses these fixed warm-start products to initialize target-case exploration under Teacher review and full-flow validation. We instantiate ReviewDSE on OpenROAD detailed placement as a representative staged open-source EDA optimizer. Across nine target tasks, ReviewDSE reduces final post-DPL half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) by 1.78\% on average under a 2$\times$ runtime gate, compared with 0.38\% for public-knob black-box DSE. A runtime-aware ReviewDSE selection retains a 1.68\% reduction at 1.11$\times$ runtime, and full-flow review exposes stage-composability failures while source-mechanism exploration repairs hard cut-row legality failures.

</details>

### [17] [Knowledge Graphs Meet Graph Neural Networks: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09666)
*Chengcheng Sun,Jiayun Tian,Cheng Zhai,Zhixiao Wang,Yajie Song,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了图神经网络在知识图谱技术中的应用，提出了双层分类框架（KG技术流程和GNN视角），涵盖构建、嵌入、推理和应用，总结了优势局限并展望未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN在知识图谱领域应用广泛，但目前缺乏对整个知识图谱技术流程中基于GNN方法的系统性综述，亟需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出一个新颖的双层分类框架：第一层为知识图谱技术流程（包括构建、嵌入、推理和应用）；第二层为GNN视角（如GCN、GAT、HGNN等模型分类），并据此对现有方法进行系统归类和分析。

Result: 基于KG生命周期中不同任务的特性，分析了GNN技术的优势；按照所提分类法详细综述了各类GNN-based KG模型，并总结了它们的优势与局限性。

Conclusion: 讨论了当前研究中未解决的挑战，并为未来研究方向提供了展望和建议。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) due to their intrinsic ability to model graph-structured data. However, there remains a lack of a systematic review about GNN-based methodologies across the entire knowledge graph technologies pipeline. To address this gap, we first propose a novel two-level taxonomy framework for GNN-based knowledge graph technologies: the KG technologies pipeline and GNN-based perspective. Specifically, the knowledge graph technologies pipeline covers knowledge graph construction, knowledge graph embedding, knowledge reasoning and knowledge graph applications. Meanwhile, the GNN-based perspective provides a new categorization of knowledge graph technologies with GNN models, such as GCN, GAT, and HGNN. Then, we analyze the advantages of GNN technology based on the characteristics of different tasks in the knowledge graph lifecycle. Furthermore, we detailed review various GNN-based models for knowledge graph following the proposed taxonomy, and summarize strengths and limitations. Finally, we discuss unresolved challenges and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [18] [Position: Every Ground Truth is a Human Construction, not an Objective Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09668)
*Charlotte Högberg,Ericka Johnson,Kiri L. Wagstaff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文认为机器学习中的基准真值数据集并非客观中立的测量，而是由人类和技术共同构建的。作者主张ML社区应承认基准真值的情境依赖性，通过提高"情境可靠性"来增强模型的可靠性和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习社区普遍将基准真值数据集视为自然给定、客观中立的参考标准，但忽视了这些数据集实际上涉及大量不可见的选择和构建过程。这种忽视可能导致对模型适用范围的误解，影响可靠性评估，并阻碍透明度和问责机制的建立。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的形式，从批判性视角分析基准真值的构建本质。通过理论论证阐述基准真值的情境性、语境依赖性特征，并引入"情境可靠性"（situated reliability）概念框架。

Result: 揭示了基准真值数据集的构建性本质——它们是人类与技术安排的产物而非客观给定。证明了承认基准真值的情境性和非普遍性可以帮助更准确地理解数据集及由此训练的模型的适用边界、限制和优势。提出了提高情境可靠性的实践路径。

Conclusion: 机器学习社区应更加关注基准真值的构建过程，明确报告其中的选择和决策，承认数据集的情境性而非普遍性。这种转变将提升模型的可靠性、透明度和问责性，并促进跨学科合作，最终改善模型在实际应用中的表现和可信度。

Abstract: Ground truth datasets play a fundamental role as reference values in the training and evaluation of machine learning models. This position paper argues that ground truths are not neutral objective measurements that are naturally given, but instead that they are constructed by arrangements of humans and technologies. We argue that the ML community will benefit from articulating and discussing these often invisible or unreported choices and acknowledging that reference data sets are contingent, not universal. Focusing on the situated and context-dependent nature of ground truths can improve reliability by enabling a better informed perspective on where, when, and how the datasets, and the models they have shaped, can best be used. We argue for increasing `situated reliability' which includes articulating the limits and strengths of models and their truth claims. Finally, paying more attention to the construction of ground truths can support transparency, accountability, and interdisciplinary work.

</details>


### [19] [AuditWeave: A Tamper-Evident, Auditor-Navigable Evidence Layer for AI-Assisted and Data-Transformation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09682)
*Vimal Nakrani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了AuditWeave，一个轻量级Python库，用于记录AI辅助决策流程到不可篡改的哈希链账本中，实现端到端的证据溯源和完整性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地用于审计、金融和医疗等监管领域的辅助决策，这带来了一个持续的合规义务：组织必须能够事后重构某个结论所依据的证据，并证明该推理记录未被篡改。现有工具主要面向机器学习工程师，解决模型可观测性、漂移监控和治理报告等问题，而非面向需要追溯特定结论证据来源的审查人员。

Method: 提出AuditWeave，一个无运行时依赖的轻量级Python库，将AI辅助和数据转换工作流程的步骤记录到单一的仅追加、哈希链式账本中。设计了小型、系统无关的事件词汇表，覆盖检索增强生成（RAG）管道和表格/数据湖转换，使结合两者的结论可通过一条记录端到端追溯。通过哈希链结构，任何对事件的修改、重排序、插入或删除都可通过链验证检测。

Result: 在参考实现上评估了记录开销、可扩展性和篡改检测正确性。完整性保证每个事件仅需数十微秒开销；在2000次随机试验中，验证成功标记了四类篡改突变中的每一次注入突变。

Conclusion: AuditWeave以极低的性能开销提供了可靠的AI决策过程完整性保证，能够有效检测各类篡改行为，满足监管领域对决策可追溯性和不可篡改性的需求。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to assist consequential decisions in regulated domains such as auditing, finance, and healthcare. This creates a recurring obligation: an organization must be able to reconstruct, after the fact, which evidence informed a given conclusion, and to show that the record of that reasoning was not altered. Existing tools address related but distinct problems - model observability, drift monitoring, governance reporting - and are built for the machine-learning engineer operating a system, not the reviewer who must trace one specific conclusion back to its supporting evidence. We present AuditWeave, a lightweight Python library, with no runtime dependencies, that records the steps of AI-assisted and data-transformation workflows into a single append-only, hash-chained ledger. A small, system-agnostic event vocabulary spans both retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines and tabular/lakehouse transformations, so a conclusion that draws on both can be traced end-to-end through one record. Within a sealed ledger, any modification, reordering, insertion, or deletion of events is detectable through chain verification. We describe the design and evaluate recording overhead, scalability, and tamper-detection correctness on the reference implementation. The integrity guarantees cost tens of microseconds per event, and, as the hash-chain construction implies, verification flagged every injected mutation across four mutation classes over 2,000 randomized trials.

</details>


### [20] [Ablation, Statistical Inference, and Validation for KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09683)
*Paolo D'Alberto,Ashish Siarasao,Elliott Delaye,Rajeev Patwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了Turbo-Quant和SpectralQuant KV-cache压缩方法，发现基于特征基的方法在重尾数据上因协方差不稳定而失效，但在结构化场景中表现优异；有效语义维度($d_{eff}$)主要适应校准预算而非真实数据秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对KV-cache压缩领域中不同量化方法的性能比较缺乏系统性统计分析的问题，旨在通过严格的统计验证方法，区分编解码器的系统性差异与实现方差，以识别真正优越的压缩方案。

Method: 采用统计验证方法系统比较Turbo-Quant和SpectralQuant KV-cache压缩技术；评估包括WHT旋转配Beta Lloyd-Max和QJL在内的非支配方案；分离系统性编解码差异与实现方差；分析基于特征基方法在不同数据分布下的表现。

Result: 基于特征基的方法在重尾数据上因协方差不稳定性而失效，但在结构化数据场景中表现优异；有效语义维度($d_{eff}$)并非反映真实数据秩，而是适应校准预算；成功识别出非支配的压缩方案。

Conclusion: KV-cache压缩方法的选择应考虑数据分布特性：基于特征基的方法适用于结构化数据场景；校准预算对压缩效果有重要影响；统计验证方法能够有效区分算法本质差异与实现细节导致的方差。

Abstract: This study systematically compares Turbo-Quant and SpectralQuant KV-cache compression, evaluating non-dominated schemes, including WHT rotation with Beta Lloyd-Max and QJL, through a statistical validation methodology that separates systematic codec differences from implementation variance. Key findings reveal that while eigenbasis-based methods fail on heavy-tailed data due to covariance instability, they excel in structured regimes, with the effective semantic dimension ($d_{eff}$) adapting to calibration budgets rather than true data rank. (this is an abstract of the abstract thank you )

</details>


### [21] [SciML in the Wild: A Diagnostic Study of When Structural Priors Help and When They Hurt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09684)
*Vrishank Sai Anand,Prathamesh Dinesh Joshi,Raj Abhijit Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在宏观经济预测任务中，结构先验可能失效的SciML基准测试：约束较少的模型（ARIMA、NODE）表现优于结构约束强的模型（PINN、UDE），表明先验不匹配时会成为错误正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SciML方法（如NODEs、PINNs、UDEs）通常假设结构先验能够反映可靠的控制动态。本文旨在探究当这一假设被违反时会发生什么，以宏观经济学预测作为压力测试领域来验证结构先验的有效性。

Method: 在23个国家的稀疏年度数据集上评估五个模型家族（ARIMA、LSTM、NODE、PINN、UDE），采用多个时间划分和五个随机种子进行系统性比较实验。

Result: 所有模型的预测表现均不理想，揭示了低频宏观经济预测的固有难度。但存在明确的相对层级：约束较少的模型（ARIMA和NODE）持续优于结构约束强的启发式先验模型（PINN和UDE）。

Conclusion: 结构先验在与数据生成过程不匹配时会成为"错误正则化器"。识别了先验错位、制度转换、结构性断裂和优化不稳定等失效模式，建议SciML实践者在假设结构有益之前先验证结构是否真正有效。

Abstract: Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) methods such as Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (NODEs), Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), and Universal Differential Equations (UDEs) are most effective when structural priors reflect reliable governing dynamics. We ask what happens when this assumption is violated. Using macroeconomic forecasting as a stress-test domain, we evaluate five model families, ARIMA, LSTM, NODE, PINN, and UDE, across 23 countries using sparse annual data, multiple temporal splits, and five random seeds. Our results show that none of the evaluated models achieve consistently strong forecasting performance, highlighting the difficulty of low-frequency macroeconomic prediction. However, a clear relative hierarchy emerges: less-constrained models, particularly ARIMA and NODE, consistently outperform more-constrained heuristic-prior models such as PINN and UDE. Rather than treating this as a rejection of SciML, we interpret it as a diagnostic result: structural priors can act as misregularizers when they do not match the data-generating process. We identify failure modes including prior misalignment, regime shifts, structural breaks, and optimization instability, and argue that SciML practitioners should test whether structure helps before assuming that more structure is beneficial.

</details>


### [22] [MawForge: Memory-Bounded Expert Materialization for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09686)
*Craig Opie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MawForge提出了一种针对受限统一内存机器的本地MoE推理系统，通过将完整模型存储在磁盘、保持常用张量常驻、按需将路由专家张量加载到有界执行缓存中来降低内存需求，其有效性取决于专家复用与常驻内存占用之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家语言模型虽然在理论上分离了总参数量和每token的计算量，但在本地推理系统中仍面临内存挑战：需要将完整模型、KV缓存、运行时缓冲区和操作系统预留空间全部加载到快速内存中，这限制了在资源受限设备上的部署。

Method: MawForge采用磁盘存储架构：（1）将完整模型存储在磁盘上；（2）保持常用张量常驻内存；（3）按需将路由专家张量动态加载到有界的执行缓存中。该系统作为有界执行机制和测量平台，而非缓存最大化策略。

Result: MawForge作为有界执行机制和测量平台是有效的，但作为缓存最大化策略效果不佳。其性能取决于多个因素的综合平衡：专家复用率、常驻内存占用、KV缓存大小、量化方案、路由局部性以及macOS内存压力。

Conclusion: 本地MoE服务在受限统一内存机器上是可行的，但需要在专家复用与常驻内存占用之间进行仔细平衡。系统设计应将其视为有界执行机制而非单纯追求缓存最大化，需综合考虑KV缓存、量化、路由局部性和操作系统内存管理等多方面因素。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models separate total parameter count from per-token active computation, but local inference systems often still require the full model, key-value cache, runtime buffers, and operatingsystem headroom to fit in fast memory. MawForge tests a different systems hypothesis: local MoE serving can be made practical on constrained unified-memory machines by storing the full model on disk, keeping common tensors resident, and materializing routed expert tensors into a bounded execution cache on demand. The central finding is that MawForge is effective as a bounded execution mechanism and measurement substrate for local MoE inference, but not as a cache-maximization policy. Performance depends on balancing expert reuse against resident footprint, KV-cache size, quantization, route locality, and macOS memory pressure.

</details>


### [23] [JEPA for AI-Native 6G: Predictive Representations and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09798)
*Sheikh Salman Hassan,Irshad A. Meer,Almoatssimbillah Saifaldawla,Yan Kyaw Tun,Mustafa Ozger,Madyan Alsenwi,Nguyen Van Huynh,Woong-Hee Lee,Cedomir Stefanovic,Mathini Sellathurai,Henk Wymeersch,Tharmalingam Ratnarajah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向6G网络的无线联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）教程，通过自监督学习处理有限的标签数据，并在波束管理案例研究中展示了其提高标签效率和鲁棒性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络正在向AI原生操作转型，需要在无线接入网（RAN）、边缘和核心网中嵌入学习模块。这一转型面临从有限标签学习、异构无线数据、部分观测、非平稳传播以及时延受限控制环路等挑战，亟需一种有效的自监督学习范式来应对这些问题。

Method: 本文提出无线导向的JEPA方法：定义了JEPA训练机制，描述了如何将CSI、波束测量、KPI、拓扑图和感知观测进行标记化和掩码处理，并将学习到的编码器定位为RAN、O-RAN、边缘和核心功能的预测表示层，配合任务特定的头部或控制器做出最终决策。在自监督预训练阶段引入了无线感知目标（辅助未来波束能量目标）。

Result: 通过波束管理案例研究验证，采用无线感知目标的JEPA方法相比监督源域，在部署条件发生变化时能够提高标签效率和鲁棒性，证明了该方法在6G网络智能应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: JEPA作为自监督学习范式在6G网络智能中具有广阔应用前景，但仍面临多时间尺度预测、动作条件建模、分布式训练、可信性、高效部署、基准测试和标准化等开放性挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) networks are moving toward AI-native operation, where learning modules are embedded across the radio access network (RAN), edge, and core. This transition requires learning from limited labels, heterogeneous wireless and network data, partial observations, non-stationary propagation, and latency-constrained control loops. Joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) is a promising self-supervised paradigm for this setting because it predicts missing or future representations in latent space instead of reconstructing raw measurements or using contrastive negative samples. This article presents a wireless-oriented tutorial on JEPA for 6G intelligence. We define the JEPA training mechanism, describe how CSI, beam measurements, KPIs, topology graphs, and sensing observations can be tokenized and masked, and position the learned encoder as a predictive representation layer for RAN, O-RAN, edge, and core functions, with task-specific heads or controllers producing final decisions. Then we present an illustrative, beam-management case study suggesting that a wireless-aware target, specifically an auxiliary future beam-energy target during self-supervised pretraining, can improve label efficiency and robustness across shifted deployment conditions relative to a supervised source domain. Finally, we outline open challenges in multi-timescale prediction, action-conditioned modeling, distributed training, trustworthiness, efficient deployment, benchmarking, and standardization.

</details>


### [24] [Prioritizing Search Space Regions in the Low Autocorrelation Binary Sequences Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09688)
*Blaž Pšeničnik,Borko Bošković,Jan Popić,Janez Brest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Thompson采样与并行自回避游走的混合搜索框架，通过多臂老虎机模型动态分配计算资源，并利用GPU加速和两阶段优化策略，成功改进了36个序列长度下的低自相关二进制序列问题的已知最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低自相关二进制序列问题（LABS）是一个在通信、信号处理和卫星导航领域具有重要应用的组合优化难题，但由于搜索空间巨大，寻找最优解极具挑战性。

Method: 提出了一种混合搜索框架：1) 将搜索空间划分为多个分区，利用Thompson采样在多臂老虎机设置下动态分配计算资源；2) 采用并行自避免游走避免搜索陷入循环；3) 使用GPU并行执行、共享后验更新、高效邻域评估和布隆过滤器进行加速；4) 实施两阶段优化策略，首先在约束的偏斜对称空间搜索，再在无约束空间精细化最优候选。

Result: 在长二进制序列实验中，该方法在450≤L≤527范围内的35个序列长度以及L=573上改进了先前已知最佳结果；特别发现了L=451时Merit Factor超过8.0的新最长序列；验证了Thompson采样能有效优先处理表现更好的分区。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为高性能Merit Factor最大化提供了可扩展且有效的解决方案，证实了在线数据驱动的资源分配在LABS优化中的价值。

Abstract: Low autocorrelation binary sequences problem (LABS) is a hard combinatorial optimization challenge with important applications in communications, signal processing, and satellite navigation. This paper proposes a hybrid search framework that combines Thompson sampling with parallel self-avoiding walks to adaptively allocate computational effort across restriction classes of the LABS search space. By modeling partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit setting, the proposed method dynamically shifts search resources toward partitions that empirically produce higher merit factors while maintaining exploration of less-sampled regions. The approach is further accelerated through GPU-parallel execution, shared posterior updates, efficient neighborhood evaluation, and a Bloom filter for cycle prevention. In addition, we use a two-stage optimization strategy that first searches constrained partitioned skew-symmetric spaces and then refines the best candidates in the unrestricted space. Experiments on long binary sequences show that the proposed method improves the previously best-known results for 35 sequence lengths in the range $450 \le L \le 527$ and for $L=573$. In particular, we report a new longest sequence with merit factor exceeding $8.0$, obtained for $L=451$. The results also show that Thompson sampling effectively prioritizes partitions with better observed performance, confirming the value of online, data-driven resource allocation in LABS optimization. Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable and effective strategy for high-performance merit factor maximization.

</details>


### [25] [What Context Does a Coding Agent Actually Need to Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09691)
*Brian Sam-Bodden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现现代编码代理在编辑代码时所需上下文极少：核心信号仅存在于被编辑代码本身，自然语言摘要无法替代源码，周围上下文影响微乎其微，压缩上下文可节省67%的令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编码代理的上下文窗口可容纳整个代码库，但大部分阅读都是浪费的。研究者质疑代理真正需要多少上下文，特别是在代码编辑这一关键时刻，以理解哪些上下文是必要的。

Method: 将"定位工作站点"与"执行操作"分离，使用预言机固定定位，仅改变代码表示方式。在SWE-bench Verified上评估，对比源码与自然语言摘要、UML骨架表示、压缩上下文等不同表示方法的效果。采用预注册假设和确定性补丁构建。

Result: 结果极度精简：自然语言摘要表现远差于源码(4/45 vs 27/45)；UML骨架表示与直接删除剩余内容无差异(N=70, p=0.75)；压缩上下文以1/3令牌达到完整文件效果(19K vs 94K)。温度为0的API推理在字节相同运行间翻转约9%结果，构成基准噪声下限。

Conclusion: 代码编辑的核心信号仅存在于被编辑代码本身，周围上下文几乎无关。压缩上下文可显著降低成本。研究提供了金标准验证环境、预注册假设等工具，揭示了该基准上小效应研究的噪声下限问题。

Abstract: A modern coding agent can hold an entire repository in its context window. Most of its reading is wasted -- and the interesting question is not how much context an agent can use, but what it actually \emph{needs}. We study that question at the moment it matters most: when the agent must \emph{edit} code. Separating \emph{finding} the work site from \emph{acting} on it, we hold localization fixed with an oracle, vary only how the code is represented, and score context against real issue resolution on SWE-bench Verified. The answer is starkly minimal. The signal lives in the code being edited itself: natural-language summaries of it answer almost none of the behavioral questions that the source answers ($4/45$ vs.\ $27/45$, held-out repositories, independent judge), and the gap belongs to the representation, not the summarizer -- a frontier model's summaries score exactly as poorly as a 3B model's. The surrounding context hardly matters either: across every multi-file instance in Verified, under a protocol frozen before any data, rendering a file's remainder as UML skeletons and signatures resolves no more issues than deleting that remainder outright ($N{=}70$, exact McNemar $p{=}0.75$). That was our registered hypothesis, and it failed. Compressed context, meanwhile, matches whole files at a third of the tokens: a resolved issue costs $19$K context tokens, not $94$K. The instrument also yielded a finding the field should keep: temperature-0 API inference flips ${\sim}9\%$ of per-instance outcomes between byte-identical runs. That is a noise floor under every small effect reported on this benchmark, including ours. We release the instrument -- gold-validated environments, per-instance proof that every reference edit is expressible from every arm's context, deterministic patch construction, and pre-registered hypotheses whose nulls we publish.

</details>


### [26] [HiFi-LLP: High-Fidelity, Low-Cost Latency Predictors with Confidence for Robust HW-NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11746)
*Shambhavi Balamuthu Sampath,Behzad Shomali,Nael Fasfous,Moritz Thoma,Judeson Anthony Fernando,Lukas Frickenstein,Pierpaolo Mori,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Alexander Frickenstein,Walter Stechele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiFi-LLP高保真低延迟预测器，结合置信度指标与混合NAS框架，在保持性能的同时实现高达8.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件感知的DNN优化技术（如压缩和NAS）依赖硬件延迟反馈，但硬件在环（HIL）测量存在顺序性瓶颈。现有延迟预测器存在两大挑战：平台特定预测器需要数万样本；预测不准确会误导NAS过程。

Method: 提出基于图注意力网络的高保真低延迟预测器HiFi-LLP，引入置信度指标。进一步提出混合NAS框架，将低置信度预测路由到HIL进行验证，结合预测器与实际硬件测量。

Result: 在LatBench数据集的六个设备上，HiFi-LLP在10%准确度边界内比先前平台特定预测器提升最高9个百分点，斯皮尔曼等级相关系数达0.996。混合NAS框架相比典型NAS实现最高8.6倍加速，同时保持竞争性的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: HiFi-LLP通过图注意力网络和置信度机制有效解决了延迟预测的准确性和数据效率问题，混合NAS框架成功平衡了搜索效率与架构质量，为边缘设备上的DNN部署提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: With deep neural networks (DNNs) increasingly deployed on edge devices, hardware (HW)-aware optimization techniques--such as HW-aware compression and HW-aware neural architecture search (HW-NAS)--have become essential. These methods rely on real feedback from the target hardware to tailor DNN architectures for efficient deployment. While the search can be parallelized, latency measurements via hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) remain a bottleneck due to their sequential nature. Recent approaches use latency predictors to replace costly HIL feedback, but challenges persist: (1) platform-specific predictors often require tens of thousands of samples, and (2) inaccurate predictions can mislead the NAS process. To address this, we introduce HiFi-LLP, a high-fidelity, low-cost latency predictor based on graph attention networks, augmented with a confidence metric. HiFi-LLP outperforms prior platform-specific predictors by up to 9 percentage points (p.p.) in the 10% accuracy bound and achieves a Spearman's rank correlation of up to 0.996 across six devices in the LatBench dataset. We further propose a hybrid NAS framework that routes low-confidence predictions to HIL, achieving up to 8.6$\times$ speedup compared to typical NAS while maintaining a competitive Pareto front.

</details>


### [27] [Reference-Based Distillation Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09692)
*Rajat Rawat,Sizhe Chen,Akshay Anand,Michael Duan,Bob Rotsted,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于参考模型的成员推断方法，用于检测模型蒸馏并识别教师模型，在单教师蒸馏场景下达到近乎完美的检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型蒸馏（使用更强的第三方模型输出进行训练）被广泛用于提升性能，但引发了对不公平优势和政策违规的担忧。这促使研究者思考一个根本性问题：能否检测一个模型是否通过蒸馏训练而成？识别教师模型对维护AI模型知识产权和合规性具有重要意义。

Method: 提出基于参考模型的成员推断方法进行蒸馏检测。给定一个模型和来自同一血统的早期检查点作为参考，通过比较学生模型相对于参考检查点对来自不同候选教师输出的偏好对齐程度，识别最可能的教师模型。针对未知蒸馏管道（如隐藏提示），直接从模型输出推断代理提示模板。此外识别了o1/o3模型特有的字形级信号。开发统计检验用于教师归属和蒸馏检测，并将框架扩展到开放世界设置。

Result: 开发了混合评估方法，涵盖受控蒸馏实验和真实世界模型。在单教师蒸馏场景下，即使蒸馏管道大部分未知，该方法仍能以近乎完美的准确率恢复真实教师。应用于当代模型（QwQ、DeepSeek-R1和GPT-OSS）发现了潜在的蒸馏关系证据。

Conclusion: 证明了在基于参考的设置下，教师模型识别变得可处理。提出的蒸馏检测方法在实践中高度有效，为AI模型的知识产权保护和合规性监管提供了重要技术手段。统计检验框架支持开放世界设置，增强了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Model distillation -- training on outputs from stronger third-party models -- is widely used to boost performance, but raises concerns about unfair advantages and policy violations. This motivates a fundamental question: can we detect whether a model was distilled from another? We show that, while identifying a teacher model from a student in isolation is highly challenging, it becomes tractable in a reference-based setting: given a model and an earlier-generation checkpoint from the same lineage, we can identify the teacher model used to train the later checkpoint. We introduce a distillation detection method based on reference-based membership inference. By comparing how strongly a student model preferentially aligns with outputs from different candidate teachers relative to a reference checkpoint, our method identifies the most likely teacher and detects evidence of distillation. To handle unknown distillation pipelines such as hidden prompts, we infer proxy prompt templates directly from model outputs. We additionally identify a distinctive glyph-level signal specific to o1/o3 models. Evaluating distillation detection is challenging because modern model lineages are already heavily entangled. To address this, we develop a hybrid evaluation spanning both controlled distillation experiments and real-world models. Across both settings, our approach recovers the true teacher with near-perfect accuracy in single-teacher distillation scenarios, even when the underlying distillation pipeline is largely unknown. We further introduce statistical tests for both teacher attribution and distillation detection, and extend our framework to open-world settings where no teacher is guaranteed to be present among the candidates. Applying our method to contemporary models yields new evidence regarding potential distillation relationships involving QwQ, DeepSeek-R1, and GPT-OSS.

</details>


### [28] [Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling for Training-Free LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09693)
*Zibin Meng,Peng Xie,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DEGS，一种无需训练的测试时采样方法，利用层级熵坍缩信号提升大语言模型推理能力，在多个基准测试上达到最优无训练准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习(RL)虽能提升大语言模型推理能力，但需要昂贵的训练过程、精选数据和奖励信号。现有测试时采样方法仅依赖输出层似然，忽略了transformer内部前向传播动态。作者观察到更强的推理模型表现出独特的"晚期坍缩"现象，这为质量信号提供了新的可能。

Method: 提出Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling (DEGS)：1) 发现强推理模型(包括RL后训练变体)在更深层次前logit-lens解码熵保持较高水平后才收敛；2) 定义每个序列的坍缩深度D(x)；3) 设计联合目标π(x)∝p(x)^α exp(βD(x))，结合序列似然与深度熵结构；4) 在MCMC功率采样框架内实现(DEGS-MCMC)。

Result: 在三个开源权重模型和四个推理基准测试上：1) 在无训练方法中达到最优准确率；2) 在域外和更难的数据分割上提升最大（恰是仅靠似然失效的场景）；3) 单数百分比级的运行时开销；4) 在GRPO训练的数学分割上略逊于GRPO，但在所有三个模型的域外GPQA上都超越GRPO。

Conclusion: DEGS证明了利用transformer内部前向传播动态的层级熵结构可以作为有效的质量信号，在无需训练、奖励模型或标注数据的情况下，实现与RL后训练模型相当甚至更好的推理性能，特别是在域外泛化场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become the dominant paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but it requires expensive training, curated data, and reward signals. Recent work shows that sampling from sharpened base-model distributions at test time recovers much of the RL gain, yet existing methods rely solely on output-layer likelihoods and ignore the transformer's internal forward-pass dynamics. We introduce Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling (DEGS), a training-free, test-time method that exploits layer-wise entropy collapse as an intrinsic quality signal. We observe that stronger reasoners -- including RL-posttrained variants -- exhibit a distinctive "late collapse": logit-lens decoded entropy stays elevated until deeper layers before converging. We define a per-sequence collapse depth $D(\mathbf{x})$ and a joint objective $π(\mathbf{x}) \propto p(\mathbf{x})^α\exp(βD(\mathbf{x}))$ that combines sequence likelihood with this depth-entropy structure, instantiated inside an MCMC power-sampling framework (DEGS-MCMC). Across three open-weight models and four reasoning benchmarks, this near-chance per-candidate signal compounds over the sampling trajectory into state-of-the-art training-free accuracy, with gains largest out of domain and on the harder splits -- exactly where likelihood alone falls short -- at single-digit-percent wall-clock overhead. DEGS narrowly trails an in-house GRPO reference on the math splits GRPO was trained for, yet surpasses it out of domain on GPQA for all three models, without any training, reward model, or labeled data.

</details>


### [29] [Low-Rank Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09694)
*Jonathan Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出低秩注意力残差（LR-AttnRes），通过使用低维键进行深度路由，解决了注意力残差中路由与表示耦合的问题，在减少计算成本的同时提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力残差方法将每个子层输出同时作为全维度的键和值，导致路由与表示耦合，且深度路由分数随隐藏宽度d的规模缩放，影响模型效率与效果。

Method: 提出两种低秩注意力残差变体：(1)投影式LR-AttnRes：从现有输出投影中学习低秩键，解耦路由与残差内容；(2)切片式LR-AttnRes：使用每个值的最后r维作为路由键，移除辅助键投影路径并减少残差侧FLOPs。两种方法均保持全维度残差值，仅使用r维（r远小于d）键进行路由。

Result: 综合实验表明，深度路由可以在远少于模型宽度的维度下有效工作。投影式LR-AttnRes在测试变体中达到最佳验证损失，切片式LR-AttnRes在减少计算量的同时仍能提升性能。

Conclusion: 低秩键路由能够有效解耦路由与表示，降低计算开销并改善模型性能，为LLM中的残差连接机制提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Attention Residuals replace the fixed residual sum with depthwise attention over previous sub-layer outputs in large language models (LLMs), but use each output as both a full-dimensional key and value. This couples routing with representation and makes depth-routing scores scale with the hidden width $d$. We propose Low-Rank Attention Residuals (LR-AttnRes), which keep full-dimensional residual values while using $r$-dimensional keys, with $r \ll d$, for routing. Projected LR-AttnRes emits learned low-rank keys from existing output projections, decoupling routing from residual content and achieving the best validation loss among the variants tested. Sliced LR-AttnRes uses the last $r$ dimensions of each value as the routing key, removing the auxiliary key-projection path and reducing residual-side FLOPs while still improving performance. Comprehensive sweeps show that depthwise routing can be effective with far fewer dimensions than the model width. We release code and models to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [30] [FedCausal-Dyn: A Causal-Dynamic Paradigm for Federated Learning under Dynamic Feature Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09695)
*Kaijie Chen,Alex Johnson,Maria Garcia,Wei Zhang,Daniel Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedCausal-Dyn框架，通过因果动态范式解决联邦学习中的动态特征漂移问题，实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设静态漂移，在非平稳环境中效果有限。现实应用（如金融科技）中数据分布随客户端和时间演变，产生动态特征漂移问题，需要新的解决方案。

Method: 提出FedCausal-Dyn框架，包含三个核心创新：(1)因果-域特征分离，通过专用投影头和对抗训练解耦域不变因果特征与虚假域特定变化；(2)可靠动态原型聚合，根据估计可靠性加权局部类原型后进行全局聚合；(3)因果特征引导的协作正则化，统一原型对比对齐和域不变性目标。

Result: 在三个联邦域泛化基准上进行了大量实验，FedCausal-Dyn持续达到最先进性能，具有最高平均准确率和最稳定的结果。消融实验验证了每个组件的关键贡献。

Conclusion: 本研究为动态特征漂移下的联邦学习提供了稳健且有原则的解决方案，FedCausal-Dyn框架通过因果动态范式有效应对了现实场景中的数据分布演变挑战。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenging problem of dynamic feature drift in federated learning, where data distributions evolve across clients and over time -- a common scenario in real-world applications like financial technology. Existing approaches often assume static drift, limiting their effectiveness in non-stationary environments. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{FedCausal-Dyn}, a novel federated learning framework built on a causal-dynamic paradigm. Its key innovation is \textit{causal-domain feature separation}, which disentangles domain-invariant causal features from spurious, domain-specific variations via specialized projection heads and adversarial training. This enables \textit{reliable and dynamic prototype aggregation}, weighting local class prototypes by estimated reliability before global aggregation. We further introduce \textit{causal-feature guided collaborative regularization}, unifying prototype contrastive alignment and domain invariance into a cohesive objective. Extensive experiments on three federated domain generalization benchmarks demonstrate that FedCausal-Dyn consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, with the highest average accuracy and the most stable results. Ablation studies confirm each component's critical contribution. Our work provides a robust and principled solution for federated learning under dynamic feature drift.

</details>


### [31] [Mitigating Early Training Collapse in CTR Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09696)
*Ergun Biçici,Erkan Çetinyamaç*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究解决了深度神经网络在点击率预测任务中第一个训练epoch后验证性能急剧下降的问题，发现通过控制特征稀疏性（移除高稀疏特征、聚合低频特征值）可以显著稳定训练过程并提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在点击率预测任务中普遍存在一个关键问题：在第一个训练epoch后，尽管训练损失持续下降，但验证性能却急剧下滑。这种不稳定性限制了有效学习和模型性能的进一步提升，需要深入分析并找到有效的缓解策略。

Method: 研究团队基于大规模工业数据集进行实证分析，评估了多种缓解策略。除了常规的学习率调整方法外，重点研究了特征稀疏性控制策略，具体包括移除高稀疏特征和对低频特征值进行聚合处理。

Result: 实验结果表明，降低学习率仅能带来微小的性能提升。相比之下，控制特征稀疏性产生了显著改善：移除高稀疏特征和聚合低频特征值不仅稳定了训练过程，使有效学习能够延续到单个epoch之后，还在离线评估指标和在线系统性能两方面都取得了实质性提升。

Conclusion: 特征稀疏性控制是解决深度学习点击率预测模型训练不稳定问题的有效方法。该策略能够突破首个训练周期的性能瓶颈，实现更持久的有效学习，为工业级推荐系统的模型训练提供了实用的优化方案。

Abstract: Deep neural models for click-through rate prediction often exhibit a sharp decline in validation performance immediately after the first training epoch despite continued improvement in training loss. This instability restricts effective learning and limits model performance. In this study, we analyze this behavior using large-scale industrial datasets and evaluate practical mitigation strategies. While reducing the learning rate provides only incremental gains, controlling feature sparsity yields substantial improvements. Removing highly sparse features and aggregating infrequent feature values stabilizes training, extends useful learning beyond a single epoch, and improves both offline evaluation metrics and online system performance.

</details>


### [32] [Safe responses matter: Output-aware safety guardrail mitigate over-refusal in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09697)
*Jiayi Li,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种输出感知的安全护栏方法（OutGuard），通过预测模型即将生成的输出是否安全来精准干预，解决了现有输入端安全护栏的过度拒绝问题，在保持安全性能的同时显著降低了误判率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型的安全机制存在根本性权衡问题：模型微调虽能实现鲁棒安全但损害通用性；输入端安全护栏虽轻量但存在严重的过度拒绝问题，会无差别阻止良性查询或模型本可安全回答的查询。作者发现过度拒绝的根本原因在于输入感知范式：安全护栏在做出安全决策时未考虑模型本身是否具备生成安全响应的能力，而MLLMs通常已具备将有害输入转化为无害输出的内在安全机制，输入端安全护栏会覆盖这一能力，降低用户体验。

Method: 提出向输出感知安全护栏的范式转变：在模型的隐藏状态空间中预测即将生成的输出是否不安全。通过多实例对比学习在隐藏状态表示上训练轻量级分类器，区分会导致不安全输出和不会导致不安全输出的输入，即使输入本身包含风险元素。该方法仅在模型实际响应会有害时进行精准干预，保留模型对风险输入的内在处理能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在安全性能上与现有方法相当，同时显著降低了过度拒绝率，有效保留了模型的通用性和内置安全能力。通过在隐藏状态空间进行预测，成功实现了对有害输出的精准识别和干预，避免了不必要的输入拦截。

Conclusion: 输出感知的安全护栏为MLLMs提供了一种平衡安全与效用的新范式，通过利用模型隐藏状态信息进行精准预测和干预，有效解决了输入端方法的过度拒绝问题，为构建更智能、更精准的AI安全机制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Existing safety mechanisms for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) face a fundamental trade-off between safety and utility. Model fine-tuning achieves robust safety but compromises general utility. Input-side safety guardrails offer a lightweight alternative, yet they suffer from severe over-refusal, indiscriminately blocking benign queries or those the model could have safely answered through refusal or advisory responses. We identify that the root cause of over-refusal lies in the input-aware paradigm: safety guardrails make safety decisions without considering whether the model itself is capable of generating safe responses. Usually, MLLMs already possess intrinsic safety mechanisms that can transform harmful inputs into harmless outputs, but input-side safety guardrails override this capability, degrading user experience. Motivated by this insight, we propose a paradigm shift toward output-aware safety guardrails. Our method operates within the model's hidden state space to predict whether the forthcoming generation will be unsafe before it is fully produced. By training a lightweight classifier via multi-instance contrastive learning on hidden state representations, our approach distinguishes between inputs that will lead to unsafe outputs and those that will not, even when the inputs themselves contain risky elements. This enables precise intervention only when the model's actual response would be harmful. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our output-aware safety guardrail matches the safety performance of existing methods while drastically reducing over-refusal, preserving the model's utility and built-in safety capabilities. Code is available at: https://github.com/kunzhan/OutGuard

</details>


### [33] [Quantum-Inspired Contextual Learning for Sparse-Ring Fraud Detection in Dynamic Transaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09704)
*Behnam Tonekaboni,Hiroshi Yamauchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对动态金融交易图中稀疏环欺诈的探索性基准测试，发现将拓扑特征与图特征混合的表示方法效果最佳，量子启发的上下文机器学习(CML)模型在此类分布式欺诈检测中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调性欺诈无法从单笔交易中识别，而是表现为跨多时期的关系模式。现有方法难以检测分布在多天内完成的稀疏环欺诈模式，需要模型同时整合时间和图结构的证据。

Method: 构建合成交易模拟器注入完整稀疏环和断环干扰；将每日有向交易图聚合为滑动窗口，采用原始图特征、持久同调摘要和混合特征向量三种表示方法；比较GRU基线模型与量子启发的上下文机器学习(CML)序列分类器。

Result: 纯拓扑摘要因移除账户对身份和边方向信息，无法独立解决监督环完成任务；混合表示方法结合身份保持图特征与拓扑摘要效果最佳；CML模型在跨时序和关系上下文的分布式欺诈模式检测中表现优异。

Conclusion: 拓扑特征最适合作为动态图特征的上下文层，而非独立解决方案；CML是处理跨时间和关系上下文的分布式欺诈模式的有前途的候选模型。

Abstract: We present an exploratory benchmark and quantum-inspired modeling prototype for fraud screening in dynamic financial transaction graphs. Coordinated fraud may not be visible from individual transactions alone, but may emerge as a multi-period relational pattern. We focus on sparse-ring fraud, a stylized pattern in which a completed directed cycle is distributed across several days, requiring models to integrate evidence across both time and graph structure. We study this problem using a synthetic transaction simulator with completed sparse-ring injections and broken-ring decoys. Daily directed transaction graphs are aggregated into rolling windows and represented using raw graph features, persistent-homology summaries, or hybrid feature vectors that combine both. We compare a gated recurrent unit (GRU) baseline with quantum-inspired Contextual Machine Learning (CML) as sequence-level classifiers. Because the benchmark uses synthetic data, a modest sample size, and sequence-level labels, the results are exploratory. Within this scope, topology-only summaries are too compressed to solve the supervised ring-completion task by themselves, largely because they remove account-pair identity and edge direction. The strongest results come from hybrid representations that combine identity-preserving graph features with topological summaries. These findings suggest that topology is most useful as a contextual layer over dynamic graph features, and that CML is a promising candidate model for fraud patterns whose evidence is distributed across temporal and relational context.

</details>

*Tian Li,Lucy Robinson,Varun Ojha,Huizhi Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HDE-Net，一种在双曲空间中进行层次决策建模的表格深度神经网络，通过将异构特征抽象为潜在决策节点并嵌入Poincaré球，有效解决了传统表格DNNs的几何不匹配问题，在30个数据集上取得最佳平均排名。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格分类通常由局部的、条件触发的规则支配，而非平滑的全局模式。然而，现有的表格深度神经网络构建于欧氏表示之上，偏好平滑变化和语义局部性。这种几何不匹配使得表格DNNs难以有效表示表格分类中常见的离散、规则分区结构。

Method: 提出HDE-Net，一种约束在双曲空间上的DNN：1）将异构特征抽象为统一的潜在决策节点(LDNs)并嵌入Poincaré球，形成类似树结构推理的连续表示；2）为数值特征引入软决策路由机制，以可微分方式近似基于范围的局部规则；3）设计熵感知容量分配算法，自适应调整每个数值特征的LDN数量以平衡表达能力和复杂度。

Result: 在TALENT-tiny-core分类基准测试（30个数据集）上，HDE-Net取得了最佳平均排名，超越了工业级GBDTs和最新的表格DNNs，同时保持高效性。

Conclusion: 通过在双曲空间中对表格数据进行层次决策建模，HDE-Net成功解决了传统欧氏表示与表格分类任务中规则分区结构之间的几何不匹配问题，为表格深度学习提供了新的几何视角和有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular classification is often governed by local, condition-triggered rules rather than smooth global patterns. However, tabular deep neural networks (DNNs) are typically built upon Euclidean representations that favor smooth variations and semantic locality. This potential geometric mismatch can make it challenging for tabular DNNs to efficiently represent the discrete, rule-partitioned structures often underlying tabular classification. To address this issue, we propose HDE-Net, a manifold-constrained DNN that enables hierarchical decision modeling in hyperbolic space. We first abstract heterogeneous features into unified Latent Decision Nodes (LDNs) and embed them in the Poincaré ball, forming a continuous representation that resembles tree-structured reasoning. For numerical features, we introduce a Soft Decision Routing mechanism that approximates range-based local rules in a differentiable manner, bringing their LDN semantics closer to those of categorical features. An entropy-aware capacity allocation algorithm further adapts the number of LDNs per numerical feature to balance expressiveness and complexity. On the TALENT-tiny-core classification benchmark (30 datasets), HDE-Net achieves the \textit{best average rank}, outperforming both industrial GBDTs and recent tabular DNNs while maintaining high efficiency.

</details>
