TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于医疗智能体的综述论文，从临床部署视角出发，提出了三级别自主性分类法、统一扩展框架（框架、能力、环境扩展），指出临床环境扩展和临床自进化是关键研究方向，并探讨了在放射科、病理科等领域的应用及部署挑战，为构建可信的自改进医学影像系统提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多从能力优先视角研究医疗智能体，而本文从临床部署的实际需求出发，探讨医疗智能体在被信任和应用于临床实践之前所需的任务、抗污染基准和交互式训练环境，推动医疗智能体从任务特定预测器向能够感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动的自主系统转变。

Method: 将医疗智能体形式化为部分可观测下的序贯决策系统，提出涵盖辅助、协作和完全自主操作的三级别自主性分类法；构建由框架扩展、能力扩展和环境扩展组成的统一扩展框架；系统梳理了临床环境扩展（工具、数据、临床训练场馆的整合）和临床自进化（智能体通过环境交互而非仅靠参数扩展实现改进）两个核心研究方向。

Result: 识别出临床环境扩展（PACS、EHR、FHIR生态系统中的智能体操作）是当前最可行但研究不足的方向；定位临床自进化为关键研究前沿；系统分析了放射科、病理科、眼科和医院工作流中的应用，以及幻觉、级联失效和公平性等部署挑战；整合了300余篇参考文献（重点关注2025-2026年进展）。

Conclusion: 本研究为构建可信、自改进的医学影像系统提供了系统性路线图，强调临床环境扩展和临床自进化是未来研究的关键方向，推动医疗智能体向实际临床实践的安全部署迈进。

Abstract: The growing ability of large language models and vision language models to jointly interpret and reason over images and text is reshaping medical agents, moving them from task specific predictors toward autonomous systems that perceive, reason, plan, remember, and act in clinical environments. This work departs from the capability first perspective of existing literature and instead begins from clinical deployment, asking what tasks, contamination resistant benchmarks, and interactive training environments are required before medical agents can be trusted in practice. Medical agents are formalized as sequential decision making systems under partial observability, together with a three level autonomy taxonomy spanning assisted, cooperative, and fully autonomous operation. The field is organized along a unified scaling spine consisting of framework scaling, capability scaling, and environment scaling. Within this framework, clinical environment scaling, the integration of tools, data, and clinical gyms, is identified as the most actionable yet underexplored direction for agents operating in PACS, EHR, and FHIR ecosystems. Clinical self evolution, where agents improve through interaction with their environments rather than parameter scaling alone, is further positioned as a key research frontier, drawing insights from self improving agents, agent gyms, and test time compute scaling. Applications across radiology, pathology, ophthalmology, and hospital workflows are examined together with deployment challenges including hallucination, cascading failures, and fairness. By consolidating more than 300 references, with particular emphasis on advances from 2025 to 2026, this work provides a roadmap toward trustworthy, self improving medical imaging systems for real clinical practice.

</details>


### [277] [SCALECUA: Scaling Computer Use Agents with Verifiable Task Synthesis and Efficient Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11185)
*Bowen Lv,Xiao Liu,Yanyu Ren,Hanyu Lai,Bohao Jing,Hanchen Zhang,Yanxiao Zhao,Shuntian Yao,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ScaleCUA框架，通过合成可验证任务和高效训练策略解决计算机使用智能体在线强化学习的数据稀缺和训练效率问题，在OSWorld和ScienceBoard基准上取得开源最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体(CUAs)通过视觉感知和GUI执行自动化复杂数字工作流具有巨大潜力，在线强化学习(RLVR)是提升其能力的关键方向。但该范式面临两大瓶颈：可验证数据稀缺问题，以及在线强化学习训练效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出ScaleCUA统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)VeriGen：端到端框架，通过迭代docker交互和多智能体反馈循环生成可验证RL任务，支持100+并发智能体工作器，生成24K+可验证任务；(2)Frontier Sampling：追踪每任务能力，将rollout分配到当前学习前沿以最大化样本效率；(3)Visual Context Segmentation：在近期视觉上下文上的滑动窗口机制，平衡rollout和训练引擎压力，实现2.83倍训练加速。

Result: 生成24K+可验证任务和近3K高质量RL任务；训练速度相比逐步分解方法提升2.83倍；在OSWorld基准上达到68.7%准确率，在ScienceBoard基准上达到54.0%准确率，建立了开源计算机使用智能体的新state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: ScaleCUA框架有效解决了CUA在线强化学习的数据和效率瓶颈，通过可验证任务合成和高效训练策略的协同设计，显著提升了智能体性能，为开源计算机使用智能体设定了新的性能基准，代码、模型和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs) are emerging as a powerful interface for automating complex digital workflows through visual perception and GUI execution. Online reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a key direction for scaling their capabilities. However, this paradigm is bottlenecked by verifiable data scarcity and online RL inefficiency. To break these barriers, we introduce ScaleCUA, a unified framework that scales online RL for CUAs via verifiable task synthesis and efficient training. At the data level, we design VeriGen, an end-to-end framework for generating verifiable RL tasks through iterative docker interactions and a multi-agent feedback loop. Scaled to 100+ concurrent agent workers via a shared docker interaction probe, this pipeline produces 24K+ verifiable tasks and nearly 3K high-quality RL tasks. To maximize sample efficiency, we propose Frontier Sampling, which tracks per-task capability and allocates rollouts to the current learning frontier. On the training side, we further design Visual Context Segmentation, a sliding window over recent visual context that balances rollout and training-engine pressure, yielding a 2.83x training speedup over step-wise decomposition. Together, ScaleCUA achieves 68.7% on OSWorld and 54.0% on ScienceBoard, establishing new state-of-the-art performance among open-source computer use agents. Code, models, and datasets are available at https://github.com/THUDM/SCALE-CUA.

</details>


### [278] [What We Talk About When We Talk About LLM Planning: Evidence for Two Distinct Planning Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11197)
*Sukai Huang,Chenyuan Zhang,Fucai Ke,Zhixi Cai,Naim Rastgoo,Gholamreza Haffari,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过多维项目反应理论模型分析发现，LLM的规划能力由两个潜在维度构成：操作推理能力和结构枚举能力，二者对模型规模和推理预算的响应模式截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM在规划任务上的性能差异归因于任务难度，但作者认为这种解释不完整。任务层面的性能变化可能反映了LLM具有多个独立的潜在规划能力维度，而非单一能力谱系上的差异。因此需要深入探究LLM规划能力的内在结构。

Method: 研究在ACPBench-Hard基准上评估多个LLM家族在不同测试时推理预算下的表现，并应用多维项目反应理论（multidimensional item response theory）模型，以揭示LLM规划能力的潜在结构。

Result: 分析揭示了塑造规划性能的两个主要维度：操作推理（评估局部动作适用性和即时状态转换的能力）和结构枚举（推理目标可达性和地标结构的能力）。操作推理能力随模型规模增大和推理链增长而提升，而结构枚举能力对此相对不敏感。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明应进行LLM规划的能力层面评估，关注重点应从“模型是否整体提升”转向“哪些规划能力提升、在何种条件下提升以及为何提升”。

Abstract: When LLMs exhibit uneven performance across planning tasks, these gaps are often attributed to task difficulty. We argue that this explanation is incomplete, as task-level variation may reflect distinct latent planning competencies rather than differences along a single ability spectrum. We study this question on ACPBench-Hard by evaluating multiple LLM families under varying test-time reasoning budgets and applying a multidimensional item response theory model to uncover the latent competency structure underlying LLM planning. The analysis reveals two principal dimensions that shape planning performance: operational reasoning, the ability to evaluate local action applicability and immediate state transitions, and structural enumeration, the ability to reason about goal reachability and landmark structure. Operational reasoning improving under model scaling and longer reasoning traces, while structural enumeration remains comparatively insensitive. Our findings motivate competency-level evaluation of LLM planning, shifting the focus from whether models improve overall to which planning competencies improve, under what conditions, and why.

</details>


### [279] [PREF-Gate: Provenance-Constrained Relational Evidence Fusion with Validation-Gated Selection for Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11212)
*Liming Liu,Chao Hu,Mingfei Lu,Yiwei Ge,Xingle Li,Heyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PREF-Gate框架，解决关系欺诈检测中图上下文与邻域证据使用的有效性问题，在三个数据集上取得竞争性性能，发现标签派生的关系证据仅在验证支持时才有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系欺诈检测可以利用无标签图上下文和标签派生的邻域证据，但这两种信息源遵循不同的有效性条件。特别是当查询节点的标签或任何验证/测试标签进入邻域风险构建时，邻域风险变得无效。这一来源约束问题需要被形式化和解决。

Method: 提出PREF-Gate可审计决策框架，包含两个固定专家和一个有限验证门控：(1)上下文专家使用属性、一跳均值、特征残差和度描述符，不使用标签；(2)证据专家添加自排除的仅训练标签邻域风险和经验贝叶斯摘要。门控在测试推理前选择专家或三种预指定概率混合之一，并固定决策阈值。

Result: 在Amazon、YelpChi和TFinance三个数据集上，使用五个相同分层分割和14个相同协议方法，PREF-Gate获得平均AUPRC值分别为0.9085、0.8104和0.8913。它在所有Amazon和YelpChi分割上选择无标签专家，在所有TFinance分割上选择证据混合。

Conclusion: 主要结果是条件性而非普遍性的：标签派生的关系证据仅在保留验证支持时才有用。该框架将竞争性排名性能与显式标签来源契约、有限选择策略、故障核算和审查预算评估相结合，为图欺诈检测提供了可审计的基于知识的决策管道。

Abstract: Relational fraud detection can exploit both label-free graph context and label-derived neighborhood evidence, but these two information sources obey different validity conditions. In particular, neighborhood risk becomes invalid when a queried node's own label, or any validation or test label, enters its construction. We formulate this issue as provenance-constrained relational evidence use and present PREF-Gate, an auditable decision framework with two fixed experts and a finite validation gate. The context expert uses attributes, one-hop means, feature residuals, and degree descriptors without labels. The evidence expert adds self-excluded, training-label-only neighborhood risk and empirical-Bayes summaries that expose support, uncertainty, availability, and shrinkage. Before test inference, the gate selects either expert or one of three pre-specified probability mixtures and fixes the decision threshold. On Amazon, YelpChi, and TFinance, using five identical stratified splits and 14 same-protocol methods, PREF-Gate obtains mean AUPRC values of 0.9085, 0.8104, and 0.8913. It selects the label-free expert on all Amazon and YelpChi splits and an evidence mixture on all TFinance splits. Thus, the main result is conditional rather than universal: label-derived relational evidence is useful only where held-out validation supports it. The framework couples competitive ranking performance with an explicit label-provenance contract, finite selection policy, failure accounting, and review-budget evaluation, providing an auditable knowledge-based decision pipeline for graph fraud detection.

</details>


### [280] [Heterogeneous Agent Cohorts for Safe Open-Ended Exploration with Runtime Constraint Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11226)
*Tengjiao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多角色LLM代理框架，通过角色分离（Disrupter、Validator、Broker）和失败约束编译机制（Scars），在保持安全性的同时提升创造力，显著降低了token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理面临两难困境：使用静态安全指令会限制创造力，而允许自由使用工具和多智能体辩论又容易导致安全违规。现有单一模型难以同时兼顾创造性和安全性。

Method: 提出角色分离的多智能体框架：Disrupter生成非常规提案，Validator在工具网关执行硬性运行时检查，Broker引入远距离类比。通过MCTS将失败编译为签名约束补丁（Scars），本地缓存并传递给后续代理。引入基于信用的通信分配评分（CAS）限制带宽使用。

Result: 在空间-语义沙盒实验中（N=20，p<0.01），该框架成功到达辩论方法无法达到的远程目标，Validator阻止了所有执行违规，Scars减少15.1%的token消耗，CAS在资源约束下降低55.9%的总体token成本。

Conclusion: 通过专业化角色分离和失败经验重用机制，可以在保证安全性的前提下提升LLM代理的创造力和效率，为构建更安全、更高效的AI系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM agents today are caught in an awkward bind. Lock them down with static safety instructions and they rarely venture beyond the obvious; give them free reign with tools and multi-agent debate, and safety violations quickly follow. Rather than forcing a single model to juggle both creativity and caution, we separate the concerns across specialized roles. A Disrupter generates unconventional proposals, a Validator enforces hard runtime checks at the tool gateway, and a Broker pulls in distant but relevant analogies. Failures are not discarded -- they are compiled, via MCTS, into compact, signed constraint patches we call Scars. These patches are cached locally and inherited by future cohorts, turning repeated failures into reusable, low-cost runtime constraints. In a spatial-semantic sandbox (N=20 runs, p<0.01), our cohort reaches remote targets where debate fails, the Validator prevents all executed breaches, and Scars reduce token consumption by 15.1% by avoiding redundant validator checks. Furthermore, credit-based Communication Allocation Scores (CAS) restrict outbound bandwidth, reducing overall token costs by 55.9% under resource constraints.

</details>


### [281] [Bringing Back Rule Induction to Fluid Intelligence Research? An Initial Validation of the ARC-AGI Benchmark in Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11263)
*Jasmin Thelen,Oliver Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次在人类样本中检验了ARC-AGI基准测试的心理测量学特性，结果显示其具有良好的心理测量学属性，与图形流体智力呈中等相关（ρ = .63），初步支持其作为人类流体智力测量工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体智力测量存在两种竞争性观点：工作记忆容量限制观和规则归纳能力限制观。现有测量工具主要反映前者，采用有限的重复规则，而规则归纳视角虽存在于定义中却鲜见于测量实践中。ARC-AGI基准测试主要需要规则归纳能力，被提议作为测量人类和人工系统流体智力的工具，但其心理测量学特性尚未在人类样本中得到验证。

Method: 研究招募100名参与者，首次检验ARC-AGI任务在人类样本中的心理测量学特性和命名网络。研究分析了ARC-AGI项目的心理测量学属性，并考察其与图形流体智力（通过图形推理测验测量）及图形原创性之间的相关关系。

Result: ARC-AGI项目汇编显示出良好的心理测量学属性，与图形流体智力呈实质性相关（ρ = .63），而与图形原创性的关联较弱。这些发现为ARC-AGI作为人类流体智力测量工具的有效性提供了初步支持。

Conclusion: 研究初步验证了ARC-AGI作为人类流体智力测量工具的有效性，支持将规则归纳任务纳入流体智力测量。研究建议系统地将AI基准测试嵌入人类认知能力的命名网络中，以促进更系统的评估和跨学科合作。这一研究的独特之处在于检验了一个最初为机器设计的任务在人类中的表现，为AI基准测试的跨学科应用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Two competing perspectives on fluid intelligence (gf) measures propose that performance is primarily constrained either by working memory capacity or by the ability to induce novel relations. The first perspective is currently dominant in measurement, as evident from the use of a limited set of recurring rules, whereas the second perspective is reflected in many definitions but rarely present in measurement. The ARC-AGI benchmark predominantly requires rule induction and was proposed as a measure of gf for both humans and artificial systems. However, its psychometric properties have not yet been examined in human samples. We therefore investigated the psychometric characteristics and nomological network of ARC-AGI in a first study with 100 participants. A compilation of ARC-AGI items showed good psychometric properties and correlated substantially with figural fluid intelligence as measured by a figural reasoning test (\r{ho} = .63). Associations with figural originality were weak. These findings provide initial support for the validity of ARC-AGI as a measure of human fluid intelligence. Future research should include more rule induction tasks as well as additional multivariate covariates. This study is unusual by studying a task in humans that was initially designed for machines. We suggest systematically embedding AI benchmarks into the nomological network of human cognitive abilities to enable more systematic evaluation and interdisciplinary cooperation.

</details>


### [282] [Valid $\ne$ Necessary: Diagnosing Latent Inefficiency in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11266)
*Daeyeop Lee,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现现有推理评估器无法识别有效但低效的推理步骤，提出了诊断基准RIV-GSM8K和无训练度量CAID，并基于此开发了PACE压缩策略，在保持准确率的同时将token消耗减少31-53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理步骤评估器存在关键盲点：虽然能有效检测逻辑谬误和事实错误，但无法惩罚有效却低效的推理步骤，这些步骤增加token使用量却对解决问题无贡献，导致CoT推理的计算成本过高。

Method: 1) 引入RIV-GSM8K诊断基准，注入五种不同类型的不效率问题；2) 提出CAID（上下文感知信息密度）度量，基于信息论识别低效用步骤，无需训练；3) 开发PACE后压缩策略验证该度量的实用性。

Result: 1) 诊断实验显示SOTA评估器难以区分不效率问题与必要推理；2) 在GSM8K、StrategyQA和ARC-Challenge上，PACE在保持准确率的同时将token消耗减少31-53%；3) 相比随机移除和PRM压缩基线，PACE在更高压缩率下保持准确率。

Conclusion: CAID成功从推理链中提炼出信息泡沫而不损害演绎有效性，有效解决了CoT推理中的过度推理问题，为LLM推理效率优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it often incurs substantial computational costs due to over-reasoning: the generation of redundant, verbose, or irrelevant steps. While existing reasoning step evaluators effectively detect logical fallacies and factual errors, our analysis reveals a critical blind spot: they fail to penalize valid but inefficient reasoning steps that inflate token usage without contributing to the solution. To systematically diagnose this limitation, we introduce RIV-GSM8K, a diagnostic benchmark injected with five distinct types of inefficiencies, including circular reasoning and excessive decomposition. Diagnostic experiments reveal that state-of-the-art evaluators struggle to distinguish these inefficiencies from necessary reasoning. To address this gap, we propose CAID (Context-Aware Information Density), a training-free metric grounded in information theory that identifies low-utility steps. To validate the metric's practical utility, we apply it within PACE, a post-hoc compression strategy. Additional control experiments show that the gains of PACE are not explained by trivial pruning: compared with random step removal and PRM-based compression baselines, it preserves accuracy at substantially higher compression rates. Empirical results on GSM8K, StrategyQA, and ARC-Challenge demonstrate that PACE reduces token consumption by 31-53% while maintaining accuracy, confirming that CAID successfully distills informational froth from reasoning chains without compromising deductive validity.

</details>


### [283] [Efficient Test-Time Optimization for Multi-Agent Proof Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11307)
*Tian-Shuo Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Zijie Geng,Haoyu Liu,Runjie Xu,Pengyuan Wang,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToMap多智能体框架用于证明自动形式化，通过识别并集中优化关键瓶颈（分解器），在ProofFlowBench上以更低的测试时成本实现19.0%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明自动形式化是一个长期挑战，需要协调多个证明步骤的声明、上下文和依赖关系，而现有方法要么依赖昂贵的模型训练，要么在推理时应用过度的无引导修复。

Method: 提出ToMap多智能体框架，采用分解器-形式化器-证明器流水线结构；通过瓶颈分析识别分解器为关键瓶颈，将测试时计算高效集中于分解器优化；采用受GEPA启发的优化循环，利用形式化验证进度和语义证明标准定义帕累托前沿来指导分解更新。

Result: 在ProofFlowBench基准上，以语法正确性和语义忠实度评估，相比最佳先前方法提升19.0%，同时测试时成本更低；扩展性分析表明大多数收益在少数分解演化迭代内出现。

Conclusion: ToMap通过智能分配测试时计算资源、识别并优化关键瓶颈，有效解决了证明自动形式化的挑战，证明了瓶颈导向优化策略的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Full-proof autoformalization bridges extensive mathematical proofs in natural language with formally validated reasoning, offering a pathway to elevate the ceiling of verifiable mathematical reasoning. Unlike statement-level formalization, proof autoformalization is a long-horizon challenge requiring coordination of claims, contexts, and dependencies across many proof steps, yet has only recently come under focused study. Current approaches either rely on costly model training or apply excessive, unguided repair at inference time. To this end, we introduce ToMap, a multi-agent framework that structures proof autoformalization as a Decomposer-Formalizer-Prover pipeline with efficient test-time optimization guided by formal verification and semantic rubrics for proof quality. Rather than distributing test-time compute across all agents, we perform bottleneck analysis and identify the Decomposer as the critical bottleneck: the quality of its atomic, self-contained proof units directly determines whether downstream agents can successfully formalize and prove each step. ToMap therefore treats the Formalizer and Prover as downstream executors and efficiently focuses test-time compute on Decomposer refinement. This refinement follows a loop inspired by GEPA, evolving prompts over candidate decompositions and using formal verification progress together with semantic proof rubrics to define a Pareto frontier that guides the next decomposition update. Experiments on ProofFlowBench show that ToMap improves over the best previous method by 19.0% when evaluated by both syntactic correctness and semantic faithfulness, while requiring lower test-time cost. Scaling analysis shows that most gains emerge within a few iterations of decomposition evolution, guiding test-time budget selection.

</details>


### [284] [Calibrated e-CUSUM Decoding for Quantized Reasoning Models: Why Token Log-Probability Is the Wrong Observable for Decoding Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11317)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对低比特量化导致的小型推理模型思维链退化问题，提出了一个新的解码监控方法，取代了传统的不适用的中心化token对数概率指标，在GSM8K数据集上实现了对失败推理链的选择性检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化虽然降低了小型推理模型的部署成本，但会损害其思维链质量，需要解码端监控器在生成不可靠时进行干预。然而，现有的自然候选指标——中心化token对数概率增量——在模型自身采样律下构造为均零鞅，仅测量采样自一致性而非轨迹健康度，且在置信重复时几乎静默。

Method: 提出了一个免训练的解码控制器，结合两个组件：(i) 融合token不确定性与显式逐字重复的退化感知报警分数；(ii) 基于校准e过程的序列检测器。该方法在条件均值零假设下满足Ville有效性，采用CUSUM下限统计量作为实证变化检测器。

Result: 在GSM8K数据集上使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B模型（FP16和INT4），校准后的监控器将原本对93-95%生成触发的监控转变为对失败轨迹的选择性检测器（φ≈0.3，精度≈0.6，基线率为0.38）。控制器减少了逐字退化信号，INT4准确率从63%提升至69%（配对McNemar检验p=0.18，n=100），但统计上不显著，token预算成本为28%。

Conclusion: 研究的主要贡献是方法论层面的：解释了中心化token对数概率在解码监控中的不足之处，并提供了一个校准的、谨慎评估的替代方案。同时发现非终止而非循环是GSM8K上的主要失败模式。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization makes small reasoning models inexpensive to deploy but can degrade their chains of thought. This motivates decoder-side monitors that intervene when generation becomes unreliable. We show that a natural candidate, the centered token log-probability increment $\log p(w_t)+H_t$, is the wrong observable for this purpose. Under the model's own sampling law it is a mean-zero martingale by construction, so it measures sampling self-consistency rather than trajectory health and is nearly silent during confident repetition, where both $\log p(w_t)$ and entropy are close to zero. We introduce a training-free decoding controller that combines (i) a degeneration-aware alarm score fusing token uncertainty with explicit verbatim repetition and (ii) a calibrated e-process-inspired sequential detector. The raw product process is Ville-valid under a conditional-mean null, while the deployed CUSUM-floored statistic is treated as an empirical change detector because the score is history-dependent and autocorrelated. On GSM8K with DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B in FP16 and INT4, calibration turns a monitor that fires on 93--95% of generations into a selective detector of failing traces ($φ\approx 0.3$, precision $\approx 0.6$ against a 0.38 base rate). In this pilot, the controller reduces measured verbatim-degeneration signals and yields a positive but statistically inconclusive INT4 accuracy change from 63% to 69% (paired McNemar $p=0.18$, $n=100$), at a 28% token-budget cost. We also find that non-termination, rather than looping, is the dominant failure mode on GSM8K. The main contribution is methodological: an explanation of why centered token log-probability is inadequate for decoder monitoring and a calibrated, cautiously evaluated replacement.

</details>


### [285] [Verifier-Guided Twelve-Tone Composition: A Generate-Verify-Repair Harness for Symbolic Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11334)
*Congren Dai,Danni Zhao,Enyang Liu,Michael Ching Yam,Zhancheng Guo,Siyi Gu,Wentao Yang,Bo Dai,Xiaobing Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个神经符号框架，通过将语言模型 proposer 与符号验证相结合的生成-验证-修复-追踪循环，显著提高了十二音序列生成的合法性和一致性，将成功交付率从13.3%提升至48.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生成十二音序列时，往往产生表面合法但实际上会退化为退化纹理的序列，缺乏事件局部一致性，且存在碰撞和序列化一致性问题。

Method: 提出了一个神经符号框架（neuro-symbolic harness），将语言模型 proposer 封装在生成-验证-修复-追踪循环中，配合符号验证机制。该框架在不声称整体合法性的前提下，改进事件局部一致性，并能在无法满足条件时显式弃权。

Result: 在40个受控任务和四对模型的测试中，审计交付成功率从原始生成的13.3%提升至框架辅助的48.1%。狭窄的碰撞和序列化一致性检查通过率从33.5%提升至58.3%，退化率维持在约0.05，包括对抗性提示下。五位专家的盲评也显示框架生成的候选序列在遵循性、感知合法性、连贯性和整体质量方面均优于原始生成。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架能有效提升大型语言模型在十二音序列生成任务中的表现，显著改善合法性、一致性和质量，同时保持较低的退化率，证明了神经符号结合方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Large language models can produce superficially legal twelve-tone scores that collapse into degenerate textures. We introduce a neuro-symbolic harness that wraps a language-model proposer in a generate-verify-repair-trace loop with symbolic verification. The complete pipeline improves event-local consistency without claiming whole-piece legality. Across 40 controlled tasks and four paired models, audited delivery yield rises from 13.3% under raw generation to 48.1% with the harness, which explicitly abstains otherwise. The pass rate of a narrower collision and serialisation-consistency check rises from 33.5% to 58.3%, while degeneracy remains near 0.05, including under exploratory adversarial prompting. A blinded evaluation by five experts also shows a descriptive aggregate preference for harness candidates over raw generation in adherence, perceived legality, coherence, and overall quality.

</details>


### [286] [AutoVSR: Automatic Visual-to-Symbolic Reasoning for Symbolic Expression Generation from Circuit Schematic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11338)
*Zhe Xiao,Longfei Li,Xu He,Haoying Wu,Zixing Zhang,Mingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoVSR框架，通过视觉语言模型实现从电路图像到符号表达式的自动生成，在准确性和效率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号表达式能有效表征和预测电路行为，但从电路原理图直接推导符号表达式面临两大挑战：1) 从图像进行精确的视觉到符号的电路结构构建；2) 正确的多步骤符号推导。这两个过程都要求严格的正确性，传统方法难以同时满足。

Method: 提出AutoVSR框架，包含两个核心创新：1) IR构建方法：通过元件规则检索和基于验证的反馈来指导中间表示构建；2) 符号求解器：实现为配备符号工具库的规划智能体，用于可靠的多步推导。框架首先将电路图重构为可执行的中间表示，然后利用符号求解器进行推理。

Result: 在符号表达式生成任务上，AutoVSR相比端到端VLM方法准确率提升30.01-59.45%，相比专业方法提升41.96-51.84%。在推理成本和计算效率方面超越了闭源的最先进VLMs。

Conclusion: AutoVSR通过引入可执行中间表示和基于规划智能体的符号求解器，成功解决了视觉到符号生成的正确性难题，显著提升了电路符号表达式生成的准确性和效率，为电路分析和建模提供了有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Symbolic expressions can effectively characterize and predict circuit behavior, but deriving them directly from circuit schematics is challenging. This process requires accurate visual-to-symbolic construction of circuit structure from images and correct multi-step symbolic derivation, both of which impose strict correctness requirements. This work proposes AutoVSR, an automated framework for visual-to-symbolic generation of circuit expressions using Vision Language Models (VLMs). By reconstructing circuit diagrams into an executable intermediate representation (Executable IR) and leveraging a symbolic solver for reasoning, AutoVSR significantly improves the accuracy of symbolic expression generation. AutoVSR introduces two key innovations: an IR construction method guided by component rule retrieval and verification-based feedback, and a symbolic solver implemented as a planning agent equipped with a symbolic tool library for reliable multi-step derivation. Compared with end-to-end VLM approaches and specialized methods on the main symbolic expression generation task, AutoVSR achieves accuracy improvements of 30.01--59.45% and 41.96--51.84%, respectively. Moreover, AutoVSR surpasses closed-source state-of-the-art VLMs in inference cost and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/LongfeiLi1/AutoVSR.

</details>


### [287] [Compile, Then Page: Executable SOP Programs and a Capability-Gated Runtime for Procedural LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11346)
*Chenglin Yu,Li Yin,Ying Yu,Hongxia Yang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将企业标准操作流程(SOP)编译为可执行伪代码，通过程序引导的栈机器运行，大模型进行语义执行。研究表明：编译文本不会降低性能且有提升，运行时引导效果取决于模型能力，强模型显著受益而弱模型受损，提出了分步实施的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体需要遵循长期、条件性、安全关键的标准操作流程(SOPs)。现有方法在处理复杂SOP时可能存在性能不足或合规性问题，需要一种既能提升执行准确性又能保证安全性的方法。

Method: 1. 将机器可读的SOP约束编译成可执行伪代码；2. 设计程序引导(PG)栈机器，在LLM执行语义操作时分页活动帧；3. 进行三臂SOPBench研究，对比编译文本、运行时引导等不同方法；4. 设计消融实验验证各组件贡献。

Result: 1. 编译文本从不显著损害性能，在官方文本表现不佳时可提升高达16.0分；2. 运行时引导呈现能力门槛效应：两个强模型在七个领域显示正向对比(58:19和75:31 discordant pairs)，弱模型反而受损；3. 全程序光标消融恢复强模型的大部分拒绝增益；4. Bank数据集上三臂从70.4提升至86.4再到92.8，拒绝正确率达100%；5. 探针和审计测量追踪到差异源于自发状态纪律而非重建能力。

Conclusion: 提出了实用的分步指导：优先采用编译方法；仅在通过模型级纪律检查后才启用活动帧分页。编译方法普适安全，而运行时引导需要根据模型能力选择性启用，为SOP合规执行提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Enterprise agents must follow long-horizon, conditional, safety-critical standard operating procedures (SOPs). We compile machine-readable SOP constraints into executable pseudo-code and run them with a program-guided (PG) stack machine that pages the active frame while an LLM performs semantic execution. A three-arm SOPBench study across six models separates representation from runtime: compiled text never significantly hurts and gains up to 16.0 points where official prose underperforms. Runtime guidance is capability-gated. Two strong models independently show positive seven-domain PG contrasts (58:19 and 75:31 discordant pairs), whereas weak models are harmed. A full-program cursor ablation (active frame first, complete program retained) recovers much of the strong-model refusal gain; selective visibility adds a smaller improvement. Paired probe and audit measurements track this divide to spontaneous state discipline rather than reconstruction ability. On Bank the three primary arms rise from 70.4 to 86.4 to 92.8, with 100% refusal correctness. Practical guidance: compile first; enable active-frame paging only after a model-level discipline check.

</details>


### [288] [From Neural Network Decisions to Training Cases: An Exact Account via Case-Based Decision Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11347)
*Manli Yan,Yuebin Lin,Yaowen Yu,Yong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于案例的神经网络决策审计方法，通过OLS线性探测实现训练样本对决策的精确贡献分解，无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域（医疗诊断、信贷审批、能源竞价）的神经网络决策需要样本级别的可解释性证据：哪些训练样本支持特定决策及其对应的结果。现有方法缺乏将决策追溯到训练样本的精确机制。

Method: 在固定神经网络表示上拟合OLS线性探测（linear probe），证明其具有精确的基于案例的分解形式：每个动作分数是训练案例回报的加权和，系数由经验Gram几何决定。识别CBDT相似性语义的充分条件域，域外情况将系数视为有符号Gram几何影响力。

Result: 在合成CBDT、PJM、Adult Income和Default Credit任务上，方法成功恢复案例级偏好结构，在对比的归因基线中达到最高平均Top-30一致性，同时在支持重构方面保持竞争力。仅需拟合OLS顶层探测，无需重训练表示或访问原始优化轨迹。

Conclusion: 该方法为高风险领域的神经网络决策提供了可操作的审计工具，能够追踪分数到训练案例、测量动作一致性、识别弱支持信号，同时保持计算简单性和理论严谨性。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly guide decisions in high-stakes domains such as medical diagnosis, credit approval, and energy bidding. Audit in these settings requires case-level evidence: which training cases support an action and what outcomes they carried. Case-based decision theory (CBDT) formalizes this reasoning by aggregating outcome support from remembered cases. We show that an OLS action readout fitted on a fixed neural representation admits an exact case-based decomposition. Each action score is a weighted sum of training-case returns, with coefficients determined by empirical Gram geometry. We identify a sufficient regime for CBDT similarity semantics; outside it, the coefficients should generally be treated as signed Gram-geometric influence. The decomposition yields audit signals that trace scores to training cases, measure action coherence, and identify weak support. Across synthetic CBDT, PJM, Adult Income, and Default Credit tasks, the method recovers case-level preference structure and achieves the highest mean Top-30 consistency among compared attribution baselines, while remaining competitive on support reconstruction. The audit requires only fitting an OLS top-layer probe, without retraining the representation or accessing the original optimization trajectory; probe fidelity is measured by score reconstruction.

</details>


### [289] [OpsMem: Dual-Memory Reasoning with Cross-Memory Resonance for Failure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11357)
*Yongqian Sun,Rongchen Gao,Yu Luo,Wenwei Gu,Shenglin Zhang,Qingyi Guo,Qiuai Fu,Yaoliang Wu,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpsMem，一个用于现代软件系统故障诊断的双记忆框架，通过协调短期诊断状态和长期运维经验，在华为微服务故障诊断数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统的故障诊断需要迭代式的证据获取和假设推理，现有的LLM方法（包括智能体推理和知识增强）缺乏一种机制来在迭代诊断过程中协调不断演化的诊断状态与运维经验，导致诊断效果受限。

Method: 提出OpsMem双记忆框架：（1）维护短期记忆用于存储当前诊断状态；（2）维护长期记忆用于存储可复用的运维经验；（3）通过跨记忆共鸣机制激活与状态相关的长期记忆；（4）基于短期记忆和激活的长期记忆进行多智能体诊断；（5）将已解决事件中的可复用经验固化回长期记忆。

Result: 在华为微服务故障诊断真实数据集上的实验表明，OpsMem在Match和Relevant指标上分别比最强基线提升高达46.88%和18.39%，显著优于代表性的智能体推理和知识增强基线方法。

Conclusion: OpsMem通过双记忆架构和跨记忆协调机制，有效解决了迭代诊断过程中诊断状态与运维经验的协同问题，为现代软件系统的故障诊断提供了一种更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Failure diagnosis in modern software systems requires iterative evidence acquisition and hypothesis reasoning guided by operational experience. Existing LLM-based methods improve diagnosis through agentic reasoning or knowledge augmentation, but they often lack a mechanism to coordinate the evolving diagnostic state with operational experience during iterative diagnosis. We propose OpsMem, a dual-memory framework that maintains a short-term memory for the current diagnostic state and a long-term memory for reusable operational experience. OpsMem uses cross-memory resonance to activate state-relevant long-term memory, conditions multi-agent diagnosis on the short-term and activated long-term memories, and consolidates reusable experience from solved incidents back into long-term memory. Experiments on a real-world Huawei microservice failure diagnosis dataset show that OpsMem outperforms representative agentic-reasoning and knowledge-augmented baselines, improving Match and Relevant by up to 46.88% and 18.39% over the strongest baseline, respectively.

</details>


### [290] [Omni-Decision: A Progressive Evidence-State Agent System for Omni-Modal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11433)
*Ming Ma,Yi Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Feida Zhu,Weigao Sun,Junhan Shi,Lingrui Mei,Tianming Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Omni-Decision，一种无需训练的证据状态系统，通过维护结构化的证据状态（包含已确认证据、未解决冲突、依赖关系和未满足需求）来协调全模态证据收集过程，在OmniGAIA和WorldSense基准上分别比基线提升27.3和30.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体多模态系统在处理跨视频、音频、图像、网页和计算结果的全模态证据寻求问答时，常将证据留在临时存储、工具轨迹或自由形式历史中，导致难以追踪已确认证据、缺失内容以及何时收集到足够证据来回答问题。

Method: 提出Omni-Decision，一种无需训练的证据状态系统，将全模态问答转化为查询范围的证据闭包过程。系统为每个查询维护包含已确认证据、未解决冲突、事实与计算依赖以及待满足证据需求的结构化证据状态。共享状态视图条件化规划、证据获取、验证、修复和最终化步骤。来自媒体、网络、计算和验证模块的异构观测被规范化、判断并通过确定性状态更新提交。

Result: Omni-Decision在OmniGAIA上达到45.6%准确率，在WorldSense上达到58.3%准确率，分别比基线提升+27.3和+30.2个百分点。无状态消融实验和轨迹审计进一步验证了显式证据状态控制在多步全模态证据寻求中的作用。

Conclusion: 通过显式维护结构化证据状态并采用确定性状态更新机制，Omni-Decision实现了目标明确的证据获取、保留了稀疏跨模态线索，并提供了对修复和停止的可检查控制，有效解决了全模态证据寻求问答中的证据追踪和决策问题。

Abstract: Omni-modal evidence-seeking QA requires agents to answer questions whose evidence is sparsely distributed across videos, audio, images, web pages, and computation results. Existing agentic multimodal systems often leave evidence in scratchpads, tool trajectories, or free-form histories, making it difficult to track what has been grounded, what remains missing, and when the evidence is sufficient to answer. We propose Omni-Decision, a training-free evidence-state system that turns omni-modal QA into a query-scoped evidence-closure process. For each query, Omni-Decision maintains a structured evidence state containing confirmed evidence, unresolved conflicts, fact and computation dependencies, and open evidence needs. A shared state view conditions planning, evidence acquisition, validation, repair, and finalization. Heterogeneous observations from media, web, computation, and verification modules are normalized, judged, and committed through deterministic state updates. This design enables targeted evidence acquisition, preserves sparse cross-modal cues, and provides inspectable control over repair and stopping. Omni-Decision achieves 45.6% accuracy on OmniGAIA and 58.3% on WorldSense, improving over the baselines by +27.3 and +30.2 percentage points, respectively. No-state ablations and trajectory audits further support the role of explicit evidence-state control in multi-step omni-modal evidence seeking.

</details>


### [291] [The Ebb and Flow of Multimodal Focus: Scheduling Visual Relay Windows for Grounded VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11436)
*Wencheng Ye,Yi Bin,Yujuan Ding,Hongye Fang,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Yun Zhang,Sirui Da,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）内部存在稳定的三阶段多模态注意力重分布模式，并提出TRACE框架来动态控制视觉信息传递，显著提升了基于证据的多模态推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多模态推理基准测试上表现良好，但其视觉证据在进入语言处理模块后往往变得不稳定，削弱了基于证据的推理能力。为了理解这种脆弱性的根源，作者从机制视角深入研究了VLM内部的动态过程。

Method: 作者首先通过机制分析发现了VLM中稳定的三阶段注意力重分布模式：早期问题条件化组织、中期关键的视觉主导中继、晚期答案生成。作者将中期阶段定义为视觉中继窗口（VRW），并基于此提出TRACE框架——一种任务自适应的推理时控制方法。TRACE通过轻量级训练模块，在预填充阶段重塑中继分配，并在解码阶段保持已组装的视觉支持信息。

Result: 在四个开源VLM骨干网络和七个基准测试上，TRACE在需要视觉定位的任务上平均提升4.33分，最高提升6.6分，同时在推理密集型任务上也有改进。实验表明VRW的几何特征随任务需求变化、与基于证据的生成存在因果关联，并能区分无依据答案与强推理路径。

Conclusion: 显式控制深度方向上的多模态焦点分布，为增强基于证据的多模态推理提供了一个统一且有效的机制。这一发现不仅揭示了VLM内部的信息处理规律，也为改进模型的证据推理能力提供了新方向。

Abstract: Vision-language models increasingly succeed on multimodal reasoning benchmarks, yet their visual evidence often becomes unstable once it enters the language stack, weakening evidence-grounded reasoning. To understand this fragility, we examine the internal dynamics of VLMs through a mechanistic lens and uncover a stable three-stage redistribution of multimodal attention focus across depth: an early question-conditioned organization, a critical middle visual-dominant relay, and a late return to answer formation. We operationalize the middle phase as the Visual Relay Window (VRW), and show that its geometry varies with task demand, is causally tied to grounded generation, and distinguishes unsupported answers from stronger reasoning trajectories. Guided by this internal rhythm, we propose TRACE, a task-adaptive inference-time control framework with lightweight trained modules. It reshapes relay allocation during prefill and preserves assembled visual support after handoff during decoding. Across four open-weight VLM backbones and seven benchmarks, TRACE delivers large gains on grounding-sensitive settings, improving them by 4.33 points on average and by up to 6.6 points, while also improving reasoning-heavy tasks. These results show that explicitly controlling multimodal focus across depth offers a unified and effective mechanism for strengthening evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [292] [Enhancing Query Efficiency for d-DNNF Representations Through Preprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11492)
*Jean Marie Lagniez,Emmanuel Lonca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了CNF公式模型访问的预处理技术，发现保持模型计数的预处理方法能有效提升均匀采样、直接模型访问和模型枚举的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预处理技术在处理CNF公式的模型访问任务（均匀采样、直接模型访问和模型枚举）时存在局限性，大多数不保持公式等价性的预处理方法不适用于这些任务，需要研究合适的预处理策略。

Method: 分析现有预处理技术的适用性，提出利用保持模型计数的预处理方法，并保留相关预处理信息，将CNF公式编译为d-DNNF表示进行实验验证。

Result: 在多个领域的基准测试集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明所提出的预处理方法高效且鲁棒，在模型访问查询方面获得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 保持模型计数的预处理技术可以有效应用于CNF公式的模型访问任务，为提升命题公式模型访问效率提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate preprocessing techniques aimed at improving the efficiency of accessing models of propositional formulas represented in conjunctive normal form (CNF). We focus on three fundamental tasks: uniform sampling, direct model access, and model enumeration. Our analysis reveals that most state-of-the-art preprocessors, when they do not preserve formula equivalence, are generally unsuitable for these tasks. In contrast, we demonstrate that preprocessors which preserve model counts can be effectively leveraged, provided relevant preprocessing information is maintained. To validate our approach, we perform extensive experiments on a diverse suite of benchmarks from multiple domains. The experimental results show that our preprocessing methods are both efficient and robust, yielding significant performance improvements for model access queries when CNF formulas are compiled into d-DNNF representations.

</details>


### [293] [Comparative Analysis of GAT and BERT for Human-Like Playtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11501)
*Kleio Fragkedaki,Theodoros Panagiotakopoulos,Matteo Biasielli,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Transformer（BERT）和图注意力网络（GAT）的通用化表示方法，用于预测益智游戏玩家行为，减少特征工程需求，在Candy Crush Saga游戏中表现优于CNN基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的玩家行为预测方法存在两个主要局限：（1）无法捕捉完整的玩家策略范围；（2）需要大量的特征工程和网络架构建模工作。当引入新游戏机制或功能时，模型需要持续调整，维护成本高昂。

Method: 提出两种通用网络架构来捕捉Candy Crush Saga（CCS）游戏棋盘的关系结构：（1）基于Transformer的BERT模型；（2）图注意力网络（GAT）模型。这些方法旨在构建更通用的表示，减少甚至消除持续特征工程维护的需求。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的BERT和GAT模型在具有挑战性的棋盘配置上相比卷积神经网络（CNN）基准模型展现出更优的性能，验证了通用化表示方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通用化表示方法能够有效捕捉游戏棋盘的关系结构特征，为设计引人入胜的益智游戏提供了更强大的玩家体验建模工具，同时降低了模型维护成本。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and understanding player experience is crucial for designing engaging puzzle games. To achieve this, a common approach involves collecting diverse user data to train predictive playtesting models that mimic player behavior. However, existing data-driven methods often lack the ability to capture the full range of player strategies and require extensive feature engineering and network architecture modeling. This limitation becomes particularly evident when new game mechanics or features are introduced, which necessitate continual adjustments to the models. To addrss these challenges, we propose a more generalized representation that reduces - or even eliminates - the need for ongoing feature-engineering maintenance. Specifically, we investigate two general-purpose network architectures: (a) a transformer-based model (BERT) and (b) a graph attention model (GAT), both of which are designed to effectively capture the relational structure of Candy Crush Saga (CCS) game boards. Our experiments compare these approaches to Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) baselines, revealing better performance on challenging board configurations and underscoring the benefits of our generalizable representation.

</details>


### [294] [Learning Residual Kinematic Corrections for Continuous Neural Decoding via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11530)
*Jiamian Li,Niall McShane,Attila Korik,Naomi du Bois,Karl McCreadie,Leen Jabban,Benjamin Metcalfe,Özgür Şimşek,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CNN-LSTM-RL两阶段解码框架，利用强化学习对CNN-LSTM的预测轨迹进行残差校正，显著提升了基于EEG的3D运动想象解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于非侵入式EEG的脑机接口在连续3D运动想象解码中面临信号变异性和残差解码误差的挑战，现有CNN-LSTM深度学习架构虽能捕获时空动态特性，但预测轨迹中仍存在系统性残差误差，限制了解码精度的进一步提升。

Method: 提出两阶段解码框架：第一阶段使用CNN-LSTM解码器从EEG信号中预测运动轨迹；第二阶段引入强化学习智能体对预测轨迹进行残差运动学校正。RL智能体在离线状态下训练，不直接接收EEG输入，而是基于预测的运动轨迹进行优化，以最大化相对于目标轨迹的运动准确性。

Result: 在2D环境下，平均相关系数从0.5076提升至0.7181（相对增益41.5%，p=0.0005），RMSE从0.0890降至0.0532（相对降低40.2%，p<0.0001）；在VR环境下，相关系数从0.6420提升至0.7780（相对增益21.2%，p=0.0059），RMSE从0.0714降至0.0441（相对降低38.2%，p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 该可扩展框架通过离线残差强化学习校正运动学误差，无需额外神经数据即可显著增强3D BCI运动想象解码性能，为神经康复、假肢控制和虚拟交互等应用提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: Decoding continuous three-dimensional (3D) motor imagery (MI) using non-invasive electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) remains challenging due to signal variability and residual decoding errors. Deep learning architectures such as convolutional neural network--long short-term memory (CNN--LSTM) models can capture spatial and temporal dynamics for continuous kinematic decoding; however, systematic residual errors persist in predicted trajectories. We propose a two-stage decoding framework that applies reinforcement learning (RL) to perform residual kinematic correction on the outputs of a CNN--LSTM decoder (CNN--LSTM--RL). The RL agent is trained offline without direct EEG input and instead operates on predicted kinematic trajectories to optimize movement accuracy relative to target trajectories. Decoding performance was quantified using Pearson correlation coefficients ($r$) and Root Mean Square Errors (RMSE) along the $x, y$, and $z$ axes. Compared to CNN--LSTM applied alone, CNN--LSTM--RL improved the mean correlation from $0.5076$ to $0.7181$ ($p = 0.0005$) in 2D and from $0.6420$ to $0.7780$ ($p = 0.0059$) in VR, with relative gains of $41.5\%$ and $21.2\%$, respectively. Correspondingly, RMSE was reduced from $0.0890$ to $0.0532$ (2D, $p < 0.0001$) and from $0.0714$ to $0.0441$ (VR, $p < 0.0001$), representing relative reductions of $40.2\%$ and $38.2\%$. These findings demonstrate that this scalable framework enhances 3D BCI MI decoding by correcting kinematic errors via offline residual RL without extra neural data, advancing neurorehabilitation, prosthetics, and virtual interaction.

</details>


### [295] [HCRMap: Pressure-Aware Hot-Expert Residency Mapping for 3.5D MoE Chiplet Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11586)
*Yongqin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HCRMap框架，通过动态管理热点专家副本的放置和重用来解决3.5D MoE推理中的专家热度倾斜问题，显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE LLM在推理时存在持续的热点专家倾斜问题，少数热点专家接收大量token，导致3.5D多芯片系统中的计算不均衡，并加剧通信、内存带宽、I/O和执行队列的压力。核心问题在于如何动态放置和重用热点专家副本，而非仅仅减少token移动。

Method: 提出HCRMap框架，基于专家热度、权重加载成本、迁移开销和运行时资源压力，动态决定专家副本的晋升、保留、降级或驱逐，并将路由token组映射到合适的驻留副本，从而联合缓解通信、内存和队列瓶颈。

Result: 实验显示HCRMap在prefill和decode阶段分别比Hydra降低43.6%和43.0%端到端延迟，比MoEntwine降低34.5%和33.1%，比PIMoE降低46.7%和46.0%。

Conclusion: HCRMap有效解决了3.5D MoE推理中的专家热点倾斜问题，通过压力感知的副本管理显著提升了推理性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLM) activate only a small number of experts during inference, but token routing introduces persistent expert hotness skew: a small set of hot experts continuously receives most tokens, while the remaining experts are lightly loaded. On 3.5D multi-chiplet systems, this skew not only causes compute imbalance but also amplifies pressure on communication, memory bandwidth, I/O, and execution queues. Therefore, the core problem is not simply to reduce token movement, but to dynamically place and reuse hot expert replicas across different memory tiers.
  This paper proposes HCRMap, a hot expert residency mapping framework for pressure-aware expert replica management in 3.5D MoE inference. Based on expert hotness, weight loading cost, migration overhead, and runtime resource pressure, HCRMap dynamically determines which experts should be promoted, retained, demoted, or evicted. It then maps routed token groups to suitable resident replicas, thereby jointly mitigating communication, memory, and queue bottlenecks.
  Experimental results show that HCRMap reduces end-to-end latency by 43.6% and 43.0% over Hydra in the prefill and decode stages, respectively; by 34.5% and 33.1% over MoEntwine; and by 46.7% and 46.0% over PIMoE.

</details>


### [296] [MAGIC: Transition-Aware Generation of Navigable Multi-Scene Game Worlds with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11594)
*Tsz Hei Fan,Choi Wing Fung,Yuxuan Wan,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGIC系统，能够从单一自然语言提示生成可运行的多场景游戏项目，解决了跨场景一致性、场景内可导航性和过渡评估三个关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代3D游戏中的多场景导航（在有限空间内完成任务后通过传送门进入下一个场景）是一个关键特性，但创作过程非常繁琐：每个传送门必须在两侧保持一致的端点，每个内部空间在布置家具后必须保持可导航，并且生成的连通性必须在多个文件中保持一致。现有的LLM和MLLM场景生成器虽然使单一室内场景合成成本大幅降低，但它们一次只能生成一个场景，无法通过简单重复产生连通的多场景世界。

Method: MAGIC是一个四阶段流水线系统：(1)规划共享的过渡感知中间表示；(2)在用洪水填充验证器强制执行传送门可达性的同时指定每个场景；(3)生成场景及其过渡脚本；(4)将它们组合成一个项目。系统还引入了一个过渡专注的评估代理，在游戏中运行每个过渡进行评估。

Result: 在100个多场景案例的新基准测试中，MAGIC为每个案例都生成了可执行项目，在端到端过渡识别上达到0.99精确率、0.95召回率和0.96 F1分数。逐阶段对比中，它比LLM基线和Holodeck恢复了更多真实传送门，并产生了明显更多可导航的布局。

Conclusion: MAGIC成功解决了单场景方法遗留的三个障碍：跨场景一致性、场景内可导航性和过渡评估，能够将单一自然语言提示转换为完整可运行的多场景游戏项目，在性能上显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Multi-scene navigation (clearing an objective in one bounded space and then crossing a portal into the next) is a defining feature of contemporary 3D games, but authoring it is laborious: every portal must have consistent endpoints on both sides, each interior must remain navigable once it is furnished, and the resulting connectivity must be kept consistent across many files. Recent large language model (LLM) and multimodal LLM (MLLM) scene generators have made single-interior synthesis dramatically cheaper, yet they produce one scene at a time and cannot, by naive repetition, yield a connected multi-scene world. We identify three obstacles that single-scene methods leave unsolved: cross-scene consistency, in-scene navigability, and the evaluation of whether a transition actually works. We present MAGIC, a prompt-to-project system that addresses all three. MAGIC is a four-stage pipeline that turns a single natural-language prompt into a runnable multi-scene game project: it plans a shared transition-aware intermediate representation, specifies each scene while enforcing portal reachability with a flood-fill validator, generates the scenes together with their transition scripts, and combines them into one project. Because existing single-scene fidelity metrics never execute a transition, we further introduce a transition-focused evaluation agent that runs each transition in play. On a new benchmark of 100 multi-scene cases, MAGIC produces an executable project for every case and reaches 0.99 precision, 0.95 recall, and 0.96 F1 on end-to-end transition identification; stage by stage, it recovers more ground-truth portals and yields markedly more navigable layouts than an LLM baseline and Holodeck. Our code is available at https://github.com/sereneee1201/MAGIC/.

</details>


### [297] [Interaction Scaling: Grounding the Third Axis of Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11598)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出第三种推理时扩展方式——交互式推理，通过外部工具的真实观测反馈打破传统推理和采样的性能天花板。在编程任务中达到100%通过率，在视觉任务中消除40-74%的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的两种推理时扩展方法（延长推理过程和多次采样选择最优）都存在固有局限性：它们都是内部过程，无法引入模型已知之外的新信息。每个额外token都来自相同的冻结权重和相同提示，无法告诉模型其未知的信息。因此作者探索第三种扩展维度——交互式推理，通过外部工具的真实观测反馈来突破这一限制。

Method: 提出交互式推理框架：模型提出artifact，外部工具观测其真实行为，模型据此修正。核心概念是"grounding"（接地性），要求反馈循环两端都必须接地：驱动修正的反馈必须来自实际观测缺陷的工具，评分结果的指标也必须基于真实观测。设计了proposer-reviewer harness架构进行实验验证。

Result: 编程任务上：推理方法和best-of-N采样都出现性能平台期（即使有oracle选择最优样本），而交互策略持续改进，proposer-reviewer harness达到100%通过率且无运行间差异，跨三个模型家族均有效。视觉任务上：传统VLM评委将14/15个明显有缺陷的图形评为"完美"（因为截图隐藏了缺陷），而测量真实布局的工具使循环消除40-74%的缺陷；同一VLM作为reviewer时反而降低布局质量。

Conclusion: 交互式扩展是真实存在且区别于推理和采样的第三种扩展维度，但只有当反馈和指标都接地时才能显现。传统VLM评委在视觉任务中存在严重盲区，而基于真实观测的接地工具能有效发现和修复缺陷。这为推理时扩展开辟了新方向。

Abstract: There are two standard ways to spend more compute at test time: let a model reason longer, or sample more attempts and keep one. Both share a hidden limit: they are internal. Every extra token comes from the same frozen weights and the same prompt, so neither can tell the model anything it does not already know. We study a third way, interaction: the model proposes an artifact, an external instrument observes how it actually behaves, and the model revises. Each cycle imports a real observation, so interaction breaks through the ceiling the other two hit.
  We argue that a single variable governs this third axis, grounding, and that it must hold on both sides of the loop. The feedback that drives revision must come from an instrument that actually observes the flaw, and so must the metric that scores the result. On hard coding tasks at a fixed token budget, reasoning-only and best-of-N sampling both plateau (the latter even when an oracle picks the best sample), while every interaction strategy keeps improving; our proposer-reviewer harness reaches a perfect 100% pass rate with no run-to-run variance, and the gain holds across three model families. On rendered visual artifacts, the usual judge (a vision-language model, or VLM, reading a screenshot) rates 14 of 15 visibly broken figures "perfect," because the screenshot hides the flaws before the judge can see them. A tool that measures the real layout instead shows the loop removing 40-74% of defects across four modalities; and that same VLM, used as the reviewer, makes slide layouts worse where the measuring tool repairs them. Interaction scaling is real and distinct from reasoning and sampling, but only visible when both the feedback and the metric are grounded.

</details>


### [298] [Auditing the Risk Claims of Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11607)
*Hari Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过严格审计发现，主流分布强化学习算法（QR-DQN、C51、IQN）学习的风险分布主要反映训练伪影而非真实环境随机性，40-95%的最强风险声称在95%置信水平下被推翻，这对分布RL在风险敏感控制中的应用提出了根本性质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习代理学习完整的回报分布，用于可解释性、风险敏感控制和安全监测等应用，但其声称的风险信息是否真实尚缺乏直接测量验证。理论上预期这些风险声称应反映环境随机性，但现有研究未对其进行系统性审计。

Method: 提出审计框架结合：(1)决策相关筛选指标——顶部两个动作间的Wasserstein间隙（等于违反一阶随机优势的质量）；(2)快照重启蒙特卡洛方法获取真实基准；(3)统计验证框架包括置换零假设、Bootstrap反驳和FDR控制，防止审计本身产生错误结论。在MinAtar测试平台对QR-DQN、C51和IQN进行33次实验验证。

Result: 在MinAtar上，40-95%的最强风险声称在95%置信水平被推翻，最强声称的位置与真实随机无统计差异，几乎没有声称可确认；学习到的"风险"反映训练伪影而非环境随机性。该伪影具有结构性特征：训练早期完全形成、与最终得分无关、每个随机种子特异。全规模Atari实验中，预训练的近SOTA QR-DQN在Breakout上的所有顶部声称均被推翻。阳性对照实验确认96-100%真实声称，相关性0.89-0.92。按CVaR建议行动效果从有益到显著差于随机。

Conclusion: 分布强化学习代理学习的风险信息存在结构性训练伪影，既非简单的校准误差也非可通过重校准解决。风险训练和集成方法无法消除该伪影，重校准虽能通过审计但实质上使声称无效。研究结果对分布RL在安全关键应用中的可靠性提出了重要质疑，同时发布了审计工具包并记录了两个审计陷阱。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning agents learn full return distributions that are increasingly read at face value: for interpretability, risk-sensitive control, and safety monitoring. We ask a question theory anticipates but that has not been measured directly: are the risk claims of a trained distributional agent true? Our audit combines a decision-relevant screening metric (the excess Wasserstein gap between the top two actions, which equals the mass by which first-order stochastic dominance is violated), ground truth from snapshot-restart Monte Carlo, and a statistical harness (permutation nulls, bootstrap refutation, FDR control) without which the audit itself manufactures false conclusions. Across QR-DQN, C51, and IQN on MinAtar (33 runs), 40-95% of the strongest claimed risk trade-offs are refuted at 95% confidence, the placement of the strongest claims is statistically indistinguishable from truth-blind, and essentially no claim is confirmable: for these agents, the learned "risk" reflects a training artifact rather than environment stochasticity. The artifact is structural (fully formed early in training, uncorrelated with final score, idiosyncratic to each seed) and appears unchanged at full-Atari scale, with every top Breakout claim of a pretrained near-state-of-the-art QR-DQN refuted. Positive controls of known magnitude confirm 96-100% of real claims (correlation 0.89-0.92): the reading measures the agents, not the audit. Acting on the heads' CVaR advice at their most-flagged states ranges from beneficial to significantly worse than chance. Neither training for risk nor ensembling removes the artifact, and recalibration passes the audit only by nullifying the claims: the head is uninformative, not merely miscalibrated. We release the toolkit and document two silent pitfalls that produced convincing but wrong audits of our own.

</details>


### [299] [Lesioned Multimodal Language Models Reproduce Aphasic Picture-Naming Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11621)
*Yong Yang,Xiang Guan,Sophie Arheix-Parras,Saeed Ahmadi,Roger Newman-Norlund,Leonardo Bonilha,Christopher Rorden,Julius Fridriksson,Rutvik H. Desai,Srihari Nelakuditi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现通过对多模态语言模型LLaVA 1.6进行受控扰动，可以重现中风后失语症患者在图片命名任务中的个体化错误模式，97.8%的患者错误模式可在6/7个错误类别上被模型拟合，提示语言模型有潜力作为失语症患者的数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中风后失语症通常会产生具有特征性模式的系统性命名错误，但目前尚不清楚未针对临床模拟设计的通用语言模型是否能够复现这些错误模式。研究旨在探索：(1)对多模态语言模型的损伤或受控扰动能否重现不同类型的图片命名错误；(2)该框架能否重现单个失语症患者的完整错误特征。

Method: 使用LLaVA 1.6多模态语言模型，评估了改变层位置、比例和施加噪声量的扰动配置。在Philadelphia命名测试数据集上考察了278名失语症患者，使用经过验证的神经分类器将响应分为七个错误类别。通过搜索扰动参数空间寻找能够匹配个体错误模式的配置，并采用蒙特卡洛基线验证匹配的统计显著性。

Result: 在七个响应类别中，有六个类别（正确、语义错误、混合错误、无关错误、新语、无响应错误）在临床可比的比例下出现于不同的参数空间区域，仅形式性错语例外。97.8%的患者在至少六个类别上找到了能重现其个体错误特征的扰动配置，79.5%的患者在全部七个类别上匹配成功。蒙特卡洛基线证实该匹配反映的是类别间的联合结构而非边缘重叠。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一个能够重现个体失语症图片命名错误模式的定量框架，证明了语言模型在模拟失语症错误模式方面的潜力，提出语言模型可作为中风后失语症患者数字孪生的可能性，为临床研究和个体化医疗提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: Aphasia following stroke commonly produces systematic naming errors with characteristic profiles, but whether general-purpose language models not designed for clinical simulation can reproduce these patterns remains untested. We investigated (1) whether lesions or controlled perturbations to a multimodal language model can reproduce different types of errors in picture naming, and (2) whether the framework can reproduce the complete error profile of individual persons with aphasia (PWAs). Using LLaVA 1.6, we evaluated perturbation configurations that varied the layer, proportion, and amount of noise applied to model units. We examined 278 PWAs on the Philadelphia Naming Test, classifying responses into seven categories using a validated neural classifier. Six of seven response categories (correct, semantic, mixed, unrelated, neologism, no response errors) emerged at clinically-comparable proportions across distinct parameter space regions, with formal paraphasia being the exception. Searching the perturbation space revealed configurations that reproduced the individual error profile in at least six of seven categories for 97.8% of PWAs and in all seven categories for 79.5% of PWAs. Monte Carlo baselines confirmed that this matching reflects joint inter-category structure rather than marginal overlap. These results establish a quantitative framework for reproducing individual aphasic error patterns in picture naming. They suggest the potential for language models to serve as digital twins of individuals with post-stroke aphasia.

</details>


### [300] [Reproducing human biases in route choice using large language models: Toward scalable behavioral modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11632)
*Jiangtao Han,Shoufeng Ma,Shuxian Xu,Geng Li,Shuai Ling,Ning Jia,Zhengbing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）是否能在不显式指定前景理论参数的情况下复现人类选择行为中的系统性偏差。实验结果表明，LLM能够生成符合前景理论预测的非理性决策行为，为大规模基于智能体的仿真和行为研究提供了可扩展的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的累积前景理论（CPT）在大规模应用时，面临个体层面参数标定的关键瓶颈。现有方法依赖问卷调查和受控实验，难以泛化且无法捕捉人类决策行为的多样性。因此，亟需探索一种无需显式参数设定即可模拟人类行为偏好的可扩展方法。

Method: 以路径选择为代表性场景，构建了行为评估框架，系统性地将LLM生成的决策与CPT预测的既有人类行为模式进行对比分析，检验LLM在不确定性下是否展现出符合前景理论效应的决策行为。

Result: 实验结果证明，LLM能够复现人类选择中的非理性偏差，并在不确定性条件下展现出与前景理论效应相一致的决策行为，无需预先显式指定前景理论参数。

Conclusion: 生成式AI模型可作为模拟人类决策过程的可扩展替代方案，为下一代大规模基于智能体的仿真及AI驱动的行为研究奠定了有潜力的基础。

Abstract: Human choice behavior, including route choice, exhibits systematic behavioral biases that deviate from the assumptions of full rationality. Cumulative prospect theory (CPT) has been widely recognized as an effective framework for characterizing such behavioral patterns. However, its large-scale application, particularly in simulation and agent-based modeling, critically depends on specifying individual-level CPT parameters, which remain a major bottleneck. Conventional approaches typically rely on surveys and controlled experiments to calibrate CPT parameters, yet these methods are difficult to generalize and often fail to capture the full diversity of human decision-making. To address this challenge, this paper investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can reproduce human behavioral biases in choice-making without explicit specification of prospect-theoretic parameters. Using route choice as a representative scenario, we design a behavioral evaluation framework and systematically compare LLM-generated decisions with established human behavioral patterns predicted by CPT. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMs are capable of reproducing non-rational human choice biases and can exhibit decision behaviors consistent with prospect-theoretic effects under uncertainty. These findings suggest that generative AI models may provide a scalable alternative for modeling human decision processes and offer a promising foundation for next-generation large-scale agent-based simulation and AI-driven behavioral research.

</details>


### [301] [Think Through a Bottleneck: Hourglass Reasoning for Rigorous Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11696)
*Huan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hourglass推理框架，通过强制阶段间的严格上下文隔离，显著提升了大语言模型的少样本归纳推理能力，在多个基准测试上相比迭代细化基线最高提升14个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数自我细化方法在少样本归纳推理中效果不佳。简单地提示模型显式陈述推理规则作用有限。关键在于需要在推理阶段之间建立结构化隔离，使信息只能以压缩的符号状态形式在阶段间传递。

Method: 引入Hourglass推理框架，强制阶段间的严格上下文隔离。冻结的LLM作为元构造器，为每个任务构建符号编码器-解码器：归纳模块将支持样本压缩为模式φ和临时支架z；演绎模块从中推导规则T并丢弃z；实现器将(φ, T)编译为制品；错误驱动的细化器修正(φ, T)并从头重新生成制品。只有(φ, T)跨越阶段边界，确保所有细化都锚定于规则。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2上将best-of-5准确率相比迭代细化基线提升14个点；在ChipBench上使用GPT-5.5将Verilog合成准确率从31%提升至58%，近乎翻倍；在BBEH-Linguini（国际语言学奥赛谜题）上缓解了显式言语化损害性能的倾向，在使用Gemini 3.1 Pro时完全逆转了该效应。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实性能提升源于阶段隔离和初始归纳质量，而非提示措辞或特定符号形式。决定冻结LLM归纳推理能力的核心因素是信息在推理过程中的流动方式，而非表达所用的语言形式。

Abstract: Self-refinement often fails to strengthen few-shot inductive reasoning in large language models. Prompting a model to explicitly state its inferred rule does little on its own. What actually matters is a structurally enforced isolation between reasoning stages, so that information can only pass between them as a compressed symbolic state.
  We introduce \textbf{Hourglass reasoning}, which enforces strict context isolation between reasoning stages. The frozen LLM acts as a meta-constructor, building for each task a symbolic encoder--decoder: an Induction module compresses the support examples into a schema $φ$ (encoder) and a transient scaffold $z$; a Deduction module derives rule $T$ (decoder) from these and discards $z$; an Implementer compiles $(φ, T)$ into artifacts; an error-driven Refiner revises $(φ, T)$ and regenerates artifacts from scratch. Only $(φ, T)$ crosses stage boundaries, so all refinement stays anchored to the rule.
  We evaluate Hourglass across three benchmarks spanning visual abstraction, hardware synthesis, and textual rule induction, using GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro. On ARC-AGI-2, it raises best-of-5 accuracy by up to 14 points over an iterative-refinement baseline. On ChipBench, it nearly doubles Verilog synthesis accuracy with GPT-5.5, from 31\% to 58\%. BBEH-Linguini draws on puzzles from the International Linguistics Olympiad, a setting where prior work has shown that explicit verbalization can hurt performance. Hourglass mitigates this tendency, and on Gemini 3.1 Pro, it reverses the effect entirely.
  Ablations confirm that these gains come from the isolation between stages and the quality of the initial induction, not from prompt wording or the particular symbolic form used. It is how information flows through the reasoning process, rather than the language used to express it, that drives inductive reasoning in frozen LLMs.

</details>


### [302] [Playful AI in Professional Email: A Field Experiment on Tone and Recipient Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11749)
*Ziv Ben-Zion,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI辅助写作通过改变邮件情感基调间接影响工作场所沟通行为，而非直接产生效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI辅助写作是否改变收件人的实际行为以及通过何种渠道产生影响。虽然大型语言模型正在重塑工作场所沟通，但其对收件人行为的实际影响机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用随机交叉现场实验，在六家公司的121名员工中开展为期三周的研究。员工在三种条件下发送工作邮件：无辅助写作、GPT-5趣味语调改写、GPT-5专业语调改写，共收集16,880封邮件数据。

Result: 趣味性编辑增加了情感积极性（B=+0.068，p<0.001），专业性编辑降低了情感积极性（B=-0.041，p<0.001）。两种条件均未直接改变打开率、回复率或响应时间。然而，发送者内部的情感积极性强烈预测打开（OR=2.05）和回复行为（OR=3.32，p<0.001），构成AI编辑影响行为的显著间接路径。

Conclusion: AI辅助沟通并非通过其使用本身，而是通过其生成语言的情感基调来塑造工作场所参与度。在不存在直接效应的情况下，AI编辑通过改变邮件情感基调这一间接途径影响收件人行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly reshaping workplace communication, yet whether AI-assisted writing changes how recipients actually behave, and through what channel, remains unknown. Here, in a randomized crossover field experiment, 121 employees across six companies sent work emails under three conditions over three weeks: unaided writing, GPT-5 rewriting in a playful tone, and GPT-5 rewriting in a professional tone. Across 16,880 emails, playful editing increased emotional positivity (B=+0.068, p<0.001), and professional editing decreased it (B=-0.041, p<0.001), yet neither condition directly altered open rates, reply rates, or response times. Instead, within-sender positivity strongly predicted both opening (OR=2.05) and replying (OR=3.32, p<0.001), a significant indirect pathway through which AI editing shaped behavior, in the absence of any direct effect. These findings suggest that AI-assisted communication shapes workplace engagement not through its use, but through the emotional tone of the language it produces.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [303] [Transfer Learning Across Policy Regimes in Adaptive Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09685)
*Roberto Garrone*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将政策制度变迁建模为自适应多智能体系统中的迁移学习问题，发现当新旧制度共享稳定结构不变量时迁移有益，但当政策-结果关系发生根本改变时会产生负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策模型通常假设政策工具与结果之间的关系在不同制度条件下保持稳定，但在自适应社会技术系统中，监管变化可能改变激励、智能体会进行策略性响应，导致从政策变量到聚合结果的映射发生变化，这一稳定性假设可能失效。

Method: 将政策制度表示为由可观察输入分布和目标函数组成的迁移学习问题，对比空白学习者（在灵活假设类中搜索）与迁移学习者（受前一个制度结构性知识限制的假设类），分析何时迁移有益或有害。通过 stylized emissions-regulation 环境和动态 ABM 鲁棒性实验进行验证。

Result: 当目标制度保持仿射单调税收-排放关系时，迁移改善了经验小样本性能；当目标制度引入阈值断点时，相同迁移结构产生负迁移：held-out误差保持高位，在线预测产生更多错误，重复在线流显示在错误设定下有更大的累积和最终窗口误差。

Conclusion: 先前监管经验应在捕获稳定结构不变量时被重用，但在政策变化改变政策-结果关系时需谨慎处理，这为政策制定提供了方法论指导。

Abstract: Policy models often assume that the relationship between a policy instrument and its outcome remains stable across institutional conditions. In adaptive socio-technical systems this assumption may fail: regulatory change can alter incentives, agents can respond strategically, and the mapping from policy variables to aggregate outcomes can change. This paper studies such regime change as a transfer-learning problem in adaptive multi-agent systems. A policy regime is represented as a learning problem induced by an observable input distribution and a target function mapping policy variables to outcomes. We compare a blank-slate learner that searches a flexible hypothesis class in the new regime with a transfer learner whose effective hypothesis class is restricted by structural knowledge from the previous regime. Transfer is beneficial when this restriction preserves the new target function while reducing effective complexity; it is harmful when the restriction excludes the new target and creates misspecification. A stylized emissions-regulation experimental environment and a dynamic ABM robustness experiment support the claim. When the target regime preserves an affine monotone tax-emissions relation, transfer improves empirical small-sample performance. When the target regime introduces a threshold break, the same transferred structure produces negative transfer: held-out error remains high, online prediction generates more mistakes, and repeated online streams show larger cumulative and final-window error under misspecification. The contribution is methodological: previous regulatory experience should be reused when it captures stable structural invariants, but treated cautiously when policy change alters the policy-outcome relationship.

</details>


### [304] [RideGym: A Standardized Interface for Real-World Large-Scale Ride-Sharing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10173)
*Zijian Zhao,Yulong Hu,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了RideGym，首个针对网约车订单调度问题设计的开源、标准化的Gym风格仿真平台，解决了现有平台缺乏统一接口、可重复性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车系统中的订单分配和行程打包直接影响城市交通效率和碳排放，是核心挑战。然而现有的仿真平台通常针对特定运营研究设计或与特定调度算法紧密耦合，缺乏标准化、学习友好的接口，导致研究人员需要从头构建定制环境，引发可重复性和公平比较的严重问题，并造成大量重复劳动。

Method: 提出了RideGym平台，采用Gym风格的标准化接口，将环境与调度算法完全解耦。该平台支持基于真实道路网络的大规模城市级仿真，提供车辆属性、订单规格和自动最短路径路由的灵活配置，使多种基于学习和基于模型的方法能够在相同条件下开发和比较。

Result: 通过复现多个基线方法验证了RideGym的有效性，展示了其高效性：涉及数千车辆和数万订单的一小时仿真在所有方法中均能在一分钟内完成。同时发现探索噪声的选择会显著影响MARL解决方案的性能和相对排名，这一方面在以往研究中常被忽视。

Conclusion: RideGym填补了网约车订单调度领域缺乏标准化仿真平台的空白，为MARL方法提供了公平、可重复的比较环境，并揭示了探索噪声对算法性能的重要影响，为未来研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Ride-sharing has become an essential component of modern urban transportation and has attracted significant attention across computer science, transportation, and management science. While the field spans a broad range of problems, such as driver relocation, dynamic pricing, and vehicle charging or fueling dispatch, the core challenge remains order assignment and trip bundling, which directly affect urban traffic efficiency and carbon emissions. Despite its importance, existing simulation platforms are typically tailored to specific operational studies or tightly coupled to a particular dispatch algorithm, and rarely expose a standardized, learning-friendly interface. As a result, most researchers still build customized environments from scratch, raising serious concerns about reproducibility and fair comparison, and incurring substantial redundant effort. To address this gap, we present RideGym, the first open-source, standardized Gym-style interface tailored to MARL-based order dispatch in real-world ride-sharing systems. By fully decoupling the environment from the dispatch algorithm, RideGym enables diverse learning-based and model-based methods to be developed and compared under identical, fully specified conditions. It supports efficient, large-scale city-level simulations on real road networks, and offers flexible configurations for vehicle attributes, order specifications, and automatic shortest-path routing. We validate RideGym by reproducing several baselines, and demonstrate its high efficiency, with a one-hour simulation involving thousands of vehicles and tens of thousands of orders completed within one minute across all methods. Moreover, we reveal that the choice of exploration noise can significantly affect both the performance and the relative ranking of MARL solutions, an aspect often overlooked in prior work.

</details>


### [305] [Distributed Agent System: Fault-Tolerant Collaboration Among Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10811)
*Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Hanqi Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布式智能体系统（DAS），这是一个设备-边缘-云框架，用于异构智能体之间的容错协作，将智能体可靠性重新定义为系统级容错能力，为工业场景中可靠的异构具身智能体协作提供了实用工程路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工程正在从大语言模型的被动文本生成转向智能体驱动的任务执行，在资源约束和环境不确定性下，长视野任务面临新的可靠性挑战。传统的错误消除优化策略无法解决累积错误传播问题，因此需要新的系统级解决方案。

Method: 提出分布式智能体系统（DAS）设备-边缘-云框架，包含两层容错架构：（1）通过容错对齐实现单智能体执行可靠性；（2）通过半形式化语言协议实现跨智能体通信可靠性。将智能体可靠性重新定义为系统级容错能力而非单轮零错误精度。

Result: 构建了一个面向异构智能体容错协作的完整框架，成功将可靠性定义从单轮零错误精度转变为系统级容错能力，提供了处理累积错误传播的新机制。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业场景中可靠的异构具身智能体协作提供了实用的工程化路径，标志着AI系统可靠性从错误消除向容错设计的范式转变。

Abstract: AI engineering is shifting from passive text generation by large language models (LLMs) to agent-driven task execution, creating new reliability challenges for long-horizon tasks under resource constraints and environmental uncertainty. Conventional error-elimination optimization strategies fail to address cumulative error propagation. This paper proposes Distributed Agent System (DAS), a device-edge-cloud framework for fault-tolerant collaboration among heterogeneous agents. We redefine agent reliability as system-level fault tolerance rather than single-turn zero-error accuracy, and present a two-layer fault-tolerance architecture: single-agent execution reliability via fault-tolerant alignment, and cross-agent communication reliability via semi-formal language protocols. This framework provides a practical engineering pathway for reliable heterogeneous embodied agents collaboration in industrial scenarios.

</details>


### [306] [Auditing Belief-Conditioned LLM Agents in Hidden-Information Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10814)
*Yuan Gao,Jiangyi Yang,Yao Zhao,Yichi Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可审计框架，用于在9人狼人杀游戏中评估LLM智能体在隐藏信息多智能体环境中的表现。实验发现主动信念条件与好人阵营胜率提升相关（从0.205升至0.390），但行动-信念一致性低（约0.21），效应机制尚不明确，主要贡献在于审计框架本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐藏信息多智能体环境中评估LLM智能体面临两大挑战：最终结果具有高方差，且难以揭示智能体决策背后的原因。现有评估方法缺乏可解释性和可审计性，需要建立能够追踪信念状态、记录决策证据的框架。

Method: 构建可审计框架，在9人狼人杀环境中实施严格的代码级信息隔离。框架维护隐藏角色的外部信念状态，将信念更新和信念-行动偏差记录为结构化证据，并支持防御性离线改进循环。通过1080场游戏进行多臂实验（包括信念禁用、主动信念、内核消融、阵营限制、消费策略和高负载条件），并进行200种子的配对A0/A1比较。

Result: 主动信念条件与好人阵营胜率显著提升相关：在200种子配对比较中，好人胜率从0.205提升至0.390（配对McNemar χ²=16.4, p<0.001），且不可逆女巫毒药错误减少。但行动-信念一致性低（约0.21），仅给狼人信念比仅给好人信念更能帮助好人阵营，表明效应机制不符合简单的持有者受益假设，无法归因于信念内容本身。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是审计框架本身：它使效应可测量，揭示了低行动-信念一致性，以证据拒绝了不可靠的强制消费干预，并分离了策略效应与负载混淆。在高噪声隐藏信息游戏中，外部信念主要定位为可审计的认知基线，同时携带决策相关信号，将不透明的智能体行为转化为可回放的证据，支持更安全、可控的迭代开发。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents in hidden-information multi-agent settings is hard: final outcomes are high-variance and rarely reveal why an agent decided as it did. We study this in a 9-player Werewolf environment where agents act under strict, code-level information isolation, and we build an auditable framework that maintains an external belief state over hidden roles, logs belief updates and belief-action deviations as structured evidence, and supports a defensive offline improvement loop that reviews bad cases before any strategy change.
  Across 1,080 frozen games spanning belief-disabled, active-belief, kernel-ablation, camp-restricted, consumption-policy, and high-load arms, and including a seed-paired A0/A1 comparison, the active-belief condition is associated with substantially better good-side outcomes: in the 200-seed A0/A1 comparison the good-side win rate rises from 0.205 to 0.390 (paired McNemar $χ^2 = 16.4$, $p < 0.001$), with fewer irreversible witch-poison errors. We do not, however, attribute this shift to belief content. Direct action-belief consistency is low ($\approx 0.21$), and giving belief only to the werewolves helps the good side more than giving it only to the good side, which argues against a simple holder-benefit account; we therefore report the effect as an association and treat its mechanism as unresolved. The contribution is the audit framework itself: it makes the effect measurable, exposes low direct action-belief consistency, rejects an unreliable forced-consumption intervention with evidence, and separates strategy effects from load confounds. We accordingly position external belief in high-noise hidden-information games primarily as an auditable cognitive baseline that also carries decision-relevant signal, turning opaque agent behavior into replayable evidence for safer, controlled iteration.

</details>


### [307] [Multi-Agent LLMs Fail to Explore Each Other](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11250)
*Hyeong Kyu Choi,Jiatong Li,Wendi Li,Xin Eric Wang,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现代LLM智能体在多智能体探索任务中的失败模式，提出了MACE框架通过结构化同伴选择显式促进探索，显著提升了多智能体系统的协调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对于多智能体系统的可靠自主性至关重要，但现代LLM智能体在与彼此交互时无法有效探索，表现出短视和极化的交互模式，导致次优协调和增加的遗憾值。

Method: 将多智能体探索形式化为部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）问题，智能体必须探测同伴以推断其能力并识别有效交互策略；提出Multi-Agent Contextual Exploration (MACE)框架，通过结构化同伴选择显式促进探索。

Result: 在上下文多样性和参数多样性设置中，MACE显著改善了探索行为和下游任务性能；理论上证明了探索的价值随智能体多样性增加而增加。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体存在根本性局限，显式引导的探索对于可靠的多智能体自主性具有重要意义，强调了在多智能体系统中明确探索机制的必要性。

Abstract: Exploration is essential for reliable autonomy in multi-agent systems, yet it remains unclear whether large language model (LLM) agents can explore effectively when interacting with one another. We show that modern LLM agents fail to do so, often exhibiting myopic and polarized interaction patterns that lead to suboptimal coordination and increased regret. We formalize this challenge as the Multi-Agent Exploration problem, modeling it as a partially observable stochastic game (POSG) problem in which agents must probe peers to infer their capabilities and identify effective interaction strategies. To address this, we introduce Multi- Agent Contextual Exploration (MACE), a lightweight framework that explicitly promotes exploration through structured peer selection. Across both contextual and parametric diversity settings, MACE substantially improves exploration behavior and downstream task performance. We further show theoretically that the value of exploration increases with agent diversity. Overall, our results highlight a fundamental limitation of current LLM agents and underscore the importance of explicitly guided exploration for reliable multi-agent autonomy. Code will be released in https://github.com/deeplearning-wisc/mace

</details>


### [308] [An Explainable Agentic System for Detection of Conversational Scams with Summary-Based Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11707)
*Ahmed Omar Salim Adnan,Yogananda Manjunath,Shivanjali Khare*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解释的智能体系统用于检测复杂对话诈骗，并发布了多类别基准数据集ConScamBench-278。系统在对话级诈骗检测上达到97.8%准确率，用户研究表明该系统有效提升了用户的信任感和安全感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能的快速发展，对话式诈骗威胁日益增长。这类诈骗通常持续数周或数月，逐步建立信任后索要金钱或敏感信息。现有的诈骗检测系统主要关注孤立消息检测，面对这种持续性对话诈骗时显得能力不足。

Method: 扩展单消息钓鱼检测方法，构建可解释的智能体系统（agentic system）来检测复杂的对话式诈骗。同时引入ConScamBench-278基准数据集，包含8种诈骗类型的278个公开多类别样本，支持可复现评估和未来扩展。

Result: 单消息检测器在孤立消息上达到100%钓鱼召回率；对话级检测器在公开LoveFraud02数据集上识别出所有83个对话诈骗案例，在ConScamBench-278上达到97.8%准确率（95%置信区间[95.4, 99.0]）。两项用户研究（N=100和N=45）显示用户在判断可疑对话时经常感到不确定，使用系统后用户的信任感、自信心和对AI诈骗检测的需求感知均显著提升（p<0.001）。系统可用性量表得分为74.7（95% CI [72.5, 76.9]），超过既定可用性基准。

Conclusion: 提出的可解释智能体系统有效解决了现有孤立消息检测方法的局限性，在对话级诈骗检测任务上表现出色，用户研究验证了系统的实用性和可用性，为对话式诈骗检测领域提供了新的解决方案和基准数据集。

Abstract: Following the rapid progress of generative Artificial Intelligence, there is a growing threat posed by conversational scams. These scams often span over multiple weeks or months, gradually build trust and request for money or sensitive information. Existing scam-detection systems mainly focus on isolated messages, which renders them inadequate against this evolving threat. This paper extends single-message phishing detection and presents an explainable agentic system for detecting sophisticated conversational scams. It also introduces ConScamBench-278, an initial public multi-category benchmark for conversational scam detection spanning eight scam types, released to support reproducible evaluation and future expansion. On isolated messages the single-message detector attains 100% phishing recall, while the conversation-level detector identifies all conversational scams in the public LoveFraud02 corpus (83/83) and reaches 97.8% accuracy (95% CI [95.4, 99.0]) on ConScamBench-278. Two user studies (N = 100 and N = 45) further motivate the system: participants report frequently experiencing uncertainty when judging suspicious conversations. In an uncontrolled pre/post comparison, users self-reported trust, self-confidence, and perceived need for AI-based scam detection all increased (p < 0.001, Wilcoxon signed-rank). The system also receives a System Usability Scale score of 74.7 (95% CI [72.5, 76.9]), above the established usability benchmark.

</details>


### [309] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for C-V2X RAT Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11744)
*Moritz Schaffenroth,Uwe Kölbel,Heike Lepke,Alexander Prinz,Alfred Höß*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合V2X通信中的链路选择决策问题，采用多智能体强化学习算法MAPPO，相比深度强化学习方法，准时交付率在单车辆场景下从0.508提升至0.535，在所有车辆协同场景下从0.548提升至0.567，并将训练时间缩短一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着V2X应用从基础的合作意识消息发展到协同驾驶、共享感知等高级应用，对延迟和可靠性的需求呈现异质性。同时多种V2X通信技术各有优劣，需要研究如何在不同场景下自适应选择蜂窝Uu链路、NR-V2X PC5侧链路或双链路并行使用，以满足不同应用需求。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习算法MAPPO解决V2X链路选择决策问题，并与深度强化学习方法、静态决策树方法和静态信道选择策略等五种基线方法进行比较。评估在城市场景和选定的通信用例中进行。

Result: 相比深度强化学习方法，准时交付率在单一控制车辆场景下从0.508提升至0.535，在所有车辆遵循学习策略场景下从0.548提升至0.567，训练时间减少一半。性能提升主要来自高级应用场景（如协同驾驶、共享感知），而非仅涉及CAM消息的场景。

Conclusion: 未来的高级V2X应用将从自适应通信策略中受益，多智能体建模对解决混合V2X通信的链路选择决策问题具有实用价值，能够有效提升通信性能并降低训练成本。

Abstract: Vehicles are increasingly equipped with advanced V2X communication capabilities. While early V2X apps utilized services such as Cooperative Awareness Messages, recent developments have allowed more advanced applications including cooperative driving, shared perception, and sensor-sharing services. The broader mix of applications leads to heterogeneous requirements for latency and reliability. At the same time multiple communication technologies for V2X are available with pros and cons. Hybrid V2X communication can exploit the distinct advantages at the right moment to fulfill the requirements of the applications. This work studies the decision problem between cellular Uu link, NR-V2X PC5 sidelink, and the simultaneous use of both channels. We address this problem by using the multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm MAPPO and compare it to five baselines consisting of a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach, a static decision tree approach and static channel selection strategies. The methods are evaluated in an urban scenario and with a set of selected communication use cases. The evaluation results show that when compared to the DRL approach, the on-time delivery ratio improves from 0.508 to 0.535 in a single-controlled-vehicle setting and from 0.548 to 0.567 when all vehicles follow the learned policy and reduces the training time by half. The gains result mainly from the advanced applications scenarios, as opposed to scenarios involving exclusively CAM messaging. This indicates future applications will benefit from such adaptive communication strategies and that multi-agent modelling is useful for addressing the underlying decision problem.

</details>


### [310] [Forgetting Our Way to Shared Meaning: Effects of Forgetting on Conceptual Alignment in a Non-Partnership Coordination Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11787)
*Landon Liu,Mary Kelly,Alan Tsang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过非合作博弈模型探讨了记忆特征如何影响共享意义的涌现与演化，发现适应性玩家实现实际概念趋同更快，而非适应性玩家更早感知到趋同，且随时间降低新信息权重的策略能产生更稳定的概念共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念语义模型缺乏协调博弈机制，难以解释共享意义如何在群体中涌现和变化；而现有博弈模型假设参与者处于合作共赢的伙伴关系设置中，这与现实人际概念协调场景不符，因此需要建立非伙伴关系的博弈模型来研究记忆特征对概念趋同的影响。

Method: 将概念对齐建模为非伙伴关系博弈，通过反事实模拟比较具有不同适应性和记忆衰退水平的智能体，分析实际概念趋同与感知概念趋同之间的差异，考察新信息权重随时间变化（递减vs固定）对概念演化稳定性的影响。
