Result: 适应性玩家实现实际概念趋同的速度更快，最终概念区域更接近；非适应性玩家更早感知到趋同；随时间降低新信息权重比固定权重策略产生更稳定的概念共识；记忆特征对实际和感知趋同的涌现与演化至关重要。

Conclusion: 记忆机制在共享意义的涌现与演化过程中发挥关键作用，不同的记忆策略和适应性水平导致实际与感知概念收敛出现显著差异，为理解人际概念协调提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Shared meaning in language requires people to learn and agree on categories. We ask how characteristics of agents' memories change the emergence and evolution of shared meaning. Without a coordination game, models of conceptual semantics cannot explain how shared meaning emerges and changes in groups of people; however, existing games assume that players share payoffs in a partnership setting. We model conceptual alignment as a non-partnership game and illustrate differences in actual and perceived conceptual convergence from counterfactual simulations using agents with varying levels of adaptiveness and memory degradation. We found that adaptive players achieved actual convergence faster and had closer final conceptual regions than non-adaptive players, while non-adaptive players perceived convergence earlier. Weighing novel information less over time resulted in more stable agreements than fixing the weight of novel information. Memory features are critical to the emergence and evolution of actual and perceived convergence.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [A Note on Diameter Certification in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09929)
*Josef Erik Sedláček*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于树结构直径认证的1-局部认证方案，证书大小仅为O(log d)比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局部认证模型中，一般图的直径认证需要很大的证书，但树结构可能允许更高效的解决方案。作者希望为树结构设计一个高效的直径认证方案。

Method: 设计了一个1-局部认证方案来认证给定树的直径是否不超过d，该方案使用大小不超过$3\lceil\log_2(d+1)\rceil$比特的证书。

Result: 成功构造了一个证书大小为O(log d)比特的1-局部认证方案，能够验证树的直径是否至多为d。

Conclusion: 树结构的直径认证可以高效实现，证书大小仅需对数级别比特，显著优于一般图的情况。

Abstract: In the local certification model, certifying the diameter of general graphs requires large certificates, but trees admit more efficient solutions. In this note, we provide a $1$-local certification scheme that certifies whether the diameter of a given tree is at most $d$ using certificates of at most $3\lceil\log_2(d+1)\rceil$ bits.

</details>


### [312] [Trusted Floors Under Untrusted Learners: A Runtime Assured-SLO Guard for ML Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09992)
*Hsiu-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可信的防护机制，包裹不可信的学习组件，通过预留和优先调度确保关键服务质量目标不受学习组件错误的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习服务系统越来越多地使用学习型组件（路由器、延迟SLO准入器等）来决策租户的服务质量，但当这些组件出错时，保证的服务级别目标（SLO）可能会被静默破坏。Kubernetes底层层（如Kueue、DRA、Gateway-API推理扩展）只会增加跨层意外。现有系统过于信任学习组件的正确性，缺乏对错误决策的有效约束。

Method: 提出一种小型的可信防护机制，采用"学习组件提议，防护器决策"的架构。将租户的保证SLO义务分解为两部分：（1）安全投影（每请求调度可行性和每类别、每时间窗的服务下限），通过预留和优先调度在运行时强制执行，不受任意错误的学习准入器影响；（2）聚合义务（尾延迟百分位）作为统计残差，采用廉价确定性筛选器进行检测。防护器基于真实类别进行预留，防止标签错误破坏服务下限。

Result: 在真实2xV100平台上，防护器在两种相反方向失败的丢弃策略下，对学习准入器的各种错误校准情况，均保持保证类别的缺失率为0.0（重复10次，最坏情况下Wilson置信上限为0.0053）。面对真实部署的GAIE流控制，错误标记的路由器会将相同的保证请求缺失率从0.0翻转为1.0，而防护器按真实类别预留，标签无法破坏其服务下限。论文还刻画了何时可信廉价静态筛选器、何时不可信。

Conclusion: 通过将不可信的学习组件包裹在可信防护器中，可以有效约束错误学习组件造成的损害，确保关键服务质量目标在各种学习组件故障情况下得到保障。该方法在实际硬件和服务模拟器上验证有效，为数据中心规模、真实模型流控制和闭式最坏情况定理提供了研究议程。

Abstract: Modern ML serving increasingly lets learned, unbounded components (routers, latency-SLO admitters, admit ladders) decide a tenant's quality of service; when one is wrong, the assured SLO can silently break, and the Kubernetes layers beneath (Kueue, DRA, the Gateway-API Inference Extension) only add cross-layer surprises. Rather than trust the learner to be right, we bound the damage a wrong one can do: a small trusted guard wraps the untrusted learner -- learned proposes, the guard disposes. A tenant's assured-SLO obligation splits into two parts with different epistemics. Its safety projection (per-request dispatch feasibility and a per-class, per-window service floor) is a controllable obligation the guard enforces at runtime, regardless of an arbitrarily-wrong learned admitter, by reservation and priority dispatch. Its aggregate obligation (a tail-latency percentile) is a statistical residual with no per-request enforcement point; a cheap deterministic screen for it is optimistically unsound not only near saturation but well below it. On real 2xV100 the guard holds assured-class miss 0.0 (reps 10, worst upper Wilson CI 0.0053) across every miscalibration of a learned admitter under two shed policies that fail in opposite directions. Against a real, deployed GAIE Flow Control on a serving simulator, a mislabelling router flips the same assured requests from miss 0.0 to 1.0; our guard reserves by the true class, so the label cannot break its floor. We characterize when a cheap static screen can be trusted and when it cannot. As a Frontiers-style submission we evaluate the stance on commodity 2xV100 and a serving simulator, and scope datacenter scale, real-model Flow Control, and a closed worst-case theorem as the agenda.

</details>


### [313] [Descriptive Execution of HPC Applications and Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10081)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个基于大语言模型的智能框架在HPC领域的应用效果，包括扩展性研究、作业转换和生物科学工作流三个任务，发现框架能完成任务但存在特定失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着软件组件的执行和编排方式从人工编写代码转变为描述性文本，HPC领域出现了应用编排、工作负载管理和系统监控等新型应用场景。在开发完全自动化的科学智能框架时，需要评估其在特定任务中的可靠性和策略。

Method: 使用结合大语言模型和工具函数资源的自主框架，在亚马逊云服务低延迟网络环境中执行三项任务：(1)优化并运行HPC扩展性研究；(2)在不同工作负载管理器之间准确转换HPC作业规范；(3)设计并运行完整的生物科学工作流。

Result: 框架成功完成所有三项任务，但表现出特定任务的失败模式：在扩展性研究中，智能体能部署和优化应用但作业监控效率低下，倾向于保守的固定等待而非事件订阅；在作业转换中，Slurm与Flux之间的规范转换准确率高，处理器亲和性标志是最常见错误；在生物科学工作流中，智能体几乎完全复现了专家编写的变异检测流程，在19次完整运行中18次与参考调用集一致，并通过多种功能等效的工作流实现达成结果。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的智能框架能够有效完成HPC相关任务，但需要针对特定失败模式进行优化改进。这些发现为未来开发由智能体处理调度和转换的多集群系统提供了宝贵经验。

Abstract: The means to execute and orchestrate software components has changed from human-written code to descriptive prose. In high performance computing, this transition is represented in application orchestration, workload management, and system monitoring and debugging, to name a few. The underlying means to enable descriptive definition of tasks is the use of the Large Language Model with associated tool functions and resources. A combination of a model with access to such resources, modeled in software, encompasses an autonomous framework. As fully automated and agentic frameworks are developed for science, it is important to assess reliability and strategies scoped to specific tasks. In this work, we assess the extent to which an agentic framework can optimize and run an HPC scaling study with a low latency network in Amazon Web Services, accurately transform HPC job specifications between workload managers, and design and run an entire biosciences workflow. We find that the framework completes all three tasks while surfacing task-specific failure modes. In the scaling study, agents deploy and optimize applications but monitor running jobs inefficiently, preferring conservative fixed waits over event subscriptions. In job translation, they convert specifications between Slurm and Flux with high accuracy, with processor-affinity flags the most common error. In the bioscience workflow, the agent reproduces an expert-written variant-calling pipeline almost exactly -- agreeing with the reference call set in 18 of 19 completed runs -- and reaches this result through many distinct yet functionally equivalent workflow implementations. This information is invaluable moving forward to developing multi-cluster setups with scheduling and transformation handled by agents.

</details>


### [314] [Consensus as Collapse Policy: Communication Evidence, Horizons, and Prefix Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10152)
*Tomislav Grospić*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于“证据（evidence）”的共识协议统一定义框架：将分布式执行视为由通信诱导的二阶证据状态，而共识输出则是该状态的一阶投影，从而将共识协议重新定义为一种“证据坍缩（collapse under evidence）”策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共识协议的规范以终端产物（如确定的值、复制的日志或已最终确定的前缀）为中心，这种“输出优先”的视角掩盖了使这些产物具有安全性的、由通信产生的证据结构。作者希望显式地刻画这一承载证据的载体，为理解、比较和规范共识协议提供一个更具表达力的语义基础。

Method: 提出一种语义/指称框架：将分布式执行解读为通信诱导的二阶证据状态，将经典共识输出视为该状态的一阶投影。在此框架下，各共识协议被建模为不同的“坍缩策略”，需回答：何种证据合法、检查何种有限视界、何时将证据投影为值或前缀、以及在证据不足时如何修复或推迟坍缩。同时，将FLP等不可能性结果重新解释为对确定性坍缩时机与条件的约束，而非通信无法积累结构的断言。

Result: 成功提供了一个可比较不同共识协议的通用框架，将协议差异系统地刻画为坍缩策略的差异。为不可能性谱系（FLP、集合一致、拓扑分布式计算等）提供了统一解释：FLP约束的是在完全异步与单故障下的确定性坍缩，集合一致揭示输出载体的宽度，拓扑分布式计算探讨历史/视图载体到输出载体的结构保持映射何时存在。

Conclusion: 共识协议可被规范为“基于证据的坍缩”。本框架并非提出新的不可能性定理或替代协议特定证明，而是一个用于统一理解和规范共识协议的指称语义框架。

Abstract: Consensus protocols are usually specified by their terminal artifact: a decided value, replicated log, or finalized prefix. This output-first view hides the communication-derived evidence that makes such artifacts safe. This paper makes that carrier explicit: distributed execution is read as an order-2 evidence state induced by communication, while classical consensus outputs are order-1 projections of that state.
  Under this view, consensus protocols can be compared as collapse policies. A protocol specifies which evidence is legitimate, which finite horizon it inspects, when it projects communication evidence into a value or prefix, and how it repairs or defers collapse when the visible evidence is insufficient. The impossibility lineage supports the same distinction. FLP is not a statement that communication cannot accumulate structure; it constrains deterministic guaranteed collapse to a terminal decision under full asynchrony with one crash failure. Set agreement then exposes the width of the output carrier, and topological distributed computing asks when a history/view carrier admits a structure-preserving map to an output carrier. The contribution is not a new impossibility theorem or a replacement for protocol-specific proofs, but a denotational specification framework: consensus is collapse under evidence.

</details>


### [315] [Automated Tensor Scheduling for Hybrid CPU-GPU LLM Inference on Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10183)
*Yangyijian Liu,Hongyi Ye,Mingyang Li,Wu-jun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ATSInfer提出了一种面向消费级设备的张量级细粒度异构推理系统，通过静态张量放置与负载感知动态传输相结合，显著提升了大语言模型在本地设备上的推理吞吐量与资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在消费级设备上的部署面临GPU显存不足的问题，现有卸载系统采用粗粒度的层级或专家级调度，忽略了层内张量的显著异构性，且难以适应设备动态负载变化。因此，需要更细粒度、更灵活的卸载机制以提升本地推理效率。

Method: ATSInfer在张量粒度进行卸载，结合静态张量放置与负载感知的动态传输，并引入异步CPU-GPU协作机制，以高效调度异构后端的存储、数据移动和计算。该方法针对密集型和MoE模型进行了系统实现。

Result: 在代表性消费级平台上的评估表明，ATSInfer将预填充吞吐量提升至多1.94倍，解码吞吐量提升至多3.29倍，同时提高了GPU利用率并更有效地利用了PCIe带宽。

Conclusion: ATSInfer通过张量级卸载与动态协调机制，显著改善了消费级设备本地部署大语言模型的用户体验，验证了细粒度调度在异构推理场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Running large language models on consumer devices such as laptops and desktops is challenging because model weights often exceed GPU memory capacity, making offloading inference necessary to extend effective model capacity with CPU memory. Existing offloading systems, however, typically rely on coarse layer-level or expert-level scheduling, which overlooks substantial heterogeneity among tensors within the same layer and adapts poorly to changing hardware load conditions on such devices. This paper presents ATSInfer, a hybrid CPU-GPU inference system for consumer devices that performs offloading at tensor granularity. ATSInfer combines static tensor placement with load-aware dynamic transfer, and introduces asynchronous CPU-GPU coordination to efficiently schedule hardware storage, data movement, and computation across heterogeneous backends. We implement ATSInfer and evaluate it on representative consumer platforms using both dense and MoE models. Compared with existing systems, ATSInfer improves prefill throughput by up to 1.94$\times$ and decode throughput by up to 3.29$\times$, while also increasing GPU utilization and making more effective use of PCIe bandwidth. These results show that ATSInfer can substantially improve the user experience of local LLM deployment on personal consumer devices.

</details>


### [316] [Byzantine Accountability Without Consensus: Strong Eventual Consistency for Non-Associative, Stochastic, Robust Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10305)
*Ryan Gillespie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ACCA框架，实现了无协调器的拜占庭鲁棒聚合，通过CRDT复制数据集合并继承强最终一致性，实验验证了其一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则（如multi-Krum）依赖中央协调器，去中心化实现面临根本性障碍：这些规则具有全局耦合、非结合性和不连续性特征，微小扰动可能导致子集选择翻转从而显著改变输出结果。

Method: 提出ACCA（可问责无共识聚合）方法：复制内容寻址的OR-Set签名贡献集和仅增的自认证等价证明集；聚合是对收敛状态确定性的纯函数，使用定点整数运算和哈希规范序；证明了CRDT收敛乘积的纯函数继承强最终一致性。

Result: 原型系统（10节点，3个拜占庭节点）通过全部16项证伪检查：对抗性gossip下字节相同的根节点、后期等价证明后的确定性重收敛、分区恢复以及三项字节一致性破坏性消融实验。

Conclusion: 证明了任意CRDT收敛乘积的纯函数（非单调、非结合或随机）都能继承强最终一致性及其逆命题；鲁棒性来源于数据格与证据格的组合应用于鲁棒选择器，而非基本提升步骤。一致性保证条件：至少2f+3个有效贡献（最多f个拜占庭）且满足量化边际条件。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust aggregation rules such as multi-Krum assume a central coordinator, and decentralising them is obstructed by the rules themselves: they are globally coupled, non-associative, and discontinuous, so an ulpscale perturbation can flip the selected subset, moving the output by a non-vanishing amount. None of this prevents coordinator-free replication, because a robust rule needs no agreed order of contributions, only an agreed set and an agreed exclusion predicate, both of which converge without consensus. ACFA (Accountable Consensus-Free Aggregation) replicates a content-addressed OR-Set of signed contributions and a grow-only set of self-authenticating equivocation proofs, offline-verifiable by anyone. Aggregation is a deterministic pure function of the converged product state: fixed-point integer arithmetic over a hash-canonical order, ties broken by content hash. We prove that any pure function of a converged product of CRDTs (non-monotone, non-associative, or stochastic) inherits Strong Eventual Consistency, together with its converse; the contribution is the composition of a data lattice with an evidence lattice applied to a robust selector, not the elementary lifting step. A prototype (10 nodes, 3 Byzantine) passes 16/16 falsification checks: byte-identical roots under adversarial gossip, deterministic re-convergence after late equivocation proofs, partition recovery, and three byte-identity-breaking ablations. The guarantee is consistency, not accuracy; robustness is imported, conditional on 2f + 3 admitted contributions (at most f Byzantine) and a stated quantisation-margin condition.

</details>


### [317] [Stateful Worlds, Stateless Elasticity: Exact-State Serving for Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10389)
*Jin Li,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldMove系统，实现了持久化交互式世界模型在GPU间的实时迁移，保证状态位级精确性，迁移速度比传统方法快101倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化交互式世界模型在GPU上维护多GB规模的注意力缓存状态，该状态无法在交互时间内重新计算或近似，导致会话被"钉"在特定设备上，形成调度难题。需要一种方法在不改变世界状态的前提下实现会话迁移。

Method: WorldMove通过以下技术实现：(1)位级精确迁移保证——目标状态与源状态位级相同或迁移失败；(2)可接受性条件判断——迁移必须在读出时间窗口内完成，带宽需覆盖状态大小加脏数据率；(3)感知突发(incast-aware)的准入控制器——防止接收端拥塞崩溃；(4)无损GPU编解码器——扩展准入范围到原始传输无法使用的网络。

Result: 同节点迁移耗时18.8ms，比保存/加载方法快101倍；在100Gb网络结构上保持校验验证的92.1-94.8 Gb/s吞吐量；在两个云提供商间执行48次迁移全部位级精确；准入控制器在1.4倍负载下保持零缺失。

Conclusion: 精确状态弹性是传输和验证的联合调度问题。位级精确性仅在同一GPU架构的受控配置内保持，迁移状态是交互时间内精确保持状态的唯一方式。传输层校验和准入控制对保证迁移可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: A persistent interactive world model keeps its running state resident on the GPU that serves it: a multi-gigabyte attention cache, almost all of it rewritten at every generation step. That state cannot be recomputed in interactive time or approximated without changing the world, so a live session pins its device. The pin is a scheduling problem. WorldMove moves a live session under one guarantee: the destination is bit-identical to the source, or nothing is installed. It relocates the cache in 18.8 ms same-node, 101x faster than save/load. It holds a checksum-verified 92.1-94.8 Gb/s on a 100 Gb fabric. At that rate the cache fits inside one interactive block. Migrating an actively generating session, it converges at a block boundary and the destination continues the world bit for bit. An admissibility condition decides each move. The move must complete inside the readout horizon, over bandwidth that covers the state plus its dirty rate. Lifted to a fleet schedulability test, it governed a consolidation loop that executed 48 of 48 migrations bit-identical across two providers. Two constraints are structural. Bit-exactness survives only inside a controlled configuration of one GPU architecture, so moving the state is the only way to preserve it exactly in interactive time. Verification cannot hide inside the wire on this fabric. Receive-path checksums stall the transport at protocol timescales under fan-in, and unscheduled incast silently collapses a receiver while every delivered byte stays correct. An incast-aware admission controller holds zero misses to 1.4x offered load and sheds overload as rejects. A lossless GPU codec widens the admission gate to fabrics raw motion cannot use. We exercise the serving loop and the mover separately, each end to end. Their composition on one fabric is unbuilt. Exact-state elasticity is a joint scheduling problem over transport and verification.

</details>


### [318] [CloudMicroHaskell: Direct-Style Distributed Haskell via Runtime Graph Serialisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10443)
*Robert Krook,Lennart Augustsson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CloudMicroHaskell，通过运行时图序列化支持直接风格的远程进程创建和消息传递，简化了Cloud Haskell的编程模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloud Haskell要求远程进程表示为静态闭包、消息满足序列化约束且节点共享代码，这些限制使得源级API存在困难边界。

Method: 构建基于MicroHaskell的CloudMicroHaskell库，运行时将代码和数据表示为组合子图，跨越节点边界时直接序列化可达图。

Result: 远程spawn可直接编写（进程体可捕获作用域变量，消息可包含普通值如函数），无需程序员编写闭包转换；评估显示编程模型更加直接，但部分源级类型保证变为动态检查。

Conclusion: 运行时图序列化使编程模型更直接，代价是某些类型保证变为动态检查，程序员需在节点间移动图时注意惰性求值和运行时持有资源的问题。

Abstract: Cloud Haskell brings Erlang-style distributed programming to Haskell, but its treatment of mobile code exposes a difficult boundary in the source-level API. Remote processes must be expressed as static closures, messages must satisfy serialisation constraints, and participating nodes are assumed to share the relevant code.
  This paper explores a different design point. We present CloudMicroHaskell, a Cloud Haskell-style library built on MicroHaskell, whose runtime represents both code and data as a combinator graph. When a process or message crosses a node boundary, CloudMicroHaskell serialises the reachable graph directly. As a result, remote spawning can be written in direct style: process bodies may capture variables from their surrounding scope, and messages may contain ordinary values, including functions, without programmer-written closure conversion.
  We describe the implementation of the CloudMicroHaskell node runtime, including remote spawn, message delivery, monitors, exit propagation, and library implementations of generic servers and supervisors. We evaluate the system with process/message benchmarks, a distributed work-pool benchmark, a file-synchronisation case study, and a heterogeneous deployment on microcontrollers. The results show that runtime graph serialisation makes the \ch{} programming model substantially more direct, while also making the tradeoff explicit: some guarantees enforced by \ch{}'s source-level types become dynamic checks, and programmers must be aware of laziness and runtime-owned resources when moving graphs between nodes.

</details>


### [319] [Ichnos+: Estimating the Carbon Footprint of Scientific Workflows Using Fitted Power Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10586)
*Kathleen West,Youssef Moawad,Philipp Thamm,Vasilis Bountris,Giulio Attenni,Magnus Reid,Yehia Elkhatib,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Ichnos+系统，用于量化Nextflow科学工作流的环境足迹（包括碳排放、能源消耗、水资源和土地使用），在三个计算集群上实现了10.8%的能耗估算误差，显著优于现有方法，并可扩展到Apache Airflow等其他工作流系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据密集型科学工作流规模扩大，其资源密集型和长时间运行的特性导致显著的能源消耗和碳排放。现有工具在共享虚拟化环境中不可用，或仅依赖基于少量通用数据点的功率模型，难以准确量化科学工作流的碳足迹。

Method: 提出Ichnos+系统，基于现有工作流追踪数据、计算资源的节点特定功率模型以及与执行时间对齐的碳强度数据，实现事后环境足迹估算。系统不仅估算运行碳排放，还扩展到隐含排放、水资源和土地使用的估算。

Result: 在三个计算集群上的评估显示，Ichnos+的能耗估算误差为10.8%，显著优于基于Green Algorithms方法的nf-core co2footprint插件（通过Intel RAPL硬件级能耗测量验证）。系统成功扩展到Apache Airflow工作流系统，保持了相似的高估算精度。

Conclusion: Ichnos+为科学工作流提供了一种准确、可扩展的环境足迹量化解决方案，能够在共享虚拟化环境中有效工作，超越了现有工具的局限性，为ICT行业的碳排放量化和减排提供了重要技术支持。

Abstract: As data-intensive scientific workflows scale to facilitate the automation of analysis of increasing amounts of data, their resource-intensive and long-running execution incurs significant energy consumption and carbon emissions. Given the already significant and rising emissions from the ICT sector, it is crucial to quantify and understand the carbon footprint of scientific workflows. However, existing tooling is commonly not usable in shared, virtualized environments or resorts to power models that are based on only one or two generic data points.
  To address this gap, this paper presents Ichnos+, a novel system to quantify the environmental footprint of Nextflow scientific workflows. Ichnos+ enables post-hoc footprint estimation based on existing workflow traces, node-specific power models for the computational resources utilized, and carbon intensity data aligned with the execution time. We evaluate Ichnos+ against hardware-level energy measurements obtained using Intel RAPL, and the nf-core co2footprint plugin, which implements the Green Algorithms methodology. We find that Ichnos+ is capable of estimating workflow energy consumption with an estimation error of 10.8% across three compute clusters, significantly outperforming the nf-core plugin. We further show that Ichnos+ extends beyond operational carbon to estimate embodied emissions as well as water and land use. Finally, we demonstrate how Ichnos+ can be extended for another workflow system, Apache Airflow, maintaining a similarly high degree of estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [320] [[AAFLOW+] Stateful Operator Abstraction with Zero-Copy Distributed KV Cache Orchestration for Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10987)
*Arup Kumar Sarker,Alexander James Halpern,Mills Staylor,Aymen Alsaadi,Gregor von Laszewski,Yue Cheng,Shantenu Jha,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AAFLOW+将KV缓存提升为分布式系统一等公民，通过KV状态共享替代文本传递，在多智能体LLM系统中实现了50.2倍的TTFT降低和7.63倍的计算成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM系统以文本为中心，需要通过昂贵的预填充重复重新计算共享上下文。虽然单请求推理可通过KV缓存管理加速，但通常局限于本地服务范围，缺乏跨智能体的KV状态共享机制。

Method: 提出AAFLOW+，一种有状态智能体工作流算子扩展：1) 将进程构建为通信感知图，并发优化数据、提示词和可复用模型状态；2) 提供KV物化、传输、分叉、组合和驱逐算子；3) 运行时支持零拷贝、传输感知执行，使智能体能复用长上下文而无需重计算。

Result: TTFT降低高达50.2倍；16智能体规模下计算成本降低高达7.63倍；KV内存减少1.72-6.10倍；吞吐量提升超过7.74倍。实验证明在中高带宽网络上，KV传输优于重计算。

Conclusion: KV状态共享通过替代文本传递显著提升了多智能体LLM系统的效率，AAFLOW+验证了将KV缓存作为分布式系统一等公民的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly integrate retrieval, planning, and reasoning, but remain fundamentally text-centric, requiring agents to repeatedly recompute shared context through expensive prefill. Although single-request inference is known to be accelerated by KV-cache management, it is usually restricted to local serving scopes. We introduce AAFLOW+, a stateful extension of agentic workflow operators that makes KV cache a first-class distributed systems object. AAFLOW+ builds processes into communication-aware graphs that concurrently optimize data, prompts, and reusable model state. It also provides operators for KV materialization, transfer, fork, composition, and eviction. Its runtime enables zero-copy, transfer-aware execution, allowing agents to reuse long context without recomputation. AAFLOW+ reduces TTFT by up to 50.2x, achieves up to 7.63x reduced multi-agent compute cost at 16-agent scale, reduces KV memory by 1.72-6.10x, and increases throughput by more than 7.74x, based on an analytical cost model parameterized by empirical hardware microbenchmarks. The results demonstrate that KV transmission outperforms recomputation on networks with moderate to high bandwidth, making sure KV-state sharing greatly increases efficiency in multi-agent LLM systems by replacing text passing.

</details>


### [321] [Domain Extension of Lock-Freedom and Wait-Freedom for Group Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11014)
*Raaghav Ravishankar,Sandeep Kulkarni,Sathya Peri,Gokarna Sharma,Manaswini Piduguralla,Yashi Rastogi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将传统的锁自由和无等待计算定义从单线程任务扩展到多线程协作的组计算场景，提出gl-自由和gw-自由的新定义，为分布式计算等新领域提供了更精细的程序特性刻画方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的锁自由和无等待计算定义仅适用于单线程完成任务的场景，无法有效刻画多线程协作完成任务（如分布式计算、NUMA系统、私有数据系统）的程序特性。现有方法将这些程序简单归类为无死锁/无活锁，忽略了其实际属性，因此需要扩展定义域以更准确地描述这类程序的特性。

Method: 通过将锁自由和无等待的定义域从单线程任务扩展到组计算（group-computation），提出gl-自由（group lock-freedom）和gw-自由（group wait-freedom）的新概念。扩展过程遵循三个约束：(1)与原始含义保持逻辑一致性；(2)不引入矛盾或歧义；(3)保留定义的核心属性。通过证明当组仅包含单个线程时，扩展定义与原始定义一致，验证了扩展的正确性。

Result: 成功构建了与原始定义一致的扩展定义框架。当组仅包含单个线程时，新定义与原始定义完全一致，满足定义域扩展的一致性要求。该扩展使得能够对新领域程序进行更细致的分类和特性刻画，而非简单归为无死锁/无活锁类别。

Conclusion: 该定义域扩展方法为分布式计算、NUMA计算系统、多线程私有数据系统等新领域提供了更精确的程序特性刻画工具，避免了传统方法中过于粗糙的分类方式。通过多个实例验证了扩展定义的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: A domain extension of a definition refers to broadening the scope of a definition so that it applies to a larger set of cases than originally specified. The notion of lock-free and wait-free computation is designed for the domain of tasks that are completed by a single thread (in competition with other threads). The goal of this paper is to extend the definition of lock-freedom and wait-freedom to group-computations (denoted by gl-freedom and gw-freedom) that require that the task at hand must be completed by a collaboration between multiple threads. When extending a definition, certain constraints must be respected: the new domain must remain logically consistent with the original meaning, the extension should not introduce contradictions or ambiguities, and it must preserve the essential properties that make the definition valid and useful. We demonstrate this by showing that our extended definition is consistent with the original definition when the group consists of a single thread. We note that extension allows us to characterize programs in a new domain (distributed computing, NUMA computation systems, systems with private data for different threads, etc.) instead of relegating them to be in the same category (deadlock/livelock-free) without regard to the actual properties of that program. We also illustrate this definition with various examples.

</details>


### [322] [Energy Calculus: A Compositional Algebra of Energy in Computational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11087)
*Mosharaf Chowdhury,Jae-Won Chung,Jeff J. Ma,Nishil Talati,Ruofan Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了能量演算（energy calculus），这是一种将能量作为一等原语的组合代数方法，为AI系统节能优化提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源是AI扩展的关键约束，但缺乏像计算、通信和学习那样的形式化处理。现有系统展示了显著的节能效果，但各自针对特定粒度和结构，无法组合不同系统的优化技术并推理其联合效应。能量仍被视为事后测量和点优化的单一标量，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出能量演算组合代数，基于可可靠测量的能量元素，每个元素携带能量签名（包括时间、静态/动态能量、硬件工作点、执行上下文和测量不确定性）。通过三个操作符（顺序、同设备并行、跨设备并行）按照计算结构组合签名，覆盖任意DAG结构执行。代数基于七个公理，处理上下文敏感性和不确定性传播。

Result: 建立了完整的能量代数理论框架，证明了两个能量特有性质：顺序组合仅在元素互为上下文不敏感时才可交换；顺序组合不对并行组合分配。提出了归约定理，在交互低于测量不确定性时恢复简单的上下文无关代数。证明了操作符可扩展到时间-能量帕累托前沿，使权衡推理具有相同的代数结构。

Conclusion: 能量演算为AI系统节能优化提供了统一的理论基础，使不同粒度和结构的优化技术能够组合并推理联合效应，将能量从事后测量转变为可组合、可预测的一等公民，为AI扩展的能源约束问题提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Energy is a binding constraint for AI scaling, yet it lacks the formal treatment that computation, communication, and learning have long enjoyed. Recent systems demonstrate large energy savings, but each targets a specific granularity and structure; one cannot combine frequency scaling from one system with critical-path analysis from another and reason about their joint effect on total energy. Energy remains a monolithic scalar that is measured after the fact and optimized with point solutions that do not generalize.
  We propose energy calculus, a compositional algebra that treats energy as a first-class primitive. It builds on energy elements, units of computation whose energy we can reliably measure, each carrying an energy signature that comprises its time, its static and dynamic energy, the hardware operating point and execution context under which we measured it, and the associated measurement uncertainty. Three operators (sequential, same-device parallel, and cross-device parallel) compose signatures along the same structure as the computation itself, covering arbitrary DAG-structured executions. The algebra rests on seven axioms that capture how hardware consumes energy, and it exhibits two properties distinctive to energy among computing resources: sequential composition commutes only when elements are mutually context-insensitive, and sequential composition does not distribute over parallel composition. We also present a Reduction Theorem that recovers simple context-independent algebra whenever interactions fall below measurement uncertainty, so practitioners pay for context dependence only where the physics demands it. Uncertainty propagates through every composition, so each prediction carries an error bound. Finally, we show that the same operators extend from energy totals to time--energy Pareto frontiers, so reasoning about tradeoffs composes with the same algebra.

</details>


### [323] [Xema: Efficient Diffusion Serving through Fine-Grained Memory Management and Auto-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11136)
*Xueze Kang,Guangyu Xiang,Suyi Li,Yuxin Wang,Shaohuai Shi,Lin Zhang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Xema，一个内存高效的扩散模型服务系统，通过利用可预测的张量生命周期进行跟踪引导的内存优化，在GPU内存和SLO约束下显著提高了服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高分辨率图像和长视频生成服务中面临GPU内存限制问题。现有的内存节省技术（如权重卸载、分片和VAE切片）虽然能节省内存，但会引入显著的性能开销，在实际生产环境中不够实用。

Method: Xema采用跟踪引导的内存优化方法：1）为每个请求模板离线推导内存跟踪，识别短期内存压力间隔；2）仅在这些间隔内按需应用内存缓解措施；3）为可预测生命周期的张量构建静态内存布局，减少碎片化；4）引入离线规划器，在GPU内存和SLO约束下联合选择并行性、并发性和内存控制策略。

Result: 在Flux.2、CogVideoX-5B和LTX-2等生产扩散管道上的实验表明：相比现有服务配置，Xema将SLO达成率提升高达3.7倍；相比网格搜索，规划成本从6.3小时降低至197秒。

Conclusion: Xema成功解决了扩散模型服务中的内存效率问题，通过离线内存跟踪和静态内存布局实现了内存优化与性能开销的有效平衡，为生产环境中的高分辨率图像和长视频生成服务提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly deployed as production visual-generation services, where serving high-resolution image and long video generation is often limited by GPU memory. Popular memory-saving techniques such as weight offloading, sharding, and VAE slicing are often not practical because they tend to introduce significant performance overhead. In this paper, we present Xema, a memory-efficient diffusion serving system that exploits predictable tensor lifetimes for trace-guided memory optimization. For each request template, Xema derives an offline memory trace to identify short memory-pressure intervals and applies memory mitigation only within these intervals and only by the amount needed to fit the target GPU budget. Xema further constructs a static memory layout for tensors with predictable lifetimes, reducing fragmentation-induced reserved memory and making offline memory reasoning reliable at runtime. Built on this memory optimization layer, Xema introduces an offline planner that jointly selects parallelism, concurrency, and memory control under GPU memory and SLO constraints. The selected plan is stored in a plan table and directly used by the online serving runtime. We implement Xema on production diffusion pipelines and evaluate it with Flux.2, CogVideoX-5B, and LTX-2. Compared with existing serving configurations, Xema improves SLO attainment by up to 3.7x and reduces planning cost from 6.3 hours to 197 seconds compared with grid search.

</details>


### [324] [GPU-Tile-Sim: A Tile-Centric GPU Simulation Framework for LLM Hardware-Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11262)
*Yitong Ding,Jiawei Huang,Renyang Guan,Yangjie Zhou,Zihan Liu,Yu Feng,Shixuan Sun,Mingyi Guo,Jingwen Leng,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了GPU-Tile-Sim，一种面向LLM的瓦片级GPU仿真框架，通过将内核执行表示为线程束级瓦片图来捕捉现代LLM内核的性能特征，在A100、H100和Blackwell上实现了高精度的性能建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM工作负载依赖于通过软硬件协同设计优化的GPU内核，这些内核通过细粒度依赖调度和计算-内存重叠实现高性能。现有GPU性能模型面临新挑战：指令驱动仿真器适应演进架构的成本高昂，而分析模型过于粗糙无法捕捉内核特性。关键洞察是现代LLM内核性能更多受控制执行顺序和重叠的依赖结构影响，而非单条指令延迟。

Method: 提出GPU-Tile-Sim (GTSim)，一个以瓦片为中心的GPU仿真框架。核心是将内核执行表示为线程束级瓦片图，其中节点捕获瓦片级操作，边编码数据和顺序约束。设计包括自动瓦片图前端和图驱动仿真后端。该表示方法能够有效捕捉现代LLM内核的依赖结构和执行特征。

Result: 在A100和H100上评估代表性GEMM、注意力机制和端到端LLM推理工作负载，涵盖传统和高度优化内核。GTSim实现高性能建模精度，MAPE（平均绝对百分比误差）为1.22%--8.71%。进一步扩展至Blackwell架构并进行初步验证，证明了其在分析软件和架构设计选择方面的有效性。

Conclusion: GPU-Tile-Sim成功解决了现代LLM内核性能建模的关键挑战，通过瓦片级图表示有效捕捉依赖驱动的执行特征，为LLM软硬件协同设计提供了高精度、可适应演进的性能分析工具。

Abstract: Modern LLM (large language model) workloads increasingly rely on optimized GPU kernels through hardware-software co-design. These kernels achieve high-performance through fine-grained dependency scheduling and computation-memory overlap. As such, they incur new challenges on existing GPU performance models. Instruction-driven simulators are costly to adapt to evolving architectures, while analytical models are too coarse to capture kernels' characteristics. We propose GPU-Tile-Sim, a tile-centric GPU simulation framework for LLM hardware-software co-design. The key insight is that modern LLM kernel performance is governed less by individual instruction latency than by the dependency structure that controls execution order and overlap. Accordingly, GTSim represents kernel execution as a warp-level tile graph whose nodes capture tile-level operations and whose edges encode data and ordering constraints. Using this representation, we design an automatic tile-graph frontend and a graph-driven simulation backend. We evaluate GTSim on representative GEMM, attention, and end-to-end LLM inference workloads. On A100 and H100 across both conventional and highly optimized kernels, GTSim achieves high performance-modeling accuracy (MAPE, Mean Absolute Percentage Error, 1.22%--8.71%). We further extend GTSim to Blackwell with preliminary validation, and demonstrate its effectiveness in analyzing software and architectural design choices.

</details>


### [325] [Decomposing Runtime, Kernel, and Quantization Speedups via a Matched FP16 Intermediate: A Hardware-Conditioned Case Study on Four NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11368)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究对量化加速进行了归因分析，发现总加速（2.58×）中约三分之二来自运行时优化，量化内核贡献有限（波动≤1.5%）。多卡分片效率低于预期，80%性能损失源于协调开销。量化使并发用户数提升约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化加速报告通常将权重格式、内核和推理运行时的性能提升合并为一个数值，缺乏对各组件贡献的细粒度分析。研究者希望解耦这些因素，明确量化内核本身的实际贡献。

Method: 在配备NVLink桥接的4块NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPU上，使用匹配的中间堆栈（保留快速运行时但不含量化内核）将总加速分解为运行时部分和量化内核部分。通过profiler追踪分析分片性能瓶颈，并比较NVLink与PCIe的带宽表现。

Result: 端到端加速达2.58×，运行时优化贡献约三分之二（对数尺度）；三个模型族中量化内核贡献波动≤1.5%。四卡分片性能翻倍未达预期，80% shortfall归因于协调开销，非链路带宽。小模型的工作负载决定分片与多实例选择，大模型在所有工作负载下均倾向双实例方案。量化将可持续并发用户数提升约4倍。

Conclusion: 量化加速主要来自运行时优化而非内核本身；多卡分片存在显著的协调开销；最佳部署策略（分片vs多实例）因模型规模和任务而异；量化有效扩展了内存受限场景下的并发能力。

Abstract: Reported serving speedups from quantized kernels typically bundle the weight format, the kernel, and the inference runtime into one number. We present an attribution study on four NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPUs, 24 GiB each, on a single host with NVLink-bridged pairs. A matched intermediate stack that keeps the faster runtime without the quantized kernel splits the full speedup into a runtime part and a kernel and quantization part. Under matched greedy decoding the full stack reaches $2.58\times$ end to end, with the runtime change accounting for about two thirds of that gain on a logarithmic scale; across three similar model families the kernel and quantization part moves by at most 1.5%. Sharding one instance across all four cards falls well below doubling: a profiler trace attributes about 80% of the per token shortfall to coordination, and an NVLink versus PCIe control on the same hardware shows similar realized bandwidth on both links, pointing away from link bandwidth as the cause. Whether to run one sharded instance or several independent ones depends on the workload and the model, with the ranking reversing on the larger model: the smaller model splits between sharding and multiple instances by workload, while the larger model favors two paired instances on every workload. Quantization extends sustainable concurrent users roughly four times past a reproducible half precision memory cliff. Differences in sampling mode and prompt pool between the two stacks are documented as threats to validity.

</details>


### [326] [Stage-Level Executor Allocation in Apache Spark with Cost-Performance Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11415)
*Miriam Rateike,Isaac Waweru Wambugu,Celia Cintas,Michael Kaufmann,Ioana Giurgiu,Skyler Speakman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对无服务器Apache Spark环境的新型执行器分配方法，通过树集成模型预测阶段运行时间和成本，实现用户自定义的成本-性能权衡，在TPC-DS和SQLStorm基准测试中分别节省约50%和40.5%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式处理系统的执行器分配方法存在以下问题：(1)应用级分配虽然成本可预测但难以优化数千个不同阶段的性能；(2)无法支持用户对完成时间和成本最小化的明确偏好；(3)难以平衡扩展成本与性能瓶颈之间的关系。需要一种能够根据用户偏好灵活调整成本-性能权衡的阶段级资源分配方法。

Method: 提出针对无服务器Apache Spark环境的阶段级执行器分配方法：首先训练树集成模型来估计阶段运行时间和成本作为分配资源的函数，然后利用这些估计值为每个阶段单独推荐资源数量，从而支持用户指定所需的成本-性能权衡参数。

Result: 在TPC-DS和SQLStorm基准测试上评估，与两个基线方法对比：在103个TPC-DS查询上实现约50%成本节省，仅约16%的性能下降；在96个SQLStorm查询上实现约40.5%成本节省，约29%的性能下降，具体效果取决于用户定义的权衡参数和设置。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了用户可控的成本-性能权衡，显著降低了资源成本同时保持可接受的性能损失，为分布式处理系统的细粒度资源分配提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Allocating executors (i.e. compute resources) to distributed processing systems must balance resource costs of scaling-out unnecessarily against artificial, performance-limiting bottlenecks. Naive approaches may allocate executors at the application level, which have predictable costs and performance but are almost guaranteed to be sub-optimal for each of the thousands of diverse, individual stages executed by the application. Users may also have explicit preferences, such as completing an application within a specific time budget while minimizing cost, that existing solutions usually fail to support. We propose a novel method for determining the number of executors per stage in a serverless Apache Spark environment, enabling users to specify their desired cost-performance tradeoff. Our approach trains tree-ensemble models to estimate the run times and costs of a stage as a function of allocated resources. These estimates are then used to recommend resources for each stage individually. We evaluate our approach on TPC-DS and SQLStorm benchmarks and compare it against two baselines. Depending on the user-defined trade-off parameter and setup, our approach achieves approx. 50% cost savings across 103 TPC-DS queries with only a approx. 16% slowdown, and approx. 40.5% on 96 SQLStorm queries at a approx. 29% slowdown.

</details>


### [327] [MemExchange: Cloud-Scale Memory Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11579)
*AmirHossein Seyri,Abhisek Pan,Balajee Vamanan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MemExchange提出了一种集群范围的多租户内存管理系统，通过RDMA动态重分配空闲内存，将分散的缓存内存转换为逻辑聚合池，显著提高了内存利用率并降低了缓存缺失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务商为了应对不可预测的工作负载峰值，通常会过度配置内存，导致数据中心集群中出现严重的内存利用率不足。同时，内存受限的租户可能遭受较高的缓存缺失率，即使基础设施其他地方仍有空闲容量闲置。这种内存资源分配不均衡的问题亟需解决。

Method: MemExchange利用基于边际效用的分配策略，该策略源自在线Miss Ratio Curve (MRC)估计。系统通过RDMA在物理节点之间重新分配空闲内存，无需集中协调或强制租户共存。为了支持高效的远程访问，设计了MemExchange Tracker Communication (MTC)协议，这是一种应用层机制，协调内存重新分配并实现无需远程CPU参与的单边RDMA操作。在Memcached中实现了该系统。

Result: 在CloudLab上进行的微基准测试、中等规模和机架级（最多100台服务器）部署评估显示：与基于TCP的设计相比，远程访问开销降低高达2.3倍；机架级部署中集群内存利用率提高13%；在倾斜工作负载下，内存受限租户的缺失率降低高达63%。

Conclusion: MemExchange成功将跨服务器的专用缓存内存转换为逻辑聚合池，实现了跨节点内存交换，有效解决了数据中心内存利用率不足问题，同时显著改善了内存受限租户的性能，证明了分布式内存管理的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: To handle unpredictable workloads, cloud providers typically over-provision memory to meet peak demand, resulting in substantial underutilization across datacenter clusters. At the same time, memory-constrained tenants may suffer elevated cache miss rates, even when idle capacity remains stranded elsewhere in the infrastructure. MemExchange is a cluster-wide, multi-tenant memory management system that dynamically right-sizes in-memory caching tenants according to workload demand. Leveraging marginal-utility-based allocation derived from online Miss Ratio Curve (MRC) estimation, MemExchange redistributes idle memory between tenants across physical nodes using RDMA. This approach transforms the dedicated caching memory scattered across servers into a logically aggregated pool, enabling cross-node memory exchange without centralized coordination or forced tenant co-location. To support efficient remote access, we design the MemExchange Tracker Communication (MTC) protocol, an application-layer mechanism that coordinates memory reallocation and enables one-sided RDMA operations without involving remote CPUs. We implement MemExchange in Memcached and evaluate it through microbenchmarks, medium and rack-scale deployments of up to 100 CloudLab servers. Our results show up to 2.3x lower remote-access overhead compared to TCP-based designs, a 13% increase in cluster-wide memory utilization at rack scale, and up to 63% reduction in miss rate for memory-constrained tenants under skewed workloads.

</details>
